/**
 * SELF-CHECK WEB APP: "<ASSIGNMENT TITLE>"
 * ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 * Students open a web page (linked from Google Classroom), type answers, and
 * get the same green / yellow / red feedback as the Sheet version. Every check
 * is saved instantly to THIS spreadsheet, so the teacher sees results in real
 * time. Students never see or touch the spreadsheet, and the rubric never
 * leaves the server, so they can't read the answer key or break the grading.
 *
 * ONE-TIME SETUP (about 10 minutes)
 *   1. Create a new Google Sheet (e.g. "Net Force Self-Check RESULTS").
 *   2. Extensions > Apps Script. Replace Code.gs with this file.
 *      File (+) > HTML > name it exactly  Index  and paste Index.html into it.
 *   3. Left sidebar: Services (+) > Google Classroom API > Add.
 *   4. Save, then reload the spreadsheet. A "Self-Check" menu appears.
 *      Run  Self-Check > Set up / repair sheets  and approve the permissions
 *      (only you ever approve; students are never asked).
 *   5. In Apps Script: Deploy > New deployment > type: Web app
 *        Execute as:      Me
 *        Who has access:  Anyone
 *      Deploy, and copy the Web app URL (ends in /exec).
 *      Why "Anyone": Google refuses to open ANY web app (whatever the access
 *      setting) in a browser where a personal or family Google account is
 *      signed in before the school one, before this script even runs. So the
 *      link students get opens CLASS PASS on the class website first (see
 *      CLASS PASS below). It checks out of sight whether Google will open this
 *      web app for them. If it will (school Chromebooks), they go right in,
 *      recognized by their school account. If it won't, Class Pass asks them
 *      to sign in with their school account instead of showing Google's error,
 *      and runs the assignment on the class website. Its requests carry no
 *      Google sign-in, which is why the access must be "Anyone". Only students
 *      on the Roster tab can sign in that way.
 *   6. Back in the Sheet: Self-Check > 1. Link Google Classroom classes
 *      The Classes tab opens with a checkbox for each class: tick EVERY
 *      class getting this assignment (the period comes from the number in
 *      front of the class name, e.g. "6 - Science" is period 6). Then:
 *                         Self-Check > 2. Create the assignment in each class
 *      (paste the /exec URL when asked). This makes a DRAFT assignment worth
 *      10 points, with the Class Pass link, in every linked class. In Classroom,
 *      set each one's due date / topic and click Assign.
 *   7. When you're ready to grade: Self-Check > 3. Upload grades to Classroom.
 *      Each student's grade goes to the assignment in their own class.
 *   8. OPTIONAL, hands-off grading: set each class's due date in Classroom, then
 *      Self-Check > Automatic grades after the due date > Turn on (or check
 *      "Automatic grades after the due date" in the Settings tab, or flip it in
 *      the Self-Check Hub). An hourly check uploads grades after each class's
 *      due date, re-sends them daily for AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS days to catch late
 *      work, then stops uploading.
 *
 * SELF-CHECK HUB: Set up / repair sheets also starts a quiet hourly check that
 * writes this sheet's status to the hidden "_hub (do not edit)" tab. The
 * Self-Check Hub (a separate spreadsheet) reads that tab and the Settings tab,
 * so you can see and change every assignment from one place.
 *
 * ALL PERIODS, ONE SHEET: every period uses the same student link and the
 * same Live Results tab. Column A is the Period, so use the filter arrows in
 * the header row to sort or show one period at a time. The Question Summary
 * tab shows % green for each period side by side.
 *
 * WHY STEP 6 CREATES THE ASSIGNMENT FOR YOU: Google only lets a script post
 * grades to assignments that the same script created. An assignment you make
 * by hand in Classroom can't receive grades from here.
 *
 * REUSING FOR ANOTHER ASSIGNMENT: File > Make a copy of this spreadsheet (the
 * script comes with it), replace the SETTINGS + ITEMS below, add the Classroom
 * service if it's missing, make a NEW deployment, then run Set up / repair
 * sheets and steps 6-7. (One spreadsheet per assignment, not per class.)
 *
 * EDITING QUESTIONS, ANSWER KEYS, PICTURES, LINKS AND SETTINGS (NO CODE):
 * After "Set up / repair sheets", the spreadsheet has a  Questions  tab and a
 * Settings  tab. Edit them like any spreadsheet. Changes are live the next
 * time a student loads the page or checks an answer, with no redeploy.
 * Run Self-Check > Recalculate all grades to re-color answers already turned in.
 * (The values further down in this file are only the STARTER content used to
 * fill those tabs the first time.)
 *
 * AFTER EDITING THIS CODE (rare, only for new features): Deploy > Manage
 * deployments > (pencil) > Version: New version > Deploy.
 *
 * CLOSING AT THE END OF THE QUARTER: once the quarter an assignment belongs to
 * is over, students can still open it and see their answers, colors, feedback
 * and grade, but nothing can be changed (the page shows a pop-up saying so, and
 * this script refuses saves). An assignment belongs to the quarter its
 * Classroom due date falls in (no due date = the day step 2 created it). On the
 * quarter's last day each period closes when its class starts (Settings tab:
 * "Class start times on the last day of the quarter", and "Different start
 * times on some last days" for a last day with another bell schedule, like a
 * Thursday). Quarter end dates come
 * from Classroom's grading periods, or from the Settings tab if typed there.
 * Self-Check > Show quarter close times lists when each period closes.
 *
 * DRAWINGS AND PICTURE UPLOADS (not graded): type  draw  in a question's "Answer type" column
 * for a drawing pad students draw on right on the page (saved as a picture; a Picture in that
 * row, like blank graphs, becomes the paper they draw on), or  upload
 * (same column, or an existing "Type" column) for an upload box: students
 * drag a picture in, paste one (Ctrl+V), or choose a file. Pictures are saved
 * privately in a "student pictures" folder next to this spreadsheet in your Drive,
 * and Live Results links to each one. Upload questions don't count toward the grade.
 * The next question reminds students who skipped it, and so does I'm finished.
 *
 * MISCONCEPTIONS: besides the Idea key words (what a right answer needs), the
 * Questions tab has Misconception columns at the end: words that show a common
 * WRONG idea, and the hint students see when their answer has them
 * ("Check this: ..."). An answer with a misconception can't turn green.
 *
 * LIMITED TRIES (multiple choice, true/false, short answers): type  choice,  truefalse  or  short  in a
 * question's "Answer type" column. Students get a few tries (Settings: Tries for ...), earn a little less
 * with each try (Credit by try: 100%, 90%, 80%; Credit when never answered right: 50%), see feedback for the
 * wrong choice they picked, and open a hint (the light bulb) before trying again. The right answer and the
 * Explanation show only once the question is done. See LIMITED TRIES further down.
 * DROPDOWNS (like IXL): type  dropdown  and put ___ in the question where each dropdown goes (Ctrl+Enter
 * starts a new line). Every dropdown offers the Choice columns; Right choice has one answer per ___, in
 * order (2, 3, 2). Table config can show a small table above the sentences. One Check = one try.
 *
 * SECTION PICTURES: a picture right under a blue section strip (not tied to any
 * question): on the row that starts the section, paste a Drive share link in
 * "Section picture" (and optional text in "Section picture caption").
 *
 * POINTS: a question's Points column sets what it's worth. Students only see
 * "2 points" next to a question when the Settings row "Show points on each
 * question" is checked (it starts unchecked).
 *
 * STAYING UP TO DATE: Self-Check > Check for skill updates compares this file's
 * SKILL_VERSION with the newest release on the class website (SKILL_UPDATES_URL)
 * or in the "Claude Skill Updates" folder in your Google Drive, shows what changed,
 * and gives you copy buttons for the new Code.gs and Index.html. Your questions,
 * settings and student work live in the tabs, so replacing the code never touches
 * them. (Website releases carry no starter questions or answer keys.)
 */

// @@STARTER-BEGIN (assignment-specific content: title, intro, sections, settings, ITEMS)
// ============================== STARTER SETTINGS ==============================
// Copied into the Settings tab the first time you run "Set up / repair sheets".
// After that, change them in the Settings tab, not here.
var ASSIGNMENT_TITLE = "Forces & Motion Basics: Net Force + Motion Self-Check";
var INTRO = "Type each answer in the box under its question, then press Check (or just click out of the box). " +
            "The box colors itself: green = complete, yellow = partial, red = try again. Your work saves automatically. Answer in full sentences, in your own words. DO NOT copy and paste your answer from online. " +
            "Follow each SCENARIO exactly as written in the simulation before you answer.";
var LINKS = [];   // optional buttons at the very top of the page (section buttons are set per section below)
// Blue section strips. "before" is the index (0-based) of the first question in the section.
// "links" are the blue simulation buttons shown right under the strip.
// Optional "img" (Drive share link or https image) and "imgCaption": a picture right under the strip, above the buttons.
var SECTIONS = [
  { before: 0, title: "NET FORCE: TUG OF WAR (Screen 1)",
    links: [{ label: "Open the Net Force (Tug of War) simulation", url: "https://phet.colorado.edu/sims/html/forces-and-motion-basics/latest/forces-and-motion-basics_en.html?screens=1" }] },
  { before: 7, title: "MOTION: PUSHING OBJECTS, NO FRICTION (Screen 2)",
    links: [{ label: "Open the Motion simulation", url: "https://phet.colorado.edu/sims/html/forces-and-motion-basics/latest/forces-and-motion-basics_en.html?screens=2" }] }
];
var MIN_ANSWER_LENGTH = 25;   // an answer shorter than this can never show green (picture boxes are exempt)

// ---------- Grade out of 10 ----------
// Each question earns a share of credit based on its color:
var CREDIT = { correct: 1.0, partial: 0.6, incorrect: 0.1, blank: 0 };
// Earning this fraction of the possible credit = full 10/10. At 0.85, "mostly
// green with some yellow" gets full credit (e.g. 8 green + 5 yellow = 10/10).
var FULL_CREDIT_AT = 0.85;
var MAX_POINTS     = 10;
var ROUND_TO       = 0.5;     // round grades to the nearest half point
var SHOW_GRADE_TO_STUDENTS = true;   // show the running "x / 10" at the top of the student page
var SHOW_POINTS            = false;  // true = each question shows what it's worth ("2 points") next to its number on the student page
var LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN     = false;  // true = students can't change answers after "I'm finished"
var UPLOAD_ZERO_FOR_MISSING = false; // true = students who never opened it get 0 in Classroom (false = left blank)
var BLOCK_PASTE            = true;   // true = students can't paste or drag text into answer boxes
var QUESTION_NUMBER_STYLE  = "1";    // how question 1 is labeled on the student page: "1"  "1."  "Q1"  "Question 1"  "#1"  "1)"
// ---------- Automatic grade upload (turn on with Self-Check > Automatic grades after the due date) ----------
var AUTO_RETURN_GRADES = true;  // true = automatic uploads post AND return grades (students see them); false = draft grades only
var AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS   = 7;     // after the first automatic upload, re-send grades once a day for this many days (catches late work)
var AUTO_GRADES        = false; // true = upload grades automatically after each class's due date (Settings row "Automatic grades after the due date")
// ---------- Late work (uses each class's due date in Classroom) ----------
var LATE_PENALTY = 0.2;  // points a student earns AFTER the due date count 20% less (0 = no late penalty)
var LATE_EMAIL   = true; // true = when a late grade is returned, email the student a short note explaining the penalty
// ---------- Close at the end of the quarter (Self-Check > Show quarter close times) ----------
var CLOSE_AT_QUARTER_END = true;   // true = after the assignment's quarter ends, students can view their work but not change it
var QUARTER_END_DATES = "";        // blank = use Classroom's grading periods; or the last day of each quarter: "10/16/2026, 12/18/2026, 3/12/2027, 5/28/2027"
var CLASS_START_TIMES = "1 = 8:10 AM, 6 = 9:10 AM, 2 = 10:18 AM, 3 = 11:26 AM, 5 = 1:47 PM";   // on a quarter's last day, each period closes when its class starts
// Last days with a different bell schedule: a weekday, a date (12/17/2026) or a quarter (Q2), a colon, then period = time.
// Separate changes with semicolons. Priority: a date beats a quarter, which beats a weekday. "none" = always the usual times.
var CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES = "Thursday: 1 = 8:10 AM, 5 = 9:10 AM, 6 = 10:33 AM, 2 = 11:26 AM, 3 = 12:54 PM";
// ---------- Limited tries: Answer type choice, truefalse, short or dropdown (see LIMITED TRIES) ----------
var TRIES_CHOICE       = 2;                 // tries for a multiple choice or dropdown question (a question's Tries column can change it)
var TRIES_TF           = 1;                 // tries for a true/false question
var TRIES_SHORT        = 3;                 // tries for a short answer question
var TRY_CREDIT         = "100%, 90%, 80%";  // credit when the first right answer comes on try 1, 2, 3 (tries past the list use the last one)
var TRIED_WRONG_CREDIT = 0.5;               // a question tried but never answered right earns this share (never tried = 0)
var HINT_BEFORE_RETRY  = true;              // true = after a wrong try, the student opens a hint (the light bulb) before trying again
var SHUFFLE_CHOICES    = true;              // true = each student gets the answer choices in its own order (true/false never shuffles)
var SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS  = false;             // true = each student gets the questions in their own order (a Self-Check: within each blue section; a review game: a new order each try)
// ---------- Review game only (a sheet whose SKILL_NAME is "review-game"; see REVIEW GAME) ----------
var START_OVER        = true;  // true = students can Start Over; their best try is the grade
var QUESTIONS_PER_TRY = 0;     // how many questions each try gets, picked at random from the Questions tab (a new mix every Start Over); 0 = every question
var WEBSITE_BOARD_URL = "";    // the class website whose Leaderboard shows review game points, e.g. https://vocabulary-room901.pages.dev
var WEBSITE_BOARD_KEY = "";    // the Review game key from that Leaderboard page (blank = game points stay in this sheet)

// ============================== STARTER QUESTIONS + ANSWER KEY ==============================
// Copied into the Questions tab the first time you run "Set up / repair sheets".
// After that, edit the Questions tab instead (same ideas, one row per question).
// Stays on the server. Students only ever receive the question text, images and links.
//
// FIELDS (the Questions tab has a column for each)
//   "q"        the question text students see.
//   "img"      OPTIONAL picture shown under the question. Paste a Google Drive
//              share link (share the image with "Hart District: anyone with
//              the link" so students can see it) or any https image address.
//   "imgCaption" OPTIONAL small caption under the picture.
//   "links"    OPTIONAL buttons under the question, e.g.
//              "links": [{ "label": "Open the Motion screen", "url": "https://..." }]
//              A url of "timer:N" (N = seconds, like "timer:120") makes the button
//              show a countdown under it instead of opening a tab.
//   "criteria" the answer key. Each criterion is ONE idea the answer must show:
//       "nudge"  the hint students see when that idea is missing.
//       "need"   a list of word lists. The idea counts when the answer has at
//                least one word from EACH list:
//                  [["zero","balanced"]]            -> "zero" OR "balanced"
//                  [["vector","arrow"],["same","equal"]] -> ("vector" OR "arrow")
//                                                           AND ("same" OR "equal")
//                Word endings are handled for you: "accelerat" matches
//                accelerate / accelerates / acceleration. Two-word phrases
//                like "net force" must appear together.
//                A list that starts with "!" forbids words (typed "NOT a, b" in
//                the Questions tab):  [["chip"],["!","stack","tower"]] -> says
//                chip but NOT stack/tower.
//   "boxes"    OPTIONAL type-in boxes placed ON the picture (fill-in-the-blank
//              diagrams). Each box is { "label", "x", "y", "w", "h" } in % of the
//              picture (left, top, width, height). Box 1 is graded by criterion 1,
//              box 2 by criterion 2, etc. Each box colors itself; the question is
//              green when every box is right. No minimum length for boxes.
//              A question with ONE box and no picture = a one-word answer box.
//   All criteria met = green, some = yellow, none = red.
//   "mis"      OPTIONAL misconceptions: wrong ideas students often have, each
//              { "nudge": "...", "need": [...] } with key words written the same
//              way as in "criteria". When an answer has a misconception's key
//              words, its nudge is shown ("Check this: ...") and the answer can't
//              turn green. Only typed answers are checked (not tables, picture
//              boxes or uploads). E.g. for "What units are used to express speed?",
//              an answer that names a time unit but no distance unit:
//              "mis": [{ "nudge": "Seconds only measure time. Speed also needs a distance unit, like cm.",
//                        "need": [["second", "sec"], ["!", "cm", "meter", "centimeter", "km", "mile", "mph", "per"]] }]
//              (typed in the Questions tab as:  second, sec + NOT cm, meter, centimeter, km, mile, mph, per)
//
// Example with a picture and a link:
//   { "q": "Look at the diagram. Which way will the box move?",
//     "img": "https://drive.google.com/file/d/1AbC.../view?usp=sharing",
//     "imgCaption": "Forces acting on the box",
//     "links": [{ "label": "Open the simulation", "url": "https://phet.colorado.edu/..." }],
//     "criteria": [ { "nudge": "Which side has the bigger force?", "need": [["left"]] } ] },
//
// In the Questions tab, key words are typed as text:  vector, arrow + same, equal
// (commas = OR, "+" = AND). Use Self-Check > Test an answer to try the key.
// Add or remove questions BEFORE students start (the Q1, Q2... columns shift),
// then run Set up / repair sheets.
var ITEMS = [];   // website releases carry no starter questions or answer keys: this sheet's questions live in its Questions tab

// @@STARTER-END

// ============================== SKILL VERSION ==============================
// Used by Self-Check > Check for skill updates. Claude bumps SKILL_VERSION (YYYY.MM.DD)
// every time the auto-grading-activity skill's template changes.
var SKILL_NAME = "net-force-escape-room";   // a one-off Self-Check with its own page (self-check\custom\net-force-escape-room\)
var SKILL_VERSION = "2026.11.11";
var SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER = "Claude Skill Updates";
var SKILL_UPDATES_URL = "https://physical-science-8.pages.dev/pass/releases/";   // releases on the class website (<kind>/latest.json); "" = only the Drive folder

// ============================== SHEET NAMES / COLORS ==============================
var SH_LIVE = "Live Results", SH_SUMMARY = "Question Summary", SH_ROSTER = "Roster",
    SH_LOG = "Answer Log", SH_STATE = "_data (do not edit)",
    SH_QUESTIONS = "Questions", SH_SETTINGS = "Settings", SH_UPLOADS = "Grade Uploads",
    SH_HUB = "_hub (do not edit)", SH_CLASSES = "Classes";
var FILL ={ correct: "#d9ead3", partial: "#fff2cc", incorrect: "#f4cccc", blank: "#ffffff" };
var LIVE_FIXED = ["Period", "Student", "Email", "Status", "Last activity", "Grade",
                  "Green", "Yellow", "Red", "Blank", "Checks"];
var LIVE_EMAIL_COL = 3;
var FIRST_Q_COL = LIVE_FIXED.length + 1;
var ROSTER_HEADERS = ["Period", "Name", "Email", "Classroom user ID", "Course ID"];
var SUMMARY_HEADERS = ["Question", "Prompt", "Green", "Yellow", "Red", "Blank", "% green (all periods)", "% right on the first try"];
/** The Question Summary's first columns: a review game has Times given after Blank (QUESTIONS PER TRY: not every try has every question). */
function summaryHeads_() { var h = SUMMARY_HEADERS.slice(); if (isGame_()) h.splice(6, 0, "Times given"); return h; }

Blank = every period closes at 11:59 PM on the last day."],
  ["Different start times on some last days", "CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES", "label", "For a quarter's last day with a different bell schedule (like a Thursday). Type the day, a colon, then period = time for that day, e.g. Thursday: 1 = 8:10 AM, 5 = 9:10 AM, 6 = 10:33 AM, 2 = 11:26 AM, 3 = 12:54 PM. The day can be a weekday (every quarter last day on that weekday), a date like 12/17/2026 (that one day), or Q2 (that quarter). Put a semicolon between changes. A date beats a quarter, which beats a weekday. A period you leave out keeps its usual time. Type none to always use the usual times. Self-Check > Show quarter close times shows the result."],
  ["Class Pass Google client ID", "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID", "text", "Leave blank. Class Pass (the student sign-in on devices where Google won't open this assignment) signs students in with Google using its own built-in client ID. Only if Google ever deletes that client (it does after about 6 months with no sign-ins): make a new one in Google Cloud and paste its client ID here (it ends in .apps.googleusercontent.com). Type off to hide the Sign in with Google button."],
  ["Allow emailed sign-in code", "ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE", "bool", "Unchecked = Class Pass signs students in with Google only: the code box is hidden and this sheet refuses code requests. Checked = students can also get a 6-digit code emailed to their school address from your Gmail (for when Google sign-in is blocked for students)."],
  ["Automatic grades after the due date", "AUTO_GRADES", "bool", "Checked = about an hour after each class's due date in Classroom, grades upload by themselves, then are re-sent once a day for the days set above. Unchecked = upload grades yourself with step 3. Grades only go to assignments made by step 2. You can also flip this from the Self-Check Hub. Takes effect at the next hourly check."]
];
// Limited tries (Answer type choice, truefalse or short; see LIMITED TRIES): a Self-Check's rows, not a review game's.
var TRY_SETTING_DEFS = [
  ["Tries for multiple choice", "TRIES_CHOICE", "number", "Only for questions whose Answer type is choice or dropdown. How many tries a student gets. A question's own Tries column (in the Questions tab) changes it for that question."],
  ["Tries for true/false", "TRIES_TF", "number", "Only for questions whose Answer type is truefalse. How many tries a student gets (1 = one try, since there are only two choices)."],
  ["Tries for short answers", "TRIES_SHORT", "number", "Only for questions whose Answer type is short (a one-line answer checked by its key words). How many tries a student gets."],
  ["Credit by try", "TRY_CREDIT", "label", "The share of a question's points when the student's first right answer comes on try 1, try 2, try 3 ..., separated by commas, e.g. 100%, 90%, 80%. Tries past the end of the list use the last one. Only for choice, truefalse and short questions."],
  ["Credit when never answered right", "TRIED_WRONG_CREDIT", "percent", "A choice, truefalse or short question the student tried but never answered right (out of tries, or stopped trying) earns this share of its points, e.g. 50%. A question never tried earns 0."],
  ["Open a hint before trying again", "HINT_BEFORE_RETRY", "bool", "Checked = after a wrong try, a student has to open a hint (the light bulb: a clue, an example or a video) before trying again. Only for questions that have hints in the Questions tab. Hints never cost points. Unchecked = they can try again right away."]
];
// Shared by every kind (a review game and a Self-Check's choice questions).
var SHUFFLE_CHOICES_DEF = ["Shuffle answer choices", "SHUFFLE_CHOICES", "bool", "Checked = each student sees the answer choices of a question in their own order (in a Self-Check, a new order after each wrong try). True/false is never shuffled. Unchecked = the order of the Choice columns."];
var SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS_DEF = ["Shuffle question order", "SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS", "bool", "Checked = each student gets the questions in their own order, so neighbors can't copy by number (a Self-Check: mixed within each blue section, numbered 1, 2, 3 ... in the student's own order, while Live Results keeps the Questions tab's numbers; a review game: a new order each time they start over). Unchecked = everyone gets the Questions tab's order."];
// A review game's own Settings rows (only a review-game sheet has them).
var GAME_SETTING_DEFS = [
  ["Students can start over", "START_OVER", "bool", "Checked = students see a Start Over button that clears their answers so they can try the whole game again. Their BEST try is their grade (in this sheet and in Classroom), and their best game points stay on the leaderboard. Unchecked = one try only (they can still leave and pick up where they left off)."],
  ["Questions per try", "QUESTIONS_PER_TRY", "number", "How many questions each student gets each time they play, picked at random from the Questions tab (a new mix every Start Over). Blank or 0 = every question."],
  SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS_DEF,
  ["Website leaderboard address", "WEBSITE_BOARD_URL", "text", "The class website whose Leaderboard page shows Review game points, like https://vocabulary-room901.pages.dev (just the address, nothing after .dev)."],
  ["Website leaderboard key", "WEBSITE_BOARD_KEY", "text", "Paste the Review game key from that website's Leaderboard page (sign in there as the teacher, then open Review game key at the bottom). Then each student's best game points go on the website's Leaderboard when they finish a game, and every hour for games still in progress. Blank = the points show only inside this game."]
];
if (triesPage_()) SETTING_DEFS = SETTING_DEFS.concat(TRY_SETTING_DEFS);
if (SKILL_NAME !== "net-force-escape-room") SETTING_DEFS = SETTING_DEFS.concat([SHUFFLE_CHOICES_DEF]);
if (triesPage_()) SETTING_DEFS = SETTING_DEFS.concat([SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS_DEF]);   // (a review game has it among its own rows)
/** Pages that show limited tries questions: the one- and two-column Self-Check pages (and the friction pages built on the two-column one),
 *  not the review game (its own multiple choice) or the escape room (its page is an older copy without the choice buttons). */
function triesPage_() { return SKILL_NAME !== "review-game" && SKILL_NAME !== "net-force-escape-room"; }
if (SKILL_NAME === "review-game") SETTING_DEFS = SETTING_DEFS.concat(GAME_SETTING_DEFS);
var AUTO_LABEL = "Automatic grades after the due date";
var Q_COLS = { section: "Section title (starts a blue strip)", secLabel: "Section button text", secUrl: "Section button link",
               secImg: "Section picture (Drive or https link)", secCap: "Section picture caption",
               q: "Question", img: "Picture (Drive or https link)", cap: "Picture caption",
               btnLabel: "Question button text", btnUrl: "Question button link",
               boxes: "Boxes on picture (label: left, top, width, height in %)",
               type: "Answer type",
               table: "Table config (JSON: the table's columns and rows)",
               sentences: "Minimum sentences (blank = no minimum)",
               short: "Allow short answer (x = skip the minimum length)",
               points: "Points (blank = 1)",
               right: "Right choice (a number, or 1, 3 for select all)", explain: "Explanation (shown after answering)",
               tries: "Tries (blank = Settings)", hintClue: "Hint: clue", hintEx: "Hint: example", hintVideo: "Hint: video or link" };
var CHOICE_COLS = 5;   // Choice 1 ... Choice 5 columns in a new review game Questions tab (up to 6 are read)
var TRY_CHOICE_COLS = 4;   // Choice 1 + Choice 1 feedback ... Choice 4 in a new Self-Check Questions tab (up to 6 are read)
var BOX_HINT = "";   // Settings "Picture box hint text" (blank = no hint in picture boxes)
var ARTICLE_DOC = "";   // Settings "Article Google Doc link" (a side-by-side reading page's article; see ARTICLE FROM A GOOGLE DOC)
var ARTICLE_BUTTON = "";   // Settings "Article button name" (a side-by-side page's left side: its button and phone tab; blank = Article, or Directions on a page with a simulation built in)
var SIM_URL = "";   // Settings "Simulation link" (a lab page with a simulation on the left: Directions / Simulation buttons; blank = the page's own address)
var SIM_BUTTON = "";   // Settings "Simulation button name" (blank = Simulation, or Simulation 1 with two)
var SIM2_URL = "";   // Settings "Simulation 2 link" (a second simulation: a third button on the left; blank = none)
var CLASSROOM_TEXT = SKILL_NAME === "review-game"   // Settings "Classroom instructions" (the Classroom assignment's text; step 2 uses it)
  ? "Open the link below and play the review game. You get one try at each question: right answers earn points, and a streak of right answers earns more. " +
    "Your work saves automatically. When you're done, click Mark as done here in Classroom."
  : "Open the link below and answer each question. Your answer box turns green, yellow, or red with feedback. Revise until you're mostly green! " +
    "Your work saves automatically. When you're done, click \"I'm finished\" on the page, then click Mark as done here in Classroom.";
var SIM2_BUTTON = "";   // Settings "Simulation 2 button name" (blank = Simulation 2)
var IDEA_COLS = 4;   // idea (hint + key words) column pairs added to a new Questions tab
var MIS_COLS = 2;    // misconception (hint + key words) column pairs added to the end of a Questions tab
var CONFIG_LOADED_ = false, CONFIG_WARNINGS_ = [];

// ---------- Speed: short-term cache so a whole class can check at once ----------
// Each save used to re-read the Settings, Questions and Roster tabs and rebuild the
// Question Summary while every other student waited in line. These keep copies for a
// minute or so. The Question Summary now refreshes at most once a minute.
var CFG_CACHE_SECS = 60, ROSTER_CACHE_SECS = 600;
function cache_() { try { return CacheService.getScriptCache(); } catch (e) { return null; } }
function cacheRaw_(k) { var c = cache_(); if (!c) return null; try { return c.get(k); } catch (e) { return null; } }
function cacheGet_(k) { var v = cacheRaw_(k); try { return v ? JSON.parse(v) : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
function cachePut_(k, v, secs) { var c = cache_(); if (!c) return; try { var s = JSON.stringify(v); if (s.length < 95000) c.put(k, s, secs); } catch (e) {} }
function cacheDel_(keys) {
  if (keys.length > 100) { for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i += 100) cacheDel_(keys.slice(i, i + 100)); return; } var c = cache_(); if (c) try { c.removeAll(keys); } catch (e) {} }
function clearConfigCache_() { cacheDel_(["cfg:settings", "cfg:questions", "roster"]); }
/** A long text (an article with its pictures, a picture) kept as pieces of BIG_PART characters: key#n = "count:length", then key#0, key#1 ...
    Gives null when any piece is missing (the cache drops things when it likes), so the caller reads the real thing again.
    32,000 characters: a piece of curly quotes and accents (3 bytes each) still fits the cache's 100 KB per piece. */
var BIG_PART = 32000, BIG_MAX_PARTS = 100;
function bigCacheGet_(key) {
  var c = cache_();
  if (!c) return null;
  try {
    var h = /^(\d+):(\d+)$/.exec(c.get(key + "#n") || ""), n = h ? Number(h[1]) : 0, keys = [], s = "";
    if (!(n >= 1 && n <= BIG_MAX_PARTS)) return null;
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) keys.push(key + "#" + i);
    var got = c.getAll(keys);
    for (var j = 0; j < n; j++) { if (got[keys[j]] == null) return null; s += got[keys[j]]; }
    return s.length === Number(h[2]) ? s : null;
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}
function bigCachePut_(key, s, secs) {
  var c = cache_(), vals = {}, n = 0;
  if (!c || s.length > BIG_PART * BIG_MAX_PARTS) return;
  do { vals[key + "#" + n] = s.substr(n * BIG_PART, BIG_PART); n++; } while (n * BIG_PART < s.length);
  vals[key + "#n"] = n + ":" + s.length;
  try { c.putAll(vals, secs); } catch (e) {}
}

function getGlobal_(name) {
  var p = name.split(".");
  return p.length === 2 ? globalThis[p[0]][p[1]] : globalThis[name];
}
function setGlobal_(name, v) {
  var p = name.split(".");
  if (p.length === 2) globalThis[p[0]][p[1]] = v; else globalThis[name] = v;
}

/** Reads the Settings + Questions tabs into the variables the rest of the script uses. */
function loadConfig_() {
  if (CONFIG_LOADED_) return;
  CONFIG_LOADED_ = true;
  // The spreadsheet is opened only when a short-term copy is missing (opening it is the slow part of a student's request).
  var ss = null;
  var setVals = cacheGet_("cfg:settings");
  if (!setVals) {
    if (!(ss = openSS_())) return;
    var st = ss.getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS);
    setVals = st && st.getLastRow() > 1 ? st.getRange(2, 1, st.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues() : [];
    cachePut_("cfg:settings", setVals, CFG_CACHE_SECS);
  }
  if (setVals.length) {
    var m = {};
    setVals.forEach(function (r) { m[String(r[0]).trim().toLowerCase()] = r[1]; });
    SETTING_DEFS.forEach(function (d) {
      var v = m[d[0].toLowerCase()];
      if (v === "" || v == null) return;
      if (Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]" && (d[2] === "text" || d[2] === "label")) v = Utilities.formatDate(v, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "M/d/yyyy");   // one typed date
      if (d[2] === "text" || d[2] === "label") setGlobal_(d[1], String(v).trim());
      else if (d[2] === "bool") setGlobal_(d[1], v === true || /^(true|yes|y|1|x)$/i.test(String(v).trim()));
      else {
        var n = typeof v === "number" ? v : parseFloat(String(v).replace("%", ""));
        if (typeof v !== "number" && String(v).indexOf("%") !== -1) n = n / 100;
        if (d[2] === "percent" && n > 1) n = n / 100;      // typed 85 instead of 85%
        if (!isNaN(n)) setGlobal_(d[1], n); else CONFIG_WARNINGS_.push("Settings: \"" + d[0] + "\" isn't a number.");
      }
    });
  }
  var qVals = cacheGet_("cfg:questions");
  if (!qVals) {
    if (!ss && !(ss = openSS_())) return;
    var qs = ss.getSheetByName(SH_QUESTIONS);
    qVals = qs && qs.getLastRow() > 1 ? qs.getDataRange().getValues() : [];
    cachePut_("cfg:questions", qVals, CFG_CACHE_SECS);
  }
  if (qVals.length > 1) {
    var parsed = parseQuestions_(qVals);
    CONFIG_WARNINGS_ = CONFIG_WARNINGS_.concat(parsed.warnings);
    if (!parsed.items.length) throw new Error("This assignment doesn't have any questions yet. Please tell your teacher.");
    ITEMS = parsed.items; SECTIONS = parsed.sections; LINKS = [];
  }
}

function parseQuestions_(rows) {
  var head = rows[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  function col(label) { return head.indexOf(label.toLowerCase()); }
  var c = {}; Object.keys(Q_COLS).forEach(function (k) { c[k] = col(Q_COLS[k]); });
  c.type = typeCol_(head);
  // Columns found by how their header starts, so older tabs with longer headers still work.
  function startsCol(re) { for (var k = 0; k < head.length; k++) if (re.test(head[k])) return k; return -1; }
  c.secImg = startsCol(/^section picture(?! caption)/); c.secCap = startsCol(/^section picture caption/);
  c.table = startsCol(/^table config/); c.sentences = startsCol(/^minimum sentences/); c.short = startsCol(/^allow short answer/); c.points = startsCol(/^points\b/);
  c.right = startsCol(/^right (choice|answer)/); c.explain = startsCol(/^explanation/);
  c.tries = startsCol(/^tries\b/); c.hintClue = startsCol(/^hint:?\s*clue/); c.hintEx = startsCol(/^hint:?\s*example/); c.hintVideo = startsCol(/^hint:?\s*(video|link)/);
  var choiceCols = [], fbCols = [];   // Choice 1, Choice 2 ... columns (a review game's, or a Self-Check's choice questions) and each one's feedback column (-1 = none)
  for (var cn = 1; cn <= 6; cn++) {
    var cc = startsCol(new RegExp("^(choice|option) " + cn + "\\b(?!\\s*feedback)"));
    if (cc !== -1) { choiceCols.push(cc); fbCols.push(startsCol(new RegExp("^(choice|option) " + cn + "\\s+feedback"))); }
  }
  var ideas = [], misCols = [];
  for (var n = 1; n <= 20; n++) {
    var h = col("Idea " + n + " hint"), k = col("Idea " + n + " key words");
    if (h !== -1 || k !== -1) ideas.push({ n: n, hint: h, keys: k });
    var mh = col("Misconception " + n + " hint"), mk = col("Misconception " + n + " key words");
    if (mh !== -1 || mk !== -1) misCols.push({ n: n, hint: mh, keys: mk });
  }
  var items = [], sections = [], warnings = [], pendingSec = null, graphRows = [];
  if (c.q === -1) return { items: [], sections: [], warnings: ["Questions tab: the \"" + Q_COLS.q + "\" column header is missing."] };
  if (isGame_() && !choiceCols.length) warnings.push("Questions tab: a review game needs Choice 1, Choice 2 ... columns. Run Set up / repair sheets to add them.");
  function cell(r, i) { return i === -1 ? "" : String(r[i] == null ? "" : r[i]).trim(); }
  for (var ri = 1; ri < rows.length; ri++) {
    var r = rows[ri], rowNo = ri + 1;
    var secTitle = cell(r, c.section);
    if (secTitle) {
      pendingSec = { title: secTitle, links: [] };
      var sl = cell(r, c.secUrl);
      if (sl) {
        var su = buttonUrl_(sl);
        if (su) pendingSec.links.push({ label: cell(r, c.secLabel) || (isTimerUrl_(su) ? "Show the timer" : "Open the simulation"), url: su });
        else warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + ": the section button link must start with https:// or be a timer like timer:120 (seconds)");
      }
      var si = cell(r, c.secImg);
      if (si) {
        if (imageUrl_(si)) { pendingSec.img = si; if (cell(r, c.secCap)) pendingSec.imgCaption = cell(r, c.secCap); }
        else warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + ": the section picture must be a Google Drive share link or an https:// picture address, so it's left out.");
      }
    } else if (cell(r, c.secImg)) {
      warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + ": a Section picture only shows on a row that also has a Section title (it goes right under that blue strip), so it's left out." +
        (isDrawType_(cell(r, c.type)) ? " For students to draw on it, move the link to this row's Picture column." : ""));
    }
    var q = cell(r, c.q);
    if (!q) continue;
    var ty = cell(r, c.type).toLowerCase(), at = "Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ")";
    var game = isGame_() ? choicesFrom_(r, choiceCols, cell(r, c.right), at, warnings) : null;
    if (isGame_() && !game) continue;   // a review game only has multiple choice questions (choicesFrom_ said why this row is left out)
    var tk = isGame_() ? "" : triedKind_(ty);   // choice, truefalse or short: limited tries (see LIMITED TRIES)
    if (tk && !triesPage_()) { warnings.push(at + ": Answer type \"" + ty + "\" (limited tries) needs the one-column or two-column Self-Check page, so it's a typed answer here."); tk = ""; ty = ""; }
    var picks = tk === "choice" || tk === "truefalse" ? triedChoices_(r, choiceCols, fbCols, cell(r, c.right), tk, at, warnings) : null;
    if ((tk === "choice" || tk === "truefalse") && !picks) continue;   // too few choices, or no usable Right choice (triedChoices_ said why)
    var drop = tk === "dropdown" ? dropdownFrom_(r, choiceCols, fbCols, cell(r, c.right), q, at, warnings) : null;
    if (tk === "dropdown" && !drop) continue;   // no ___, too few choices, or a Right choice that doesn't fit (dropdownFrom_ said why)
    if (pendingSec) { pendingSec.before = items.length; sections.push(pendingSec); pendingSec = null; }
    var item = { q: q, criteria: [] };
    if (game) {
      item.choices = game.choices; item.right = game.right;
      if (cell(r, c.explain)) item.explain = cell(r, c.explain).slice(0, 1000);
      ty = "";
    } else if (tk) {
      item.kind = tk;
      if (picks) { item.choices = picks.choices; item.right = picks.right; }
      if (drop) {   // DROPDOWNS: the options (every dropdown offers them all), one right option per ___, box N's hint = Idea N hint, a table to show
        item.choices = drop.choices; item.right = drop.right; item.blanks = drop.right.length;
        var dh = [];
        for (var bk = 0; bk < item.blanks; bk++) {
          var idc = ideas.filter(function (x) { return x.n === bk + 1; })[0];
          dh.push(idc ? cell(r, idc.hint).slice(0, 1000) : "");
        }
        while (dh.length && !dh[dh.length - 1]) dh.pop();
        if (dh.length) item.dhints = dh;
        ideas.forEach(function (x) {
          if (cell(r, x.keys)) warnings.push(at + " is a dropdown question, so its Idea " + x.n + " key words are ignored (Right choice grades it; Idea " + x.n + " hint is what students see when box " + x.n + " is wrong).");
          else if (x.n > item.blanks && cell(r, x.hint)) warnings.push(at + ": Idea " + x.n + " hint is for box " + x.n + ", but the question has " + item.blanks + (item.blanks === 1 ? " box" : " boxes") + " (___), so it's ignored.");
        });
        var tcv = cell(r, c.table);
        if (tcv) { var dt = displayTable_(tcv); if (dt.table) item.dtable = dt.table; if (dt.warn) warnings.push(at + ": " + dt.warn); }
      }
      if (cell(r, c.explain)) item.explain = cell(r, c.explain).slice(0, 1000);
      else warnings.push(at + " has no Explanation, so students only see that it's done. Type a short reason in Explanation (they see it once they get it right or run out of tries).");
      var tv = cell(r, c.tries);
      if (tv !== "") {
        if (/^\d+$/.test(tv) && +tv >= 1 && +tv <= 10) item.tries = +tv;
        else warnings.push(at + ": Tries \"" + tv + "\" isn't a whole number from 1 to 10, so this question uses Settings.");
      }
      ty = "";
    }
    var hints = {}, hv = cell(r, c.hintVideo);
    if (cell(r, c.hintClue)) hints.clue = cell(r, c.hintClue).slice(0, 1000);
    if (cell(r, c.hintEx)) hints.example = cell(r, c.hintEx).slice(0, 1000);
    if (hv) {
      if (/^https:\/\/[^\s"'<>\\]+$/i.test(hv)) hints.video = hv;
      else warnings.push(at + ": the Hint: video or link cell must be an https:// link, so it's left out.");
    }
    if (Object.keys(hints).length) {
      if (tk) item.hints = hints;
      else if (!game) warnings.push(at + " has hints, but hints only show on choice, truefalse, short and dropdown questions (Answer type), so they're ignored.");
    }
    if (isDrawType_(ty)) { item.upload = true; item.draw = true; }
    else if (isUploadType_(ty)) item.upload = true;
    else if (/^(table|data)/.test(ty)) {
      var tc = parseTableConfig_(cell(r, c.table));
      if (tc.table) item.table = tc.table;
      else warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + "): " + tc.error + " So it's a typed answer for now.");
    }
    else if (ty && !/^(text|typed|answer|rubric)$/.test(ty)) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + "): Answer type \"" + ty + "\" isn't one this version knows (blank = typed answer, choice, truefalse, short, dropdown, table, draw = drawing pad, upload = picture upload), so it's a typed answer.");
    var ms = parseInt(cell(r, c.sentences), 10);
    if (ms > 0) item.minSentences = Math.min(ms, 20);
    if (/^(x|yes|y|true|1)$/i.test(cell(r, c.short))) item.short = true;
    var pts = cell(r, c.points);
    if (pts !== "") {   // what this question is worth (blank = 1, the same as every other question)
      var pv = parseFloat(pts);
      if (isNaN(pv) || pv < 0) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + "): Points \"" + pts + "\" isn't a number, so this question counts 1.");
      else item.points = Math.min(pv, 1000);
    }
    if (cell(r, c.img)) item.img = cell(r, c.img);
    if (cell(r, c.cap)) item.imgCaption = cell(r, c.cap);
    var bx = cell(r, c.boxes);
    if (bx) {
      var pb = parseBoxes_(bx);
      if (pb.warnings.length) pb.warnings.forEach(function (w) { warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + ": " + w); });
      if (pb.boxes.length) item.boxes = pb.boxes;
    }
    var bu = cell(r, c.btnUrl);
    if (bu) {
      var qu = buttonUrl_(bu);
      if (qu) item.links = [{ label: cell(r, c.btnLabel) || (isTimerUrl_(qu) ? "Show the timer" : "Open link"), url: qu }];
      else warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + ": the question button link must start with https:// or be a timer like timer:120 (seconds)");
    }
    ideas.forEach(function (idea) {
      if (item.kind === "dropdown") return;   // (its Idea hints are its boxes' hints, read above)
      var keys = cell(r, idea.keys), hint = cell(r, idea.hint);
      if (!keys) {
        if (hint) warnings.push("Row " + rowNo + " (Q" + (items.length + 1) + "): Idea " + idea.n + " has a hint but no key words, so it's ignored.");
        return;
      }
      var need = parseKeyWords_(keys);
      if (!need.length) return;
      var crit = { nudge: hint || "Reread the question and add the missing idea.", need: need };
      var rowTag = /^\[([^\]]{1,40})\]\s*/.exec(hint);   // "[Use of Data] Cite a number..." = the idea belongs to the rubric row "Use of Data"
      if (rowTag) { crit.row = rowTag[1].trim(); crit.nudge = hint.slice(rowTag[0].length) || "Reread the question and add the missing idea."; }
      item.criteria.push(crit);
    });
    misCols.forEach(function (mc) {   // misconceptions: words that show a wrong idea; their hint is shown and the answer can't turn green
      var keys = cell(r, mc.keys), hint = cell(r, mc.hint), at = "Row " + rowNo + " (Q" + (items.length + 1) + "): Misconception " + mc.n;
      if (!keys) {
        if (hint) warnings.push(at + " has a hint but no key words, so it's ignored.");
        return;
      }
      var need = parseKeyWords_(keys);
      if (!need.length) return;
      if (!hint) warnings.push(at + " has key words but no hint, so students only see a general tip. Type what they should rethink in its hint.");
      if (need.every(function (g) { return g[0] === "!"; })) warnings.push(at + " has only NOT words, so it shows up on every answer that leaves those words out. Add the words that show the wrong idea, like  second, sec + NOT cm, meter.");
      (item.mis = item.mis || []).push({ nudge: hint || "Part of your answer isn't right. Reread it and rethink that part.", need: need });
    });
    if (item.kind && item.kind !== "short") {   // choice, true/false and dropdown: graded by Right choice only
      var kn = { choice: "multiple choice", truefalse: "true/false", dropdown: "dropdown" }[item.kind];
      if (item.criteria.length || item.mis) warnings.push(at + " is a " + kn + " question, so its " + (item.kind === "dropdown" ? "Misconception" : "Idea and Misconception") + " key words are ignored (Right choice grades it).");
      if (item.boxes) warnings.push(at + " is a " + kn + " question, so its picture boxes are ignored.");
      item.criteria = []; delete item.mis; delete item.boxes;
    }
    if (item.kind === "short") {
      if (item.boxes) { warnings.push(at + " is a short answer (one line), so its picture boxes are ignored."); delete item.boxes; }
      if (!item.criteria.length) warnings.push(at + " is a short answer with no key words, so any answer counts as right on the first try. Type the right answer's key words in Idea 1 key words.");
    }
    if (item.boxes) {
      if (item.boxes.length !== item.criteria.length) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") has " + item.boxes.length + " boxes on the picture but " + item.criteria.length + " ideas with key words. Box 1 uses Idea 1, box 2 uses Idea 2, and so on, so these should match.");
      if (!item.img) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") has picture boxes but no picture yet, so students see the boxes as a plain list until you paste the picture link.");
    }
    if (item.table && item.table.graph) {
      var gTop = graphHasTop_(item), gTitle = !!item.table.graph.title;
      var nAxes = item.table.graph.labels === false ? 0 : (gTop ? 3 : 2) + (gTitle ? 1 : 0);
      if (item.criteria.length > nAxes && nAxes) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") is a graph: Idea 1 checks the x-axis label, Idea 2 the y-axis label" + (gTop ? ", Idea 3 the top axis label" : "") + (gTitle ? ", Idea " + nAxes + " the graph title" : "") + ". Ideas after " + nAxes + " are ignored.");
      item.criteria = item.criteria.slice(0, nAxes);
      graphRows.push({ n: items.length + 1, rowNo: rowNo, item: item });
    }
    if (item.table && !item.table.graph && item.criteria.length > 3) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") is a data table: Idea 1 checks the graph title, Idea 2 the x-axis label and Idea 3 the y-axis label. Ideas after 3 are ignored.");
    if (item.table) item.criteria = item.criteria.slice(0, 3);
    if (item.upload) {
      if (item.criteria.length) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") is a picture upload, so its key words are ignored (uploads aren't graded).");
      if (item.boxes) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") is a picture upload, so its picture boxes are ignored.");
      item.criteria = []; delete item.boxes;
    } else if (!item.criteria.length && !item.table && !item.choices && !item.kind) warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + ") has no key words, so any answer at least the minimum length counts as green.");
    if (item.mis && (item.upload || item.table || item.boxes)) {
      warnings.push("Q" + (items.length + 1) + " (row " + rowNo + "): misconceptions only check typed answers (not tables, picture boxes or uploads), so this question's are ignored.");
      delete item.mis;
    }
    items.push(item);
  }
  graphRows.forEach(function (g) {   // each line of a graph needs a question with boxes to take its numbers from
    g.item.table.graph.lines.forEach(function (L, k) {
      var at = "Q" + g.n + " (row " + g.rowNo + "): line " + (k + 1) + " of the graph (" + L.name + ") ", src = items[L.from - 1];
      if (!src || L.from === g.n) { warnings.push(at + "takes its numbers from Q" + L.from + ", " + (src ? "which is this graph itself" : "but there is no Q" + L.from) + ". Set \"from\" to the question with the numbers."); return; }
      if (L.xcol != null) {   // columns of a data table
        if (!(src.table && !src.table.graph && !src.table.lab)) warnings.push(at + "takes columns from Q" + L.from + ", which isn't a data table.");
        else if (Math.max(L.col, L.xcol) >= src.table.cols.length) warnings.push(at + "uses column " + Math.max(L.col, L.xcol) + ", but Q" + L.from + "'s table has columns 0 to " + (src.table.cols.length - 1) + ".");
        return;
      }
      var count = src.boxes ? src.boxes.length : src.table && !src.table.graph && !src.table.lab ? src.table.inputs : 0;
      if (!count) warnings.push(at + "takes its numbers from Q" + L.from + ", which has no boxes to type numbers in (it needs picture boxes or a data table).");
      else if (count !== L.x.length) warnings.push(at + "has " + L.x.length + " x numbers, but Q" + L.from + " has " + count + " boxes. Give one x number for each box" + (count > L.x.length ? " (the extra boxes are left off the graph)." : "."));
    });
  });
  return { items: items, sections: sections, warnings: warnings };
}

/** The Questions tab's answer-type column: "Answer type", or an older column whose header starts with "Type". -1 = none. */
function typeCol_(head) {
  var i = head.indexOf(Q_COLS.type.toLowerCase());
  if (i !== -1) return i;
  for (var k = 0; k < head.length; k++) if (/^type\b/.test(head[k])) return k;
  return -1;
}
/** "upload" (also picture, photo, image, sketch, drawing) = a picture-upload question. */
function isUploadType_(t) { return /^(upload|picture|photo|image)/i.test(String(t || "").trim()); }
/** "draw" (also drawing, sketch) = a drawing pad: students draw right on the page; saved like a picture upload. */
function isDrawType_(t) { return /^(draw|drawing|sketch)/i.test(String(t || "").trim()); }

/** A review game sheet (SKILL_NAME "review-game"): multiple choice questions only, its own page (/pass/review/). See REVIEW GAME. */
function isGame_() { return SKILL_NAME === "review-game"; }
/** One review game row's choices: { choices: [{ t, img }], right: [0-based, sorted] }, or null (and a warning) for a row the game leaves out.
 *  A choice cell is its words, or just a picture link (Drive share link or https picture address). Right choice: 2, or 1, 3 (or B, or A, C). */
function choicesFrom_(r, cols, rightText, at, warnings) {
  var choices = [], place = {};   // place[column number - 1] = that choice's place (an empty Choice cell is skipped, the numbers stay the columns')
  cols.forEach(function (ci, k) {
    var v = String(r[ci] == null ? "" : r[ci]).trim();
    if (!v) return;
    var pic = /^\S+$/.test(v) && /^https:\/\//.test(v) ? imageUrl_(v) : "";
    place[k] = choices.length;
    choices.push(pic ? { t: "", img: v } : { t: v.slice(0, 500) });
  });
  if (!cols.length) return null;   // (parseQuestions_ says so once)
  if (choices.length < 2) { warnings.push(at + " has " + (choices.length ? "only one choice" : "no choices") + ", so the game leaves it out. Type at least two choices in the Choice columns."); return null; }
  var right = [];
  String(rightText || "").toUpperCase().split(/[\s,;&+]+|\band\b/i).forEach(function (p) {
    if (!p) return;
    var k = /^\d+$/.test(p) ? Number(p) - 1 : /^[A-F]$/.test(p) ? p.charCodeAt(0) - 65 : -1, n = place.hasOwnProperty(k) ? place[k] : -1;
    if (n >= 0 && right.indexOf(n) === -1) right.push(n);
  });
  if (!right.length) { warnings.push(at + ": the Right choice cell" + (rightText ? " (\"" + rightText + "\") doesn't name one of its " + choices.length + " choices" : " is empty") + ", so the game leaves it out. Type the number of the right choice, like 2 (or 1, 3 when more than one is right)."); return null; }
  return { choices: choices, right: right.sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }) };
}

/** A Self-Check's Answer type with limited tries: "choice", "truefalse", "short" or "dropdown" (see LIMITED TRIES), or "" for any other. */
function triedKind_(ty) {
  ty = String(ty || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (/^(choice|choices|multiple choice|multiple-choice|mc|mcq)$/.test(ty)) return "choice";
  if (/^(truefalse|true\/false|true-false|true false|true or false|tf|t\/f)$/.test(ty)) return "truefalse";
  if (/^(short|short answer)$/.test(ty)) return "short";
  if (/^(dropdown|dropdowns|drop down|drop downs|drop-down|drop-downs)$/.test(ty)) return "dropdown";
  return "";
}
/** DROPDOWNS (2026.11.06d, Donny's IXL practice): a dropdown row -> { choices: [{ t, fb }], right: [the right option for each ___, 0-based] },
 *  or null (and a warning) when students can't answer it yet. Every ___ (3 or more underscores) in the question is one dropdown, in order,
 *  and every dropdown offers all the Choice cells (never shuffled). Right choice: one answer per ___, separated by commas: choice
 *  numbers (2, 3, 2) or the options' own words (1/2, equal to, 1/2); separate them with | when an option has a comma in it. */
function dropdownFrom_(r, cols, fbCols, rightText, q, at, warnings) {
  function text(ci) { return ci == null || ci === -1 ? "" : String(r[ci] == null ? "" : r[ci]).trim(); }
  var blanks = (String(q).match(/_{3,}/g) || []).length, choices = [], place = {};
  if (!blanks) { warnings.push(at + " is a dropdown question with no ___ (three underscores) in it, so students don't see it yet. Type ___ in the question where each dropdown goes."); return null; }
  if (!cols.length) { warnings.push(at + " is a dropdown question, but the Questions tab has no Choice 1, Choice 2 ... columns. Run Set up / repair sheets to add them."); return null; }
  cols.forEach(function (ci, k) {
    var v = text(ci), f = text(fbCols[k]);
    if (!v) { if (f) warnings.push(at + ": Choice " + (k + 1) + " feedback has no Choice " + (k + 1) + " to go with, so it's ignored."); return; }
    if (/^\S+$/.test(v) && /^https:\/\//.test(v) && imageUrl_(v)) warnings.push(at + ": Choice " + (k + 1) + " is a link, but a dropdown can only show words, so students see the link itself.");
    var ch = { t: v.slice(0, 200) };
    if (f) ch.fb = f.slice(0, 1000);
    place[k] = choices.length;
    choices.push(ch);
  });
  if (choices.length < 2) { warnings.push(at + " is a dropdown question with " + (choices.length ? "only one choice" : "no choices") + ", so students don't see it yet. Type the options every dropdown offers in Choice 1, Choice 2 ..."); return null; }
  var s = String(rightText == null ? "" : rightText).trim();
  if (!s) { warnings.push(at + ": the Right choice cell is empty, so students don't see this dropdown question yet. Type one answer for each ___, in order, separated by commas, like 2, 3, 2 (or the words: 1/2, equal to, 1/2)."); return null; }
  var parts = s.split(s.indexOf("|") !== -1 ? /\s*\|\s*/ : /\s*[,;]\s*/).filter(function (p) { return p !== ""; }), right = [], bad = [];
  parts.forEach(function (p) {
    var low = p.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " "), byText = -1, n = -1;
    choices.forEach(function (ch, j) { if (byText === -1 && ch.t.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ") === low) byText = j; });
    if (/^\d$/.test(p) && place.hasOwnProperty(Number(p) - 1)) {
      n = place[Number(p) - 1];
      if (byText !== -1 && byText !== n) warnings.push(at + ": Right choice \"" + p + "\" is read as Choice " + p + " (" + choices[n].t + "), not the option \"" + choices[byText].t + "\". Use the choice numbers to be sure.");
    } else n = byText;
    if (n === -1) bad.push(p);
    right.push(n);
  });
  if (bad.length) { warnings.push(at + ": Right choice " + bad.map(function (b) { return "\"" + b + "\""; }).join(", ") + " doesn't match one of its " + choices.length + " choices, so students don't see this dropdown question yet. Use the choice numbers (2 = Choice 2) or the choices' own words."); return null; }
  if (right.length !== blanks) { warnings.push(at + ": the Right choice cell has " + right.length + (right.length === 1 ? " answer" : " answers") + ", but the question has " + blanks + " ___ " + (blanks === 1 ? "box" : "boxes") + ", so students don't see it yet. Type one answer for each ___, in order, separated by commas."); return null; }
  if (choices[right[0]].fb && right.every(function (m) { return m === right[0]; })) warnings.push(at + ": \"" + choices[right[0]].t + "\" is the right answer in every box, so its Choice feedback is never shown.");
  return { choices: choices, right: right };
}
/** A dropdown question's Table config: a small table shown above its sentences, display only (no boxes, not graded, no graph):
 *  {"cols":["Mammal","Mass (kg)"],"rows":[["lion",125],["wildebeest",250]]} -> { table: { cols, rows (all text) }, warn }. */
function displayTable_(text) {
  var t;
  try { t = JSON.parse(text); } catch (e) { return { table: null, warn: "the Table config isn't valid JSON (check the quotes, commas and brackets), so the table is left out. Example: {\"cols\":[\"Mammal\",\"Mass (kg)\"],\"rows\":[[\"lion\",125],[\"wildebeest\",250]]}" }; }
  if (!t || !Array.isArray(t.cols) || !t.cols.length || !Array.isArray(t.rows)) return { table: null, warn: "the Table config needs \"cols\" (the column names) and \"rows\" (a list of rows), so the table is left out." };
  var n = Math.min(t.cols.length, 12), empty = 0;
  function txt(v) { if (v == null || v === "") { empty++; return ""; } return String(v).slice(0, 200); }
  var out = { cols: t.cols.slice(0, n).map(txt), rows: t.rows.slice(0, 40).map(function (row) {
    var r = Array.isArray(row) ? row.slice(0, n) : [row];
    while (r.length < n) r.push(null);
    return r.map(txt);
  }) };
  return { table: out, warn: empty ? "the Table config has " + empty + " empty " + (empty === 1 ? "cell" : "cells") + " (null). A dropdown's table is only for reading (students don't type in it), so " + (empty === 1 ? "it shows" : "they show") + " blank." : "" };
}
/** A dropdown try ("2,3,1") -> the option picked in each box (0-based), -1 for a box left blank or a number out of range. */
function dropPicks_(item, answer) {
  var p = String(answer == null ? "" : answer).split(/\s*,\s*/), out = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < item.blanks; k++) {
    var n = /^\d+$/.test(p[k] || "") ? Number(p[k]) - 1 : -1;
    out.push(n >= 0 && n < item.choices.length ? n : -1);
  }
  return out;
}
/** One dropdown try: right only when every box is right. A wrong try names what to fix in each wrong box: box N's Idea N hint, else that
 *  option's Choice feedback, else "That isn't the right answer." (Box N: in front when more than one box is wrong). marks = each box right or not. */
function dropGrade_(item, answer) {
  var p = dropPicks_(item, answer), wrong = [];
  if (p.indexOf(-1) !== -1) return { status: "blank", message: "" };
  var marks = p.map(function (n, k) { if (n !== item.right[k]) wrong.push(k); return n === item.right[k]; });
  if (!wrong.length) return { status: "correct", message: "That's right!", marks: marks };
  var said = wrong.map(function (k) { return (item.dhints && item.dhints[k]) || item.choices[p[k]].fb || ""; });
  var msg = said.every(function (m) { return !m; })
    ? (wrong.length === 1 ? "That isn't the right answer." : "The red boxes aren't the right answers yet.")
    : wrong.map(function (k, j) { return (wrong.length > 1 ? "Box " + (k + 1) + ": " : "") + (said[j] || "That isn't the right answer."); }).join(" ");
  return { status: wrong.length < p.length ? "partial" : "incorrect", message: msg, marks: marks };
}
/** "That didn't use a try" notes for a try that isn't a new one. */
var DROP_BLANK_NOTE = "Choose an answer in every box. (That didn't use a try.)";
function repeatNote_(item) { return item.kind === "dropdown" ? "You already tried those answers. (That didn't use a try.)" : TRY_REPEAT_NOTE; }
/** The name of a picture choice for the teacher, from an https picture address ("KE mass - curve" from .../KE%20mass%20-%20curve.png); "" for a Drive link. */
function pictureName_(u) {
  u = String(u || "");
  if (/drive\.google\.com/.test(u)) return "";
  var m = /\/([^\/?#]+?)(\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|svg|bmp))?(?:[?#].*)?$/i.exec(u), s = "";
  try { s = m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : ""; } catch (e) { s = m ? m[1] : ""; }
  return s.replace(/[_+]+/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, 60);
}
/** A Self-Check choice or true/false row: { choices: [{ t, img, fb }], right: [0-based] }, or null (and a warning) when students can't
 *  answer it yet. fb = what a student sees after picking that wrong choice (its "Choice N feedback" cell). A true/false question's
 *  choices are always TRUE and FALSE (its Choice 1 and 2 cells can stay blank; Choice 1 feedback is for TRUE, Choice 2 feedback for FALSE). */
function triedChoices_(r, cols, fbCols, rightText, kind, at, warnings) {
  function text(ci) { return ci == null || ci === -1 ? "" : String(r[ci] == null ? "" : r[ci]).trim(); }
  var choices = [], place = {}, colOf = [], what = kind === "truefalse" ? "true/false" : "multiple choice";
  if (kind === "truefalse") {
    choices = [{ t: "TRUE" }, { t: "FALSE" }]; place = { 0: 0, 1: 1 }; colOf = [1, 2];
    [0, 1].forEach(function (k) { var f = text(fbCols[k]); if (f) choices[k].fb = f.slice(0, 1000); });
  } else {
    if (!cols.length) { warnings.push(at + " is a multiple choice question, but the Questions tab has no Choice 1, Choice 2 ... columns. Run Set up / repair sheets to add them."); return null; }
    cols.forEach(function (ci, k) {
      var v = text(ci), f = text(fbCols[k]);
      if (!v) { if (f) warnings.push(at + ": Choice " + (k + 1) + " feedback has no Choice " + (k + 1) + " to go with, so it's ignored."); return; }
      var pic = /^\S+$/.test(v) && /^https:\/\//.test(v) ? imageUrl_(v) : "";
      var ch = pic ? { t: "", img: v } : { t: v.slice(0, 500) };
      if (f) ch.fb = f.slice(0, 1000);
      place[k] = choices.length; colOf.push(k + 1);
      choices.push(ch);
    });
    if (choices.length < 2) { warnings.push(at + " is a multiple choice question with " + (choices.length ? "only one choice" : "no choices") + ", so students don't see it yet. Type at least two choices in the Choice columns."); return null; }
  }
  var right = rightChoices_(rightText, choices, place, kind);
  if (!right.length) {
    warnings.push(at + ": the Right choice cell" + (rightText ? " (\"" + rightText + "\") doesn't match one of its " + choices.length + " choices" : " is empty") + ", so students don't see this " + what + " question yet. " +
      (kind === "truefalse" ? "Type TRUE or FALSE." : "Type the number of the right choice, like 2 (or the right choice's own words)."));
    return null;
  }
  if (right.length > 1) { warnings.push(at + ": the Right choice cell names " + right.length + " choices, but a Self-Check question has one right choice, so only choice " + colOf[right[0]] + " counts."); right = right.slice(0, 1); }
  if (choices[right[0]].fb) warnings.push(at + ": Choice " + colOf[right[0]] + " is the right choice, so its feedback is never shown (Choice feedback is for wrong choices; the Explanation is shown instead).");
  return { choices: choices, right: right };
}
/** Right choice cell -> [0-based choices]: numbers (2, or 1, 3), letters (B), or the right choice's own words (case doesn't matter);
 *  for true/false also TRUE / FALSE / T / F (1 = TRUE, 2 = FALSE). place = column order -> place in choices (an empty Choice cell is skipped). */
function rightChoices_(rightText, choices, place, kind) {
  var s = String(rightText == null ? "" : rightText).trim(), out = [];
  if (!s) return out;
  if (kind === "truefalse") { var tf = /^(true|t|1)$/i.test(s) ? 0 : /^(false|f|2)$/i.test(s) ? 1 : -1; return tf === -1 ? [] : [tf]; }
  if (/^([1-9]|[a-f])(\s*([,;&+]|\band\b)\s*([1-9]|[a-f]))*$/i.test(s)) {
    s.toUpperCase().split(/[\s,;&+]+|\bAND\b/).forEach(function (p) {
      var k = /^\d$/.test(p) ? Number(p) - 1 : /^[A-F]$/.test(p) ? p.charCodeAt(0) - 65 : -1, n = place.hasOwnProperty(k) ? place[k] : -1;
      if (n >= 0 && out.indexOf(n) === -1) out.push(n);
    });
    if (out.length) return out;
  }
  var low = s.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  choices.forEach(function (ch, n) { if (!ch.img && ch.t.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ") === low) out.push(n); });
  return out;
}
/** A number question: a short question (limited tries) whose Idea key words (every word that isn't after NOT) are all numbers.
 *  Its answers are read as numbers (25, 25 J, 25J and 25.0 all read 25), its misconceptions' numbers too. Worked out once per item. */
function isNumberQ_(item) {
  if (item.numberQ_ != null) return item.numberQ_;
  var terms = [];
  if (item.kind === "short" && !isGame_()) item.criteria.forEach(function (cr) { cr.need.forEach(function (g) { if (g[0] !== "!") terms = terms.concat(g); }); });
  return (item.numberQ_ = terms.length > 0 && terms.every(function (t) { return /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(t); }));
}

/** Data table + graph (Answer type "table"). The Table config cell is JSON:
 *    {"cols":["Mass (g)","Force (N)"],"rows":[[0,0],[200,null],[400,null]]}
 *  A number is a fixed cell; null is a box the student types in. The graph plots column 1 (x) against column 2 (y).
 *  "origin":true = the data go through 0, 0 (directly proportional), so the best-fit line starts at 0, 0.
 *  "noGraph":true = just the table (no graph, title or axis label boxes), graded on its data only.
 *  "calc":{...} = check the student's own math box by box (see CHECKED MATH IN A TABLE).
 *  Under the graph the student types the graph title and both axis labels: Idea 1 checks the title, Idea 2 the
 *  x-axis label, Idea 3 the y-axis label (leave an idea's key words blank and any label counts).
 *  A timed-trials lab table (a stopwatch, tests for each distance on each surface, and a graph with one colored line per
 *  surface) is set up with {"lab":{"surfaces":["Counter","Carpet"],"distances":[20,40,60],"tests":3,"unit":"cm","control":"Ramp only (control)"}}
 *  (see parseLabConfig_). The page that has a lab view draws it; the sheet only needs to know the boxes and that each holds a number.
 *  A graph of another question's numbers (no table of its own) is set up with {"graph":{"lines":[...]}} (see parseGraphConfig_). */
function parseTableConfig_(text) {
  var t;
  try { t = JSON.parse(String(text || "").replace(/[\u201c\u201d]/g, '"')); } catch (e) { return { error: "the Table config cell isn't valid JSON (it should look like {\"cols\":[\"Mass (g)\",\"Force (N)\"],\"rows\":[[0,0],[200,null]]})." }; }
  if (t && t.lab && typeof t.lab === "object") return parseLabConfig_(t.lab);
  if (t && t.graph && typeof t.graph === "object") return parseGraphConfig_(t.graph);
  if (!t || !Array.isArray(t.cols) || t.cols.length < 2 || !Array.isArray(t.rows) || !t.rows.length) return { error: "the Table config needs \"cols\" (2 or more column names) and \"rows\"." };
  var cols = t.cols.map(function (x) { return String(x == null ? "" : x).slice(0, 40); }), n = cols.length, inputs = 0;
  var rows = t.rows.slice(0, 60).map(function (row) {
    var out = [];
    for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      var v = Array.isArray(row) ? row[k] : null;
      if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") { out.push(null); inputs++; }
      else out.push(typeof v === "number" ? v : String(v).slice(0, 40));
    }
    return out;
  });
  if (!inputs) return { error: "the Table config has no boxes for students (use null for a cell they fill in)." };
  var table = { cols: cols, rows: rows, inputs: inputs };
  if (t.pairs === true && n >= 4 && n % 2 === 0) table.pairs = true;   // "pairs":true = graph columns 1+2, 3+4, ... as separate colored lines
  if (t.origin === true) table.origin = true;   // "origin":true = every line starts at 0, 0
  if (t.noGraph === true) table.noGraph = true;   // "noGraph":true = no graph, title or axis labels under this table (graded on its data only)
  if (t.calc && typeof t.calc === "object") {   // "calc": check the student's own math (see CHECKED MATH IN A TABLE)
    var cc = parseCalcConfig_(t.calc, n);
    if (cc.error) return { error: cc.error };
    table.calc = cc.calc;
  }
  return { table: table };
}
/** A plain data table's Table config as Check the Questions tab writes it back (keeps "pairs", "origin", "noGraph" and "calc"). */
function tableConfigOut_(table) {
  var out = { cols: table.cols, rows: table.rows };
  if (table.pairs) out.pairs = true;
  if (table.origin) out.origin = true;
  if (table.noGraph) out.noGraph = true;
  if (table.calc) out.calc = table.calc;
  return out;
}

// ============================== CHECKED MATH IN A TABLE ==============================
// A plain data table can check the student's own math, box by box: Table config "calc", e.g. for a density lab
//   "calc":{"blocks":0,"mass":1,"length":2,"width":3,"height":4,"volume":5,"density":6,"edge":[2.4,2.5]}
// (numbers = column positions counted from 0). Every column is optional:
//   mass                   any number above 0 (it comes off the scale)
//   length, width          between the two "edge" numbers (cm), give or take 0.05
//   height                 blocks x edge (a stack of blocks), give or take 0.1; "blocks" = the column holding 1, 2, 3, 4
//   volume                 the student's length x width x height
//   density                the student's mass / volume (their own volume, or length x width x height)
// A box is green within 2% (or 0.05), yellow within 8% (a rounding slip), else red. The table's data counts
// only when every box is green. The page colors each box from the result's "cells" (a page from before
// 2026.10.29d ignores them and shows the hints only).
var CALC_KEYS_ = ["blocks", "mass", "length", "width", "height", "volume", "density"];
function parseCalcConfig_(c, nCols) {
  var out = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < CALC_KEYS_.length; i++) {
    var k = CALC_KEYS_[i];
    if (c[k] == null) continue;
    var v = Math.round(Number(c[k]));
    if (!(v >= 0 && v < nCols)) return { error: "the table's calc \"" + k + "\" must be a column number from 0 to " + (nCols - 1) + " (0 = the first column)." };
    out[k] = v;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(c.edge) && c.edge.length) {
    var e = c.edge.map(Number).filter(function (x) { return isFinite(x) && x > 0; });
    if (e.length) out.edge = [Math.min.apply(null, e), Math.max.apply(null, e)];
  }
  if (out.volume == null && out.density == null && !out.edge) return { error: "the table's calc needs a volume or density column (or an edge size to check)." };
  return { calc: out };
}
/** "ok" within 2% (or 0.05), "close" within 8%, else "bad". */
function calcCompare_(given, truth) {
  var d = Math.abs(given - truth);
  if (d <= Math.abs(truth) * 0.02 || d <= 0.05) return "ok";
  if (d <= Math.abs(truth) * 0.08) return "close";
  return "bad";
}
function calcNum_(s) { s = String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/,/g, "").trim(); return s === "" ? null : (isNumberText_(s) ? Number(s) : NaN); }
function calcRound_(v) { return String(Math.round(v * 100) / 100); }
/** Every box of a calc table -> { cells: ["correct"|"partial"|"incorrect"|"blank"], tips: [hint text], allOk }. */
function calcCells_(item, vals) {
  var T = item.table, C = T.calc, cells = [], tips = [], at = 0;
  var STATUS = { ok: "correct", close: "partial", bad: "incorrect" };
  T.rows.forEach(function (row, r) {
    var box = {}, k;
    for (k = 0; k < row.length; k++) if (row[k] === null) box[k] = at++;
    function val(col) { if (col == null) return null; if (box.hasOwnProperty(col)) return calcNum_(vals[box[col]]); return typeof row[col] === "number" ? row[col] : null; }
    function set(col, st, tip) { if (col == null || !box.hasOwnProperty(col)) return; cells[box[col]] = st; if (tip && tips.length < 3) tips.push(name + ": " + tip); }
    var nBlocks = C.blocks != null ? Number(row[C.blocks]) : r + 1;
    if (!(nBlocks > 0)) nBlocks = r + 1;
    var name = row[0] != null && row[0] !== "" ? (typeof row[0] === "number" ? row[0] + (row[0] === 1 ? " block" : " blocks") : String(row[0])) : "Row " + (r + 1);
    Object.keys(box).forEach(function (col) { var v = calcNum_(vals[box[col]]); cells[box[col]] = v === null ? "blank" : (isNaN(v) ? "incorrect" : "correct"); });
    var m = val(C.mass), L = val(C.length), W = val(C.width), H = val(C.height), V = val(C.volume), D = val(C.density);
    Object.keys(box).forEach(function (col) { if (isNaN(calcNum_(vals[box[col]])) && tips.length < 3) tips.push(name + ": use numbers only, like 12.4, with no units."); });
    if (C.mass != null && m !== null && !isNaN(m) && !(m > 0)) set(C.mass, "incorrect", "the mass from the scale should be more than 0 g.");
    if (C.edge) {
      var lo = C.edge[0], hi = C.edge[1], range = lo === hi ? lo + " cm" : lo + " to " + hi + " cm";
      [["length", L], ["width", W]].forEach(function (p) {
        if (p[1] === null || isNaN(p[1])) return;
        if (p[1] < lo - 0.05 || p[1] > hi + 0.05) set(C[p[0]], "incorrect", "each block's " + p[0] + " should be about " + range + ". Measure again from the 0 mark of the ruler.");
      });
      if (H !== null && !isNaN(H) && (H < nBlocks * lo - 0.1 || H > nBlocks * hi + 0.1)) {
        set(C.height, "incorrect", "a stack of " + nBlocks + (nBlocks === 1 ? " block" : " blocks") + " should be about " + calcRound_(nBlocks * lo) + (lo === hi ? "" : " to " + calcRound_(nBlocks * hi)) + " cm tall. Measure the whole stack.");
      }
    }
    var dimsOk = L > 0 && W > 0 && H > 0, lwh = dimsOk ? L * W * H : null;
    if (C.volume != null && V !== null && !isNaN(V)) {
      if (!dimsOk) set(C.volume, "incorrect", "type the length, width and height first, then multiply them.");
      else {
        var cv = calcCompare_(V, lwh);
        if (cv === "ok") set(C.volume, "correct");
        else if (cv === "close") set(C.volume, "partial", "your volume is close. Check your rounding (tenths place, like 15.6).");
        else if (calcCompare_(V, L + W + H) === "ok") set(C.volume, "incorrect", "it looks like you added. Volume = length \u00d7 width \u00d7 height.");
        else set(C.volume, "incorrect", "volume = length \u00d7 width \u00d7 height. Multiply your three measurements again.");
      }
    }
    if (C.density != null && D !== null && !isNaN(D)) {
      var vol = V > 0 ? V : lwh;
      if (!(m > 0)) set(C.density, "incorrect", "type the mass first, then divide it by the volume.");
      else if (!(vol > 0)) set(C.density, "incorrect", "find the volume first, then divide the mass by it.");
      else {
        var cd = calcCompare_(D, m / vol);
        if (cd !== "ok" && lwh) { var c2 = calcCompare_(D, m / lwh); if (c2 === "ok" || (c2 === "close" && cd === "bad")) cd = c2; }
        if (cd === "ok") set(C.density, "correct");
        else if (cd === "close") set(C.density, "partial", "your density is close. Check your rounding (hundredths place, like 8.83).");
        else if (calcCompare_(D, vol / m) === "ok") set(C.density, "incorrect", "it looks like you divided volume by mass. Density = mass \u00f7 volume.");
        else if (calcCompare_(D, m * vol) === "ok") set(C.density, "incorrect", "it looks like you multiplied. Density = mass \u00f7 volume.");
        else set(C.density, "incorrect", "density = mass \u00f7 volume. Divide your mass by your volume again.");
      }
    }
  });
  var allOk = cells.length > 0 && cells.every(function (c) { return c === "correct"; });
  return { cells: cells, tips: tips, allOk: allOk };
}
/** A timed-trials lab table: one row of boxes for the control (ramp only, optional) and, for every surface and every
 *  distance, a row with a box for each test. The boxes are saved in that order (control first, then each surface's
 *  distances in turn), so the page and the sheet agree on which box is which. Every box must hold a number. */
function parseLabConfig_(L) {
  function text(v, n) { return String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().slice(0, n); }
  var surfaces = (Array.isArray(L.surfaces) ? L.surfaces : []).map(function (s) { return text(s, 30); }).filter(function (s) { return s; }).slice(0, 6);
  var distances = (Array.isArray(L.distances) ? L.distances : []).map(Number).filter(function (d) { return isFinite(d) && d > 0 && d < 100000; }).slice(0, 6);
  var tests = Math.round(Number(L.tests));
  if (!(tests >= 1 && tests <= 5)) tests = 3;
  var unit = text(L.unit == null ? "cm" : L.unit, 10) || "cm";
  var control = L.control === false ? "" : text(L.control == null ? "Ramp only (control)" : L.control, 40);
  if (!surfaces.length || !distances.length) return { error: "the lab table needs \"surfaces\" (a list of names) and \"distances\" (a list of numbers), like {\"lab\":{\"surfaces\":[\"Counter\",\"Carpet\"],\"distances\":[20,40,60],\"tests\":3,\"unit\":\"cm\"}}." };
  var cols = ["Surface", "Distance (" + unit + ")"], rows = [], inputs = 0, k;
  for (k = 1; k <= tests; k++) cols.push("Test " + k + " (s)");
  function blanks() { var b = []; for (var j = 0; j < tests; j++) b.push(null); return b; }
  if (control) { rows.push([control, ""].concat(blanks())); inputs += tests; }
  surfaces.forEach(function (s) { distances.forEach(function (d) { rows.push([s, d].concat(blanks())); inputs += tests; }); });
