  return { table: { cols: cols, rows: rows, inputs: inputs, lab: { surfaces: surfaces, distances: distances, tests: tests, unit: unit, control: control } } };
}
/** A graph question: it draws numbers the student typed in earlier questions, and the student types the axis labels.
 *    {"graph":{"lines":[{"name":"Experiment 1: speed","from":1,"x":[2.78,5.55,8.33]},
 *                       {"name":"Experiment 2: mass","from":2,"x":[900,1000,1100],"top":true}]}}
 *  Each line takes its y numbers from the boxes of question "from" (picture boxes or a data table's boxes, in order) and its
 *  x numbers from "x" (one for each box). A line with "top": true uses its own number line along the top of the graph (for two
 *  experiments with different x units on one graph); every line shares the y-axis. Every line starts at 0, 0 unless the graph
 *  says "origin": false. Idea 1 checks the x-axis label (the bottom one), Idea 2 the y-axis label, Idea 3 the top axis label.
 *  A line can instead graph two columns of a data table (question "from"): "col" up the side, "xcol" along the bottom (counted
 *  from 0), one point per row, up to 8 lines; "color":"#rrggbb" gives a line its own color. "title": true adds a title box above
 *  the graph (checked by the idea after the axis labels); "labels": false = no label boxes at all, and the graph isn't graded.
 *  There's no table, so nothing else is graded here. */
function parseGraphConfig_(G) {
  function text(v, n) { return String(v == null ? "" : v).trim().slice(0, n); }
  var lines = (Array.isArray(G.lines) ? G.lines : []).slice(0, 8).map(function (L) {
    L = L && typeof L === "object" ? L : {};
    var o = { name: text(L.name, 40), from: Math.round(Number(L.from)), x: (Array.isArray(L.x) ? L.x : []).slice(0, 40).map(Number), top: L.top === true };
    // From a data table: "col" = the column whose numbers go up the side, "xcol" = the column whose numbers go along the bottom
    // (both counted from 0), so one graph can show a column from several tables (2026.10.29c).
    if (L.col != null && L.xcol != null) { o.col = Math.round(Number(L.col)); o.xcol = Math.round(Number(L.xcol)); }
    if (/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(String(L.color || ""))) o.color = String(L.color).toLowerCase();   // this line's color (blank = the page's own)
    return o;
  });
  var eg = " like {\"graph\":{\"lines\":[{\"name\":\"Experiment 1\",\"from\":1,\"x\":[1,2,3]}]}}";
  if (!lines.length) return { error: "the graph needs \"lines\"," + eg + " (from = the question whose boxes hold the numbers, x = one number for each box)." };
  for (var k = 0; k < lines.length; k++) {
    if (!(lines[k].from >= 1)) return { error: "line " + (k + 1) + " of the graph needs \"from\": the number of the question whose boxes it graphs (like 1 for Q1)." };
    if (lines[k].xcol != null) {
      if (!(lines[k].col >= 0 && lines[k].xcol >= 0)) return { error: "line " + (k + 1) + " of the graph: \"col\" and \"xcol\" must be column numbers counted from 0 (0 = the first column)." };
      lines[k].x = [];
    } else if (lines.length > 4) return { error: "a graph of numbers typed in boxes can have 4 lines at most. For more, graph columns of data tables with \"col\" and \"xcol\"." };
    else if (!lines[k].x.length || lines[k].x.some(function (v) { return !isFinite(v); })) return { error: "line " + (k + 1) + " of the graph needs \"x\": a list of numbers, one for each box it graphs (or \"col\" and \"xcol\" to graph columns of a data table)." };
    if (!lines[k].name) lines[k].name = "Q" + lines[k].from;
  }
  if (lines.every(function (L) { return L.top; })) lines.forEach(function (L) { L.top = false; });   // a top axis only makes sense next to a bottom one
  var graph = { lines: lines };
  if (G.origin === false) graph.origin = false;   // lines start at their first point instead of at 0, 0
  if (G.labels === false) graph.labels = false;   // no axis label boxes: the graph only shows the student's data, and isn't graded
  else if (G.title === true) graph.title = true;   // a box above the graph for its title: the idea after the axis labels checks it
  return { table: { cols: [], rows: [], inputs: 0, graph: graph } };
}
/** A graph answer -> { x: bottom (x-axis) label, y: y-axis label, x2: top axis label }. */
function graphAnswer_(answer) {
  var a = null;
  try { a = JSON.parse(String(answer || "")); } catch (e) { a = null; }
  a = a && typeof a === "object" && !Array.isArray(a) ? a : {};
  return { x: String(a.x || "").trim().slice(0, 80), y: String(a.y || "").trim().slice(0, 80), x2: String(a.x2 || "").trim().slice(0, 80), t: String(a.t || "").trim().slice(0, 120) };
}
function graphHasTop_(item) { return item.table.graph.lines.some(function (L) { return L.top; }); }
/** Graph question: each axis label colors itself (Idea 1 the x-axis, Idea 2 the y-axis, Idea 3 the top axis). */
function graphFeedback_(item, answer) {
  var a = graphAnswer_(answer), top = graphHasTop_(item);
  var labels = [[a.x, top ? "Label the bottom axis (the numbers along the bottom)." : "Label the x-axis (along the bottom)."], [a.y, "Label the y-axis (up the side)."]];
  if (top) labels.push([a.x2, "Label the top axis (the numbers along the top)."]);
  if (item.table.graph.title) labels.push([a.t, "Give your graph a title."]);
  if (labels.every(function (L) { return !L[0]; })) return { status: "blank", message: "", parts: labels.map(function () { return "blank"; }) };
  var parts = [], unmet = [], score = 0;
  labels.forEach(function (L, k) {
    var cr = item.criteria[k], text = L[0];
    if (!text) { parts.push("blank"); unmet.push(cr ? cr.nudge : L[1]); return; }
    var padded = normalize_(text), ok = !cr || criterionMet_(cr, padded, padded.trim().split(" "), text);
    parts.push(ok ? "correct" : "incorrect");
    if (ok) score++; else unmet.push(cr.nudge);
  });
  var sc = "Score: " + score + "/" + labels.length + ". ", frac = score / labels.length;
  if (score === labels.length) return { status: "correct", message: sc + (item.table.graph.title ? "Nice work: your graph has a title and every axis is labeled!" : "Nice work: every axis on your graph is labeled!"), parts: parts, frac: frac };
  if (score === 0) return { status: "incorrect", message: sc + unmet.join(" "), parts: parts, frac: frac };
  return { status: "partial", message: sc + "Good start! Still missing: " + unmet.join(" "), parts: parts, frac: frac };
}
/** A table answer -> { v: [what the student typed in each box, row by row], t: title, x: x label, y: y label }. */
function tableAnswer_(item, answer) {
  var a = null, n = item.table.inputs;
  try { a = JSON.parse(String(answer || "")); } catch (e) { a = null; }
  if (Array.isArray(a)) {   // sheets before 2026.10.01f saved the whole second column as an array
    var old = a, v = [];
    item.table.rows.forEach(function (row, r) { row.forEach(function (cellV, k) { if (cellV === null) v.push(k === 1 && old[r] != null ? String(old[r]) : ""); }); });
    a = { v: v };
  }
  a = a && typeof a === "object" ? a : {};
  var vals = (Array.isArray(a.v) ? a.v : []).slice(0, n).map(function (x) { return String(x == null ? "" : x).trim().slice(0, 20); });
  while (vals.length < n) vals.push("");
  return { v: vals, t: String(a.t || "").trim().slice(0, 120), x: String(a.x || "").trim().slice(0, 80), y: String(a.y || "").trim().slice(0, 80) };
}
function isNumberText_(s) { return /^-?(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)$/.test(String(s).replace(/,/g, "")); }
/** Data table question: the data (every box a number), the title, the x-axis label and the y-axis label each color themselves. */
function tableFeedback_(item, answer) {
  if (item.table.graph && item.table.graph.labels === false) return { status: "blank", message: "" };   // nothing to grade: it only shows the data
  if (item.table.graph) return graphFeedback_(item, answer);   // (a graph's axis labels, and its title when it has one)
  var a = tableAnswer_(item, answer), filled = a.v.filter(isNumberText_).length, typed = a.v.filter(function (x) { return x; }).length;
  if (!typed && !a.t && !a.x && !a.y) return { status: "blank", message: "", parts: ["blank", "blank", "blank", "blank"] };
  var parts = [], unmet = [], score = 0, calc = item.table.calc ? calcCells_(item, a.v) : null;
  var dataOk = filled === a.v.length && (!calc || calc.allOk);
  // A calc table whose math isn't right yet is "partial" here, so a page from before 2026.10.29d doesn't paint every number green.
  parts.push(dataOk ? "correct" : !typed ? "blank" : calc ? "partial" : "incorrect");
  if (dataOk) score++;
  else {
    if (filled < a.v.length) unmet.push("Fill in a number for every box in the table (" + filled + " of " + a.v.length + " done" + (typed > filled ? "; use numbers only, like 2.5" : "") + ").");
    if (calc && calc.tips.length) unmet.push("Check these boxes: " + calc.tips.join(" ") );
  }
  var labels = item.table.noGraph ? [] : [["graph title", a.t, "Give your graph a title."], ["x-axis label", a.x, "Label the x-axis (along the bottom)."], ["y-axis label", a.y, "Label the y-axis (up the side)."]];
  labels.forEach(function (L, k) {
    var cr = item.criteria[k], text = L[1];
    if (!text) { parts.push("blank"); unmet.push(cr ? cr.nudge : L[2]); return; }
    var padded = normalize_(text), ok = !cr || criterionMet_(cr, padded, padded.trim().split(" "), text);
    parts.push(ok ? "correct" : "incorrect");
    if (ok) score++; else unmet.push(cr.nudge);
  });
  var nParts = 1 + labels.length, sc = "Score: " + score + "/" + nParts + ". ", frac = score / nParts;   // data, title, x-axis label, y-axis label: a quarter each (for a question worth points)
  if (calc && !dataOk) {   // a table that checks the math earns its data part box by box
    var good = calc.cells.filter(function (c) { return c === "correct"; }).length;
    frac = (score + good / calc.cells.length) / nParts;
    sc = good + " of " + calc.cells.length + " boxes check out. ";
    if (good && score === 0) score = 0.5;   // some boxes right: yellow, not red
  }
  var out;
  if (score === nParts) out = { status: "correct", message: sc + (labels.length ? "Nice work: your table is complete and your graph is labeled!" : "Nice work: every box in your table checks out!"), parts: parts, frac: frac };
  else if (score === 0) out = { status: "incorrect", message: sc + unmet.join(" "), parts: parts, frac: frac };
  else out = { status: "partial", message: sc + "Good start! Still missing: " + unmet.join(" "), parts: parts, frac: frac };
  if (calc) out.cells = calc.cells;   // each box's own color (green, yellow, red)
  return out;
}
/** For the teacher's Live Results: "Data: 0, 1.2, ... | Title: ... | X: ... | Y: ..." */
function tableText_(item, answer) {
  if (!answer) return "";
  if (item.table.graph) { var g = graphAnswer_(answer); return "X: " + (g.x || "_") + " | Y: " + (g.y || "_") + (graphHasTop_(item) ? " | Top: " + (g.x2 || "_") : "") + (item.table.graph.title ? " | Title: " + (g.t || "_") : ""); }
  var a = tableAnswer_(item, answer);
  return "Data: " + a.v.map(function (x) { return x || "_"; }).join(", ") + " | Title: " + (a.t || "_") + " | X: " + (a.x || "_") + " | Y: " + (a.y || "_");
}

/** Sentences in an answer: pieces ending in . ! or ? (or the end) that have at least 3 words. */
function countSentences_(text) {
  return String(text || "").split(/[.!?]+(?:\s+|$)/).filter(function (p) { return p.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(function (w) { return /[a-z0-9]/i.test(w); }).length >= 3; }).length;
}

/** A button link from the Questions tab: an https address, or "timer:N" (N = 1 to 86400 seconds, shown as a countdown). "" = not usable. */
function buttonUrl_(text) {
  var s = String(text || "").trim();
  if (/^https?:\/\//.test(s)) return s;
  var m = /^timer\s*:\s*(\d+)$/i.exec(s);
  return m && +m[1] >= 1 && +m[1] <= 86400 ? "timer:" + (+m[1]) : "";
}
function isTimerUrl_(u) { return /^timer:\d+$/.test(String(u || "")); }

/** "vector, arrow + same, equal" -> [["vector","arrow"],["same","equal"]]
 *  Special groups (each one is a piece between + signs):
 *    NOT a, b         none of these words may appear                    -> ["!","a","b"]   (capital NOT only)
 *    @2of a, b, c, d  at least 2 of these words must appear             -> ["@2","a","b","c","d"]
 *    @3sentences      at least 3 complete sentences                     -> ["@s","3"]
 *    @clear           3 or more sentences, each starting with a capital letter and at least 4 words long,
 *                     the answer ending with . ! or ?                   -> ["@c"]
 *  Special words inside a group:  #num = any number (a digit anywhere);  ^if = the answer must START with this word. */
function parseKeyWords_(text) {
  return String(text).split("+").map(function (g) {
    // "NOT stack, tower" = none of these words allowed. Capital NOT only, so a lowercase
    // phrase like "not move, still" stays an ordinary key word.
    var neg = /^\s*NOT\s+/.test(g), m;
    if (neg) { g = g.replace(/^\s*NOT\s+/, ""); if (/^\s*@/.test(g)) return []; }   // NOT can't go with @2of, @3sentences or @clear: dropped rather than turned into its opposite
    if ((m = /^\s*@(\d{1,2})\s*sentences?\s*$/i.exec(g))) return ["@s", String(+m[1])];
    if (/^\s*@clear\s*$/i.test(g)) return ["@c"];
    var atLeast = 0;
    if ((m = /^\s*@(\d{1,2})\s*of\s+/i.exec(g))) { atLeast = +m[1]; g = g.slice(m[0].length); }
    var terms = g.split(/[,;\n]/).map(function (t) {
      t = t.trim();
      if (/^#\s*num$/i.test(t)) return "#num";   // any number
      var start = /^\^\s*[a-z0-9]/i.test(t);      // ^if = must start the answer (a lone ^ or # is just ignored, as before)
      var w = normalize_(start ? t.replace(/^\^\s*/, "") : t).trim();
      return start && w ? "^" + w : w;
    }).filter(function (t) { return t; });
    if (!terms.length) return [];
    return neg ? ["!"].concat(terms) : atLeast ? ["@" + atLeast].concat(terms) : terms;
  }).filter(function (g) { return g.length; });
}
function keyWordsText_(need) {
  return need.map(function (g) {
    if (g[0] === "!") return "NOT " + g.slice(1).join(", ");
    if (g[0] === "@s") return "@" + g[1] + "sentences";
    if (g[0] === "@c") return "@clear";
    if (/^@\d+$/.test(g[0])) return "@" + g[0].slice(1) + "of " + g.slice(1).join(", ");
    return g.join(", ");
  }).join(" + ");
}

/** "Source Object: 43, 39.5, 21, 10" (one box per line or ;) -> [{label,x,y,w,h}] in % of the picture. */
function parseBoxes_(text) {
  var boxes = [], warnings = [];
  String(text).split(/[;\n]/).forEach(function (line, k) {
    line = line.trim();
    if (!line) return;
    var label = "", nums = line;
    var ci = line.lastIndexOf(":");
    if (ci !== -1) { label = line.slice(0, ci).trim(); nums = line.slice(ci + 1); }
    var n = nums.split(",").map(function (x) { return parseFloat(x); });
    if (n.length < 3 || n.slice(0, 3).some(isNaN)) { warnings.push("box \"" + line + "\" needs at least 3 numbers: left, top, width (height is optional)."); return; }
    boxes.push({ label: label || ("Box " + (boxes.length + 1)), x: n[0], y: n[1], w: n[2], h: isNaN(n[3]) ? 10 : n[3] });
  });
  return { boxes: boxes, warnings: warnings };
}
function boxesText_(boxes) {
  return (boxes || []).map(function (b) { return b.label + ": " + [b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h].join(", "); }).join("\n");
}
var BOX_SEP = " | ";   // picture-box answers are saved as one text: box1 | box2 | box3 ...
function splitBoxes_(answer, n) {
  var parts = String(answer || "").split("|").map(function (p) { return p.trim(); });
  while (parts.length < n) parts.push("");
  return parts.slice(0, n);
}

/** Fills brand-new Settings / Questions tabs from the starter values in this file. */
function seedConfigTabs_() {
  var ss = openSS_();
  var st = ss.getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS);
  if (!st || st.getLastRow() < 2) {
    st = st || ss.insertSheet(SH_SETTINGS);
    var rows = [["Setting", "Value", "What it does"]].concat(SETTING_DEFS.map(function (d) {
      var v = getGlobal_(d[1]);
      return [d[0], v, d[3]];
    }));
    st.getRange(1, 1, rows.length, 3).setValues(rows).setFontFamily("Poppins").setVerticalAlignment("top");
    st.getRange(1, 1, 1, 3).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8");
    st.getRange(2, 1, rows.length - 1, 1).setFontWeight("bold");
    SETTING_DEFS.forEach(function (d, i) {
      var cell = st.getRange(i + 2, 2);
      if (d[2] === "bool") cell.insertCheckboxes();
      if (d[2] === "percent") cell.setNumberFormat("0%");
      if (d[2] === "label") cell.setNumberFormat("@");   // plain text, so "1." or "1)" is kept exactly as typed
    });
    st.setColumnWidth(1, 260); st.setColumnWidth(2, 380); st.setColumnWidth(3, 520);
    st.getRange(1, 1, rows.length, 3).setWrap(true);
    st.setFrozenRows(1);
  } else {
    addMissingSettings_(st);
  }
  var qs = ss.getSheetByName(SH_QUESTIONS);
  if (isGame_()) { seedGameQuestions_(ss, qs); return; }
  if (!qs || qs.getLastRow() < 2) {
    qs = qs || ss.insertSheet(SH_QUESTIONS, 0);
    var ideaCount = IDEA_COLS, misCount = MIS_COLS, choiceCount = TRY_CHOICE_COLS;
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
      ideaCount = Math.max(ideaCount, it.criteria.length, Math.min(20, (it.dhints || []).length)); misCount = Math.max(misCount, (it.mis || []).length);
      if (it.kind && it.choices) choiceCount = Math.max(choiceCount, Math.min(6, it.choices.length));
    });
    var head = [Q_COLS.section, Q_COLS.secLabel, Q_COLS.secUrl, Q_COLS.secImg, Q_COLS.secCap, Q_COLS.q, Q_COLS.img, Q_COLS.cap, Q_COLS.btnLabel, Q_COLS.btnUrl, Q_COLS.boxes];
    for (var n = 1; n <= ideaCount; n++) head.push("Idea " + n + " hint", "Idea " + n + " key words");
    head.push(Q_COLS.type, Q_COLS.table, Q_COLS.sentences, Q_COLS.short, Q_COLS.points);
    var tryCols = triesPage_();   // LIMITED TRIES: choice, truefalse and dropdown questions' columns, then every tried question's
    if (tryCols) {
      for (n = 1; n <= choiceCount; n++) head.push("Choice " + n, "Choice " + n + " feedback");
      head.push(Q_COLS.right, Q_COLS.explain, Q_COLS.tries, Q_COLS.hintClue, Q_COLS.hintEx, Q_COLS.hintVideo);
    }
    for (n = 1; n <= misCount; n++) head.push("Misconception " + n + " hint", "Misconception " + n + " key words");
    var secAt = {}; SECTIONS.forEach(function (s) { secAt[s.before] = s; });
    var out = [head];
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it, i) {
      var s = secAt[i], sl = s && s.links && s.links[0], ql = it.links && it.links[0];
      var row = [s ? s.title : "", sl ? sl.label : "", sl ? sl.url : "", s && s.img ? s.img : "", s && s.imgCaption ? s.imgCaption : "", it.q, it.img || "", it.imgCaption || "",
                 ql ? ql.label : "", ql ? ql.url : "", boxesText_(it.boxes)];
      for (var n = 0; n < ideaCount; n++) {
        var cr = it.criteria[n];
        if (!cr && it.kind === "dropdown") { row.push((it.dhints || [])[n] || "", ""); continue; }   // a dropdown: Idea N hint = box N's hint
        row.push(cr ? (cr.row ? "[" + cr.row + "] " : "") + cr.nudge : "", cr ? keyWordsText_(cr.need) : "");
      }
      row.push(it.kind ? it.kind : it.draw ? "draw" : it.upload ? "upload" : it.table ? "table" : "",
               it.table ? JSON.stringify(it.table.graph ? { graph: it.table.graph } : it.table.lab ? { lab: it.table.lab } : tableConfigOut_(it.table)) :
               it.kind === "dropdown" && it.dtable ? JSON.stringify(it.dtable) : "",
               it.minSentences || "", it.short ? "x" : "", it.points == null ? "" : it.points);
      if (tryCols) {
        var tfc = it.kind === "truefalse" ? [{ t: "TRUE" }, { t: "FALSE" }].map(function (c, k) { var own = (it.choices || [])[k]; return own && own.fb ? { t: c.t, fb: own.fb } : c; }) : null;
        for (var cn = 0; cn < choiceCount; cn++) {
          var ch = it.kind && it.kind !== "short" ? (tfc || it.choices || [])[cn] : null;
          row.push(ch ? (ch.img || ch.t) : "", ch && ch.fb ? ch.fb : "");
        }
        var hn = it.kind && it.hints ? it.hints : {};
        row.push(it.kind && it.right ? (it.kind === "truefalse" ? (it.right[0] === 0 ? "TRUE" : "FALSE") : it.right.map(function (x) { return x + 1; }).join(", ")) : "",
                 it.kind ? it.explain || "" : "", it.kind && it.tries ? it.tries : "", hn.clue || "", hn.example || "", hn.video || "");
      }
      for (var m = 0; m < misCount; m++) {
        var mc = (it.mis || [])[m];
        row.push(mc ? mc.nudge : "", mc ? keyWordsText_(mc.need) : "");
      }
      out.push(row);
    });
    qs.getRange(1, 1, out.length, head.length).setValues(out).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment("top").setFontFamily("Poppins");
    qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8");
    qs.setFrozenRows(1); qs.setFrozenColumns(0);
    var widths = [200, 170, 200, 200, 160, 420, 200, 160, 150, 200, 260];
    widths.forEach(function (w, i) { qs.setColumnWidth(i + 1, w); });
    var idea1 = head.indexOf("Idea 1 hint") + 1;   // first idea column (1-based)
    for (var j = 0; j < ideaCount; j++) { qs.setColumnWidth(idea1 + j * 2, 280); qs.setColumnWidth(idea1 + 1 + j * 2, 240); }
    for (j = 0; j < ideaCount; j++) qs.getRange(1, idea1 + 1 + j * 2, out.length, 1).setBackground("#fff2cc");
    head.forEach(function (h, i) {   // misconception columns: key words on light red, so they don't look like the ideas
      if (/^Misconception \d+ hint$/.test(h)) qs.setColumnWidth(i + 1, 280);
      if (/^Misconception \d+ key words$/.test(h)) { qs.setColumnWidth(i + 1, 240); qs.getRange(1, i + 1, out.length, 1).setBackground("#f4cccc"); }
      var tw = tryColWidth_(h);   // LIMITED TRIES columns
      if (tw) qs.setColumnWidth(i + 1, tw);
      if (h === Q_COLS.right) qs.getRange(1, i + 1, out.length, 1).setBackground("#d9ead3").setNumberFormat("@");   // the right choice in green, kept as typed
    });
    qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setBackground("#c9daf8");
    var notes = head.map(function (h) {
      if (tryColNote_(h)) return tryColNote_(h);
      if (/^Misconception \d+ hint$/.test(h)) return MIS_HINT_NOTE_;
      if (/^Misconception \d+ key words$/.test(h)) return MIS_KEYS_NOTE_;
      if (h === Q_COLS.section) return "Type a title here on the FIRST question of a section to start a blue strip. Leave blank otherwise.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.secUrl) return "Link for the blue button shown under the section strip (e.g. the PhET simulation). Must start with https://\nOr type timer:N (N = seconds, like timer:120) for a countdown timer under the button instead.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.secImg) return SEC_IMG_NOTE_;
      if (h === Q_COLS.secCap) return "OPTIONAL: small italic text under the section picture.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.btnUrl) return "OPTIONAL link for a blue button under the question. Must start with https://\nOr type timer:N (N = seconds, like timer:120) for a countdown timer under the button instead.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.boxes) return "OPTIONAL: type-in boxes placed ON the picture, one per line:\n  Source Object: 43, 39.5, 21, 10\n= label: left, top, width, height (all % of the picture; height optional).\nBox 1 is graded by Idea 1, box 2 by Idea 2, etc. Each box colors itself. Leave blank for a normal answer box.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.img) return "Upload the picture to Google Drive, Share > Hart District: anyone with the link, then paste the link here.";
      if (/key words$/.test(h)) return "Words that show this idea. Commas mean OR:  zero, balanced\nA + means AND:  vector, arrow + same, equal  (needs one word from each side).\nWord starts count: accelerat matches accelerate/acceleration.\nStart a group with capital NOT to forbid words:  chip + NOT stack, tower\nSpecial words: #num = any number;  ^if = the answer must START with if.\nSpecial groups: @2of a, b, c = at least 2 of these words;  @3sentences = at least 3 complete sentences;  @clear = clearly written (3+ sentences, each with a capital letter and 4+ words, ending with a period).\nLeave every key-word cell blank for a question where any thoughtful answer counts.";
      if (/hint$/.test(h)) return "What students see when this idea is missing from their answer. On a dropdown question, Idea 1 hint is what they see when box 1 (the first ___) is wrong, Idea 2 hint for box 2, and so on (no key words needed).To group ideas under a rubric row, start the hint with the row name in brackets, like [Use of Data] Cite a number from your table. Students then see their score for each row (Use of Data 1/2).";
      if (h === Q_COLS.type) return TYPE_NOTE_;
      if (h === Q_COLS.table) return TABLE_NOTE_;
      if (h === Q_COLS.sentences) return "OPTIONAL: the fewest complete sentences an answer needs to turn green, e.g. 3. A sentence ends with a period (or ! or ?) and has at least 3 words.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.short) return "Type x to let a short answer turn green (skips the Minimum characters rule), e.g. a one-word answer.";
      if (h === Q_COLS.points) return POINTS_NOTE_;
      return "";
    });
    qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setNotes([notes]);
  } else {
    // Existing Questions tab: add the Answer type column at the end if it has none (nothing else changes).
    var lastC = qs.getLastColumn();
    var heads = qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastC).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
    var addCol = function (label, note) {
      lastC++;
      qs.getRange(1, lastC, 1, 1).setValues([[label]]).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setFontFamily("Poppins").setNotes([[note]]);
      qs.setColumnWidth(lastC, 170);
    };
    var has = function (re) { return heads.some(function (h) { return re.test(h); }); };
    if (!has(/^section picture(?! caption)/)) addCol(Q_COLS.secImg, SEC_IMG_NOTE_);
    if (!has(/^section picture caption/)) addCol(Q_COLS.secCap, "OPTIONAL: small italic text under the section picture.");
    if (!has(/^boxes on picture/)) addCol(Q_COLS.boxes, "OPTIONAL: type-in boxes placed ON the picture, one per line:\n  Source Object: 43, 39.5, 21, 10\n= label: left, top, width, height (all % of the picture; height optional).\nBox 1 is graded by Idea 1, box 2 by Idea 2, etc.");
    if (typeCol_(heads) === -1) addCol(Q_COLS.type, TYPE_NOTE_);
    if (!has(/^table config/)) addCol(Q_COLS.table, TABLE_NOTE_);
    if (!has(/^minimum sentences/)) addCol(Q_COLS.sentences, "OPTIONAL: the fewest complete sentences an answer needs to turn green, e.g. 3.");
    if (!has(/^allow short answer/)) addCol(Q_COLS.short, "Type x to let a short answer turn green (skips the Minimum characters rule).");
    if (!has(/^points\b/)) addCol(Q_COLS.points, POINTS_NOTE_);
    if (!has(/^misconception \d+ (hint|key words)$/)) {
      for (var mn = 1; mn <= MIS_COLS; mn++) {
        addCol("Misconception " + mn + " hint", MIS_HINT_NOTE_); qs.setColumnWidth(lastC, 280);
        addCol("Misconception " + mn + " key words", MIS_KEYS_NOTE_); qs.setColumnWidth(lastC, 240);
        if (qs.getLastRow() > 1) qs.getRange(2, lastC, qs.getLastRow() - 1, 1).setBackground("#f4cccc");
      }
    }
    // LIMITED TRIES (2026.11.06): choice, truefalse and short questions
    if (!triesPage_()) return;
    var addTry = function (label) { addCol(label, tryColNote_(label)); qs.setColumnWidth(lastC, tryColWidth_(label) || 170); };
    if (!has(/^(choice|option) 1\b(?!\s*feedback)/)) for (var cn = 1; cn <= TRY_CHOICE_COLS; cn++) { addTry("Choice " + cn); addTry("Choice " + cn + " feedback"); }
    if (!has(/^right (choice|answer)/)) { addTry(Q_COLS.right); qs.getRange(1, lastC, Math.max(1, qs.getLastRow()), 1).setNumberFormat("@"); }
    if (!has(/^explanation/)) addTry(Q_COLS.explain);
    if (!has(/^tries\b/)) addTry(Q_COLS.tries);
    if (!has(/^hint:?\s*clue/)) addTry(Q_COLS.hintClue);
    if (!has(/^hint:?\s*example/)) addTry(Q_COLS.hintEx);
    if (!has(/^hint:?\s*(video|link)/)) addTry(Q_COLS.hintVideo);
  }
}
/** The notes on the LIMITED TRIES columns of a Self-Check's Questions tab ("" = not one of them). */
function tryColNote_(h) {
  h = String(h || "");
  if (/^Choice \d+$/.test(h)) return "Only for Answer type choice or dropdown: one answer choice (its words, or a picture link alone). Students see the choices as buttons, shuffled for each student (Settings: Shuffle answer choices). When every choice is a picture, they show 2 to a row. Leave extra Choice cells blank. A truefalse question's choices are always TRUE and FALSE, so its Choice cells can stay blank. A dropdown question: the options every dropdown offers, in this order (never shuffled, words only).";
  if (/^Choice \d+ feedback$/.test(h)) return "OPTIONAL, for choice and truefalse questions: what a student sees after picking this choice when it's WRONG. Coach them toward the right idea, e.g. That's mass x velocity (7 x 4). Did you square the velocity? On a truefalse question, Choice 1 feedback is for TRUE and Choice 2 feedback for FALSE. The right choice's feedback is never shown (the Explanation is). On a dropdown question it's shown for a wrong box that has no Idea hint.";
  if (h === Q_COLS.right) return "For choice and truefalse questions: the number of the right choice (2 = Choice 2), or the right choice's own words. A truefalse question: TRUE or FALSE. One right choice per question. A dropdown question: one answer for each ___, in order, separated by commas, like 2, 3, 2 (or the words: 1/2, equal to, 1/2). Students never see it until they answer right or run out of tries.";
  if (h === Q_COLS.explain) return "For choice, truefalse, short and dropdown questions: shown once the question is done (answered right, or out of tries), like a short reason why the right answer is right. Never shown before that.";
  if (h === Q_COLS.tries) return "OPTIONAL, for choice, truefalse, short and dropdown questions: how many tries this question gets (a whole number, 1 to 10). Blank = Settings (Tries for multiple choice, also used by dropdowns, Tries for true/false, Tries for short answers).";
  if (h === Q_COLS.hintClue) return "OPTIONAL, for choice, truefalse and short questions: a clue students can open from the light bulb (Give me a clue). Point toward the answer without giving it away. Hints never cost points. A question with no hints has no light bulb.";
  if (h === Q_COLS.hintEx) return "OPTIONAL, for choice, truefalse and short questions: a worked example of a similar problem, opened from the light bulb (Show me an example).";
  if (h === Q_COLS.hintVideo) return "OPTIONAL, for choice, truefalse and short questions: an https:// link (a YouTube video is fine) that opens in a new tab from the light bulb (Watch a video).";
  return "";
}
function tryColWidth_(h) {
  h = String(h || "");
  if (/^Choice \d+$/.test(h)) return 190;
  if (/^Choice \d+ feedback$/.test(h)) return 280;
  return { "Right choice (a number, or 1, 3 for select all)": 130, "Explanation (shown after answering)": 300, "Tries (blank = Settings)": 90,
           "Hint: clue": 260, "Hint: example": 280, "Hint: video or link": 220 }[h] || 0;
}
/** A review game's Questions tab: only what a multiple choice question needs. A new tab gets the starter questions;
 *  an existing one only gets the Choice / Right choice / Explanation columns it is missing. */
function seedGameQuestions_(ss, qs) {
  var maxC = CHOICE_COLS;
  ITEMS.forEach(function (it) { if (it.choices) maxC = Math.max(maxC, Math.min(6, it.choices.length)); });
  var choiceHeads = [];
  for (var n = 1; n <= maxC; n++) choiceHeads.push("Choice " + n);
  var notes = {};
  notes[Q_COLS.section] = "OPTIONAL: type a title on the FIRST question of a part of the game. Students see it on that question.";
  notes[Q_COLS.q] = "The question students see. Every row with a question needs at least two choices and a Right choice.";
  notes[Q_COLS.img] = "OPTIONAL picture above the question: a Google Drive share link (Share > Hart District: anyone with the link) or an https picture address. Students can click it to zoom.";
  notes[Q_COLS.cap] = "OPTIONAL: small text under the picture.";
  notes[Q_COLS.right] = "The number of the right choice: 2 means Choice 2. For select all that apply, type every right one: 1, 3. (Letters work too: B, or A, C.)";
  notes[Q_COLS.explain] = "OPTIONAL: shown to the student after they answer (right or wrong), like a short reason why the right choice is right.";
  notes[Q_COLS.points] = "OPTIONAL: what this question is worth. Blank = 1. The grade is the share of points earned (Full credit at and Points possible in Settings).";
  choiceHeads.forEach(function (h) { notes[h] = "An answer choice: its words, or a picture link alone (Drive share link or https picture address) for a picture choice. Leave extra Choice cells blank. Choices are shuffled for each student unless Settings says not to."; });
  if (!qs || qs.getLastRow() < 2) {
    qs = qs || ss.insertSheet(SH_QUESTIONS, 0);
    var head = [Q_COLS.section, Q_COLS.q, Q_COLS.img, Q_COLS.cap].concat(choiceHeads, [Q_COLS.right, Q_COLS.explain, Q_COLS.points]);
    var secAt = {}; SECTIONS.forEach(function (s) { secAt[s.before] = s; });
    var out = [head];
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it, i) {
      if (!it.choices) return;
      var row = [secAt[i] ? secAt[i].title : "", it.q, it.img || "", it.imgCaption || ""];
      for (var k = 0; k < maxC; k++) { var ch = it.choices[k]; row.push(ch ? (ch.img || ch.t) : ""); }
      row.push(it.right.map(function (x) { return x + 1; }).join(", "), it.explain || "", it.points == null ? "" : it.points);
      out.push(row);
    });
    qs.getRange(1, 1, out.length, head.length).setValues(out).setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment("top").setFontFamily("Poppins");
    qs.getRange(2, head.length - 2, Math.max(1, out.length - 1), 1).setNumberFormat("@");   // Right choice: "1, 3" stays as typed
    qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setNotes([head.map(function (h) { return notes[h] || ""; })]);
    qs.setFrozenRows(1);
    var widths = [170, 380, 220, 140];
    choiceHeads.forEach(function () { widths.push(190); });
    widths.push(150, 300, 90);
    widths.forEach(function (w, i) { qs.setColumnWidth(i + 1, w); });
    qs.getRange(1, head.length - 2, out.length, 1).setBackground("#d9ead3");   // Right choice in green
    qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setBackground("#c9daf8");
    return;
  }
  var lastC = qs.getLastColumn();
  var heads = qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastC).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
  var has = function (re) { return heads.some(function (h) { return re.test(h); }); };
  var addCol = function (label) {
    lastC++;
    qs.getRange(1, lastC, 1, 1).setValues([[label]]).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setFontFamily("Poppins").setNotes([[notes[label] || ""]]);
    qs.setColumnWidth(lastC, 190);
  };
  if (!has(/^(choice|option) 1\b/)) choiceHeads.forEach(addCol);
  if (!has(/^right (choice|answer)/)) addCol(Q_COLS.right);
  if (!has(/^explanation/)) addCol(Q_COLS.explain);
  if (!has(/^points\b/)) addCol(Q_COLS.points);
}
var SEC_IMG_NOTE_ = "OPTIONAL: a picture shown right under this row's blue section strip (above its button), not tied to any question. Only used on a row that has a Section title. Upload the picture to Google Drive, Share > Hart District: anyone with the link, then paste the link here.";
var POINTS_NOTE_ = "OPTIONAL: what this question is worth. Blank = 1, the same as every other question. The grade out of Points possible (Settings) is split by points, so a question worth 10 counts ten times as much as one worth 1. A question with key words earns its points by how many of its ideas the answer shows (3 of 5 ideas = 60% of its points; a table or picture boxes work the same way); a question with no key words earns them by its color (green all, yellow the Credit for yellow share, red the Credit for red share). Tip: make the points add up to Points possible (Settings) and set Full credit at to 100%.";
var MIS_HINT_NOTE_ = "OPTIONAL: what students see when their answer shows this WRONG idea (the misconception's key words are in it), e.g. Seconds only measure time. Speed also needs a distance unit, like cm. Coach them toward the right idea instead of just saying it's wrong. They see it as  Check this: ...  and the box can't turn green until they fix that part.";
var MIS_KEYS_NOTE_ = "OPTIONAL: words that show a common WRONG idea (misconception) for this question. Typed the same way as the Idea key words: commas mean OR, + means AND, capital NOT forbids words. Example for the units of speed (a time unit with no distance unit):\n  second, sec + NOT cm, meter, mile, per\nWhen an answer has these words, the student sees this misconception's hint, and the box can't turn green (it earns at most the yellow share of its points). Only typed answers are checked (not tables, picture boxes or uploads). Leave blank when there's no common wrong idea. Try it with Self-Check > Test an answer against the key.";
var TABLE_NOTE_ = "For Answer type  dropdown: OPTIONAL, a small table shown above the sentences, only for reading (no boxes, not graded, no graph), e.g.\n{\"cols\":[\"Mammal\",\"Mass (kg)\",\"Speed (m/s)\"],\"rows\":[[\"lion\",125,16],[\"wildebeest\",250,16]]}\nFor Answer type  table: JSON with the column names and rows. A number is a fixed cell, null is a box students fill in, e.g.\n{\"cols\":[\"Mass (g)\",\"Force (N)\"],\"rows\":[[0,0],[200,null],[400,null]]}\nThe graph plots the first column (x) against the second (y). Add \"pairs\":true (4, 6 or 8 columns) to graph columns 1+2, 3+4, ... as separate colored lines with a key, e.g. Trial 1 and Trial 2. Add \"origin\":true when the data go through 0, 0 (directly proportional): the line then starts at 0, 0. Idea 1 checks the graph title, Idea 2 the x-axis label, Idea 3 the y-axis label.\nAdd \"noGraph\":true for just the table (no graph, title or axis labels; graded on its data only).\nAdd \"calc\" to check the student's own math box by box (column numbers counted from 0, each optional), e.g. {\"blocks\":0,\"mass\":1,\"length\":2,\"width\":3,\"height\":4,\"volume\":5,\"density\":6,\"edge\":[2.4,2.5]}: volume = their length x width x height, density = their mass / volume; each box turns green, yellow (a rounding slip) or red.\nA graph of numbers typed in an earlier question (no table): {\"graph\":{\"lines\":[{\"name\":\"Experiment 1\",\"from\":1,\"x\":[1,2,3]}]}} (from = that question's number, x = one number for each of its boxes; \"top\":true puts a second line's numbers along the top; lines start at 0, 0 unless \"origin\":false). Idea 1 checks the x-axis label, Idea 2 the y-axis label, Idea 3 the top axis label.\nA graph of data tables: lines like {\"name\":\"Brass\",\"from\":1,\"col\":1,\"xcol\":5,\"color\":\"#3366cc\"} (col = the column up the side, xcol = the column along the bottom, counted from 0; up to 8 lines). \"title\":true adds a title box (the idea after the axis labels checks it); \"labels\":false = no label boxes, and the graph isn't graded.";
var TYPE_NOTE_ = "Leave blank for a typed answer (students revise until it's right). Limited tries (Settings: Tries for ..., Credit by try): type  choice  for multiple choice (the Choice columns and Right choice),  truefalse  for TRUE / FALSE (Right choice TRUE or FALSE), or  short  for a one-line answer checked by its key words (all numbers = a number answer: 25, 25 J and 25.0 all count), or  dropdown  for sentences with dropdowns in them (like IXL): type ___ in the question where each dropdown goes (Ctrl+Enter starts a new line), the options in the Choice columns, and one Right choice per ___ (2, 3, 2); one Check sends them all. These show their Explanation once done, and their Hint columns under a light bulb. Type  table  for a data table with a live graph (set it up in Table config). Type  draw  to give this question a drawing pad (pen, marker, shapes, arrows, labels, colors) that students draw on right on the page. A drawing question's Picture (like blank graphs) becomes the paper they draw on. Type  upload  to make it a picture upload box instead: students drag a picture in, paste one (Ctrl+V), or choose a file. Drawings and uploads aren't graded, and their key words are ignored. Pictures are saved privately in a \"student pictures\" folder in your Google Drive, next to this spreadsheet.";

/** Existing Settings tab: add any settings that are newer than the tab (existing values are never changed).
    Returns how many rows were added. */
function addMissingSettings_(st) {
  if (!st || st.getLastRow() < 2) return 0;
  var have = {}, added = 0;
  st.getRange(2, 1, st.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) { have[String(r[0]).trim().toLowerCase()] = true; });
  SETTING_DEFS.forEach(function (d) {
    if (have[d[0].toLowerCase()]) return;
    var r = st.getLastRow() + 1;
    st.getRange(r, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[d[0], seedValue_(d), d[3]]]).setFontFamily("Poppins").setVerticalAlignment("top").setWrap(true);
    st.getRange(r, 1).setFontWeight("bold");
    if (d[2] === "bool") st.getRange(r, 2).insertCheckboxes();
    if (d[2] === "percent") st.getRange(r, 2).setNumberFormat("0%");
    if (d[2] === "label") st.getRange(r, 2).setNumberFormat("@");
    added++;
  });
  return added;
}

/** The value a newly added Settings row starts with. On a sheet from before 2026.10.02, automatic grades were ON
    exactly when the hourly timer existed, so the new checkbox starts checked in that case (nothing changes for him). */
function seedValue_(d) {
  if (d[1] === "AUTO_GRADES") { try { if (autoTriggers_().length) return true; } catch (e) {} }
  return getGlobal_(d[1]);
}

/** Writes one value in the Settings tab (by its label). Returns false if the row isn't there. */
function setSetting_(label, value) {
  var st = openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS);
  if (!st || st.getLastRow() < 2) return false;
  var labels = st.getRange(2, 1, st.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {
    if (String(labels[i][0]).trim().toLowerCase() === label.toLowerCase()) {
      st.getRange(i + 2, 2).setValue(value);
      clearConfigCache_();
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** Menu: list anything in the Questions / Settings tabs that won't work as expected. */
function checkQuestionsTab() {
  menuOnly_();
  clearConfigCache_();
  CONFIG_LOADED_ = false; CONFIG_WARNINGS_ = [];
  loadConfig_();
  var crit = ITEMS.reduce(function (a, it) { return a + it.criteria.length; }, 0), mis = ITEMS.reduce(function (a, it) { return a + (it.mis || []).length; }, 0);
  var qd = parseQuarterDates_(QUARTER_END_DATES), pt = parsePeriodTimes_(CLASS_START_TIMES);
  if (qd.bad.length) CONFIG_WARNINGS_.push("Settings: couldn't read these quarter end dates: " + qd.bad.join(", ") + " (use month/day/year, like 10/16/2026).");
  if (pt.bad.length) CONFIG_WARNINGS_.push("Settings: couldn't read these class start times: " + pt.bad.join(", ") + " (use period = time, like 1 = 8:10 AM).");
  var ch = parseCloseChanges_(CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES);
  if (ch.bad.length) CONFIG_WARNINGS_.push("Settings: couldn't read these different start times: " + ch.bad.join("; ") + " (use a day, a colon, then period = time, like Thursday: 1 = 8:10 AM, 6 = 10:33 AM).");
  var pointsSum = 0, anyPoints = false;
  ITEMS.forEach(function (it) { if (ungraded_(it)) return; pointsSum += it.points == null ? 1 : it.points; if (it.points != null) anyPoints = true; });
  if (anyPoints && Math.abs(pointsSum - MAX_POINTS) > 0.001) CONFIG_WARNINGS_.push("Points: the questions are worth " + (Math.round(pointsSum * 100) / 100) + " in all, but Points possible (Settings) is " + MAX_POINTS + ". The grade is scaled to Points possible, so they don't have to match, but they usually should.");
  var per = perTry_(), bank = gameQuestions_(),
      perText = !isGame_() || !per ? "" : per < bank ? " Each try gets " + per + " of the " + bank + " questions at random." : " Questions per try is " + per + ", so each try gets all " + bank + " questions.";
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert((isGame_() ? "Found " + ITEMS.length + " multiple choice questions (" + ITEMS.filter(function (it) { return it.right.length > 1; }).length + " of them select all that apply), " + SECTIONS.length + " sections." + perText :
    "Found " + ITEMS.length + " questions, " + SECTIONS.length + " sections, " + crit + " key ideas, " + mis + (mis === 1 ? " misconception" : " misconceptions") + "." +
    (ITEMS.some(isTried_) ? " " + ITEMS.filter(isTried_).length + " of the questions have limited tries (" + ["choice", "truefalse", "short", "dropdown"].map(function (k) { var n = ITEMS.filter(function (it) { return isTried_(it) && it.kind === k; }).length; return n ? n + " " + k : ""; }).filter(String).join(", ") + ")." : "")) + "\n\n" +
    (CONFIG_WARNINGS_.length ? "Check these:\n• " + CONFIG_WARNINGS_.join("\n• ") : "No problems found."));
}

/** Friendly reminder when the Questions or Settings tab is edited. */
function onEdit(e) {
  try {
    var n = e.range.getSheet().getName();
    if (n === SH_QUESTIONS || n === SH_SETTINGS) {
      clearConfigCache_();
      e.source.toast("Saved. Students get this the next time they load the page or check an answer. " +
                     "To re-color answers already submitted, run Self-Check > Recalculate all grades.", "Self-Check", 6);
    } else if (n === SH_STATE) {   // a hand edit in the hidden _data tab: those students' short-term copies go (see readRecordQuick_)
      var r0 = Math.max(2, e.range.getRow()), r1 = e.range.getLastRow();
      if (r1 >= r0) cacheDel_(e.range.getSheet().getRange(r0, 1, r1 - r0 + 1, 1).getValues().map(function (v) { return recKey_(String(v[0]).toLowerCase()); }));
    } else if (n === SH_CLASSES) {   // step 1's checkboxes: a tick is saved at once
      classesEdited_(e);
    }
  } catch (err) { /* never block editing */ }
}

// ============================== WEB APP (student side) ==============================
// The /exec page comes with the student's work already in it (BOOT_TAG_, filled by doGet), so it draws at once instead of loading,
// then asking the sheet in a second trip. A page without the tag, a student Google doesn't name, or any problem: the page asks as before.
var BOOT_TAG_ = '<script id="bootState" type="application/json"></script>';
function doGet(e) {
  if (e && e.parameter && e.parameter.probe) return probePage_(e.parameter.from);   // Class Pass's out-of-sight check
  try { loadConfig_(); } catch (err) { /* page shows the error after it loads */ }
  var out = HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile("Index");
  try {
    var html = out.getContent(), at = html.indexOf(BOOT_TAG_), st = at === -1 ? null : getStudentState("");
    if (st && !st.needSignIn) {
      var json = JSON.stringify(st).replace(/</g, "\\u003c").replace(/\u2028/g, "\\u2028").replace(/\u2029/g, "\\u2029");   // can't end the script early
      out = HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(html.slice(0, at) + '<script id="bootState" type="application/json">' + json + "</script>" + html.slice(at + BOOT_TAG_.length));
    }
  } catch (err) { /* the page asks for it itself */ }
  return out.setTitle(ASSIGNMENT_TITLE)
    .addMetaTag("viewport", "width=device-width, initial-scale=1");
}

/** Everything the page needs to draw itself + the student's saved work. No rubric. */
function getStudentState(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var who = whoIs_(token);
  if (!who.email) return signInState_();   // Google can't tell who this is: the page shows Class Pass
  var email = who.email;
  var rec = readRecordQuick_(email) || newRecord_();
  var waiting = allWaitingTries_(email);   // limited tries checked a moment ago and still on their way into the record (see FAST CHECK)
  Object.keys(waiting).forEach(function (k) {
    rec.tries = rec.tries || {};
    rec.tries[k] = withWaiting_(Number(k), rec.tries[k], waiting[k]);
    if (rec.tries[k].a.length) rec.answers[k] = rec.tries[k].a[rec.tries[k].a.length - 1];
  });
  var results = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) results.push(isTried_(ITEMS[i]) ? triedFeedback_(i, rec) : feedbackFor_(i, rec.answers[i]));   // limited tries: never an open question's answer
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster, true), pastDue = !!due && Date.now() > due;   // never waits on Classroom
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster, true), closed = isClosed_(close);       // end of the quarter (see QUARTER CLOSE)
  var base = INTRO + (pastDue ? " " + lateNote_() : "");
  var drafts = rec.drafts || [], shown = [], unchecked = [];
  for (var j = 0; j < ITEMS.length; j++) {
    var hasDraft = drafts[j] != null && drafts[j] !== rec.answers[j];
    shown.push(hasDraft ? drafts[j] : (rec.answers[j] || ""));
    unchecked.push(hasDraft);
  }
  return {
    version: SKILL_VERSION,   // the page shows it small at the bottom (spots a deployment that is not the newest code)
    title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE, intro: base + (close ? " " + (closed ? closedNote_(close) : closingSoonNote_(close)) : "").replace(/ $/, ""),
    links: LINKS, sections: sectionsForClient_(),
    questions: questionsForClient_(),
    answers: shown, checkedAnswers: rec.answers, unchecked: unchecked, results: results,
    name: roster[email] ? roster[email].name : email,
    email: email, via: who.via, domain: studentDomain_(roster), googleClient: googleClient_(),
    code: !!ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE, minutes: SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES, passLink: who.via === "code" ? passLink_() : "",
    turnedIn: rec.turnedIn || "",
    locked: !!(LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) || closed,
    // End of the quarter: closed = view only (the page shows the pop-up); otherwise the page closes itself at `at`.
    close: close ? { closed: closed, at: close.at, quarter: close.name, ended: dayLabel_(close.end), note: closedNote_(close), base: base } : null,
    now: Date.now(),
    showGrade: SHOW_GRADE_TO_STUDENTS,
    showPoints: !!SHOW_POINTS,   // the "2 points" tag next to each question (the page still uses Points for the running grade)
    grade: lateGrade_({ statuses: statusesOf_(results), answers: rec.answers, tries: rec.tries, onTime: rec.onTime, best: rec.best, game: rec.game }, due).grade, maxPoints: MAX_POINTS,
    shuffleChoices: !!SHUFFLE_CHOICES,   // LIMITED TRIES: the page shuffles choice questions' buttons per student (true/false never)
    shuffleQuestions: !isGame_() && !!SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS,   // 2026.11.06b: each student's own question order, within each section (the page's questionOrder)
    quickTries: true,   // 2026.11.06b: the page checks a try with quickTry, then saves it with tryAnswer (FAST CHECK); an older sheet has only tryAnswer
    teacher: email === teacherEmail_(),   // 2026.11.06c: the sheet's owner trying their own copy: the page offers Reset my answers (resetMyAnswers)
    minLength: MIN_ANSWER_LENGTH,
    blockPaste: !!BLOCK_PASTE,
    numberStyle: String(QUESTION_NUMBER_STYLE == null || QUESTION_NUMBER_STYLE === "" ? "1" : QUESTION_NUMBER_STYLE),
    boxHint: String(BOX_HINT == null ? "" : BOX_HINT).trim(),   // placeholder in picture boxes ("" = none)
    article: articleVersion_(),   // 0 = no article stored from a Google Doc; the reading page then shows its built-in one (if any)
    simUrl: String(SIM_URL == null ? "" : SIM_URL).trim().slice(0, 300),   // a lab page's simulation address (the page only uses an https one on an allowed site)
    simBtn: String(SIM_BUTTON == null ? "" : SIM_BUTTON).trim().slice(0, 60),   // the first simulation's button name ("" = the page's own; plain text on the page)
    sim2Url: String(SIM2_URL == null ? "" : SIM2_URL).trim().slice(0, 300),   // a second simulation: a third button on the left (the page only uses an https one on an allowed site)
    sim2Btn: String(SIM2_BUTTON == null ? "" : SIM2_BUTTON).trim().slice(0, 60),   // its button name ("" = Simulation 2)
    artBtn: String(ARTICLE_BUTTON == null ? "" : ARTICLE_BUTTON).trim().slice(0, 60),   // the left side's name on a side-by-side page ("" = the page's own; the page shows it as plain text, 24 characters at most)
    // grade formula only (no answers) so the page can update the running grade instantly.
    // After the due date it's left out, so the page shows the server's grade (with the late penalty) after each save.
    credit: pastDue ? null : CREDIT, fullCreditAt: FULL_CREDIT_AT, roundTo: ROUND_TO,
    game: isGame_() ? gameStateFor_(rec, email) : null   // a review game: points, streak, power-ups, best try, its settings (see REVIEW GAME)
  };
}

/** Lets the next save in line go. Everything this run wrote goes into the sheet FIRST (flush): Apps Script holds writes back
 *  until a run ends, so without it the next save could read a student's row from before this one and write the old answers back
 *  (2026.11.05c: an October Sky answer checked a second before a drawing saved came back blank). */
function unlock_(lock) {
  try { SpreadsheetApp.flush(); } catch (e) {}
  lock.releaseLock();
}
/** Waits for our turn to write. Throws a friendly "busy" error the page retries on its own. */
function lockOrBusy_(ms) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(ms)) throw new Error("BUSY: Lots of students are saving right now.");
  return lock;
}

/** Rebuilds the Question Summary tab at most once a minute (it reads every student's work). */
function maybeRenderSummary_() {
  var c = cache_();
  try { if (c && c.get("summaryFresh")) return; if (c) c.put("summaryFresh", "1", 60); } catch (e) {}
  try { renderSummary_(); } catch (e) { /* summary is only for the teacher; never fail a student's save */ }
}

/**
 * FAST FEEDBACK: grade only. No waiting in line, no spreadsheet writes.
 * The page calls this first so the color and hint appear right away,
 * then calls checkAnswer in the background to save.
 */
function gradeAnswer(idx, answer, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length)) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  if (ITEMS[idx].upload) throw new Error("This question takes a picture, not typing. Reload the page to get the upload box. If you still don't see one, add your picture from a school Chromebook.");
  if (isTried_(ITEMS[idx])) throw new Error("This question has a limited number of tries now. Reload the page.");   // only tryAnswer answers them
  if (ITEMS[idx].choices) throw new Error("This question is part of a review game. Reload the page.");   // one try each: only answerChoice grades them
  var email = currentStudent_(token);   // must be signed in
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, readRoster_(), true);   // never waits on Classroom
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  answer = String(answer == null ? "" : answer).slice(0, 5000).trim();
  return { result: feedbackFor_(idx, answer) };
}

/** Grade one answer, save it, update the teacher's live view. */
function checkAnswer(idx, answer, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length)) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  if (ITEMS[idx].upload) throw new Error("This question takes a picture, not typing. Reload the page to get the upload box. If you still don't see one, add your picture from a school Chromebook.");
  if (isTried_(ITEMS[idx])) throw new Error("This question has a limited number of tries now. Reload the page.");   // only tryAnswer saves them
  if (ITEMS[idx].choices) throw new Error("This question is part of a review game. Reload the page.");   // one try each: only answerChoice saves them
  answer = String(answer == null ? "" : answer).slice(0, 5000).trim();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  var result = feedbackFor_(idx, answer);   // grade before waiting in line
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);   // also before waiting in line (may ask Classroom, at most once an hour per class)
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));   // the quarter is over: view only
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000), out, changed, rec;
  try {
    rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) throw new Error("You already turned this in, so answers are locked.");
    keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due);   // first save after the due date: remember the work as it was at the due date
    changed = rec.answers[idx] !== answer;
    rec.answers[idx] = answer;
    rec.statuses[idx] = result.status;
    if (rec.drafts) rec.drafts[idx] = null;   // the checked answer replaces any autosaved draft
    if (changed) rec.checks = (rec.checks || 0) + 1;
    rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
    out = { result: result, grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  // Teacher-only extras happen after the student's save is done, outside the line.
  try { if (changed) logEvent_(email, idx, result.status, answer); } catch (e) {}
  maybeRenderSummary_();
  return out;
}

/**
 * Autosave while typing (called a few seconds after the student stops typing).
 * Saves the text so nothing is lost, but does NOT grade it or change its color:
 * that still happens on Check / clicking out of the box.
 */
function saveDraft(idx, text, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length)) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  if (ITEMS[idx].upload || ITEMS[idx].choices || isTried_(ITEMS[idx])) return { saved: false, locked: true };   // pictures go through uploadPicture, review game answers through answerChoice, limited tries through tryAnswer (an old page stops autosaving it)
  text = String(text == null ? "" : text).slice(0, 5000).trim();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  var roster = readRoster_();
  if (isClosed_(closeForStudent_(email, roster, true))) return { saved: false, locked: true };   // the quarter is over
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(4000)) return { saved: false };   // busy moment: the page quietly tries again
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) return { saved: false, locked: true };
    rec.drafts = rec.drafts || [];
    rec.drafts[idx] = text === (rec.answers[idx] || "") ? null : text;
    rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster);
    return { saved: true };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}

/** "I'm finished" button. */
function turnIn(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000);
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    rec.turnedIn = new Date().toISOString();
    rec.updated = rec.turnedIn;
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
    return { turnedIn: rec.turnedIn, locked: !!LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN, grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}

// ============================== LIMITED TRIES ==============================
// Answer type choice (multiple choice), truefalse or short (a one-line typed answer checked by its key words), in a Self-Check
// (never a review game). Donny, 2026-11-06: it replaces a Google Forms practice set where students clicked choices until one turned
// green. Each question gets a few tries (Settings: Tries for multiple choice 2, true/false 1, short answers 3; a question's Tries
// column changes it) and earns less with each one: Credit by try (100%, 90%, 80%) for the try its first right answer came on,
// Credit when never answered right (50%) for a question tried but never right, 0 for one never tried (tryState_). After a wrong
// try the student opens a hint (the light bulb: a clue, an example or a video, from the question's Hint columns) before trying
// again (Settings: Open a hint before trying again). Hints never cost points.
// DROPDOWNS (2026.11.06d, Donny rebuilding IXL's "Use tables and graphs" skill, whose 4-in-a-row score resets after one slip): Answer type
// dropdown = sentences with a dropdown at each ___ (dropdownFrom_), every dropdown offering the Choice columns, an optional table above
// (displayTable_). One Check sends every box as one try ("2,3,1", the option numbers); it's right only when every box is right
// (dropGrade_). After a wrong try the page gets which boxes were right (marks) and keeps those green; the right answers of the wrong
// boxes come only once the question is done. Tries: Settings "Tries for multiple choice".
// tryAnswer is the only way to answer these (inside the script lock, so every try is counted once); openHint records each hint.
// rec.tries[idx] = { a: [each try's answer, in order], h: [{ k: "clue" | "example" | "video", after: tries used when it was opened }],
// id: the last try's attemptId }; rec.answers[idx] = the latest try; rec.statuses[idx] = the question's color (green = right,
// yellow = tried with tries left, red = out of tries). Grades and colors always come from the tries with today's key, so fixing a
// Right choice and running Recalculate all grades re-grades them. The page never gets an open question's right answer or
// Explanation (triedFeedback_), and never a choice's feedback until that choice is picked.
function isTried_(it) { return !!(it && it.kind) && triesPage_(); }
/** The tries a question gets: its Tries column, else the Settings row for its kind (1 to 10). */
function maxTries_(it) {
  var n = Math.floor(Number(it.tries || (it.kind === "choice" || it.kind === "dropdown" ? TRIES_CHOICE : it.kind === "truefalse" ? TRIES_TF : TRIES_SHORT)));
  return n >= 1 ? Math.min(n, 10) : 1;
}
/** Settings "Credit by try" ("100%, 90%, 80%") -> [1, 0.9, 0.8]. */
function tryCredits_() {
  var out = [];
  String(TRY_CREDIT == null ? "" : TRY_CREDIT).split(/[,;\s]+/).forEach(function (p) {
    var n = parseFloat(p);
    if (isNaN(n)) return;
    if (/%$/.test(p) || n > 1) n = n / 100;
    out.push(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, n)));
  });
  return out.length ? out : [1];
}
/** The share of a question's points for a first right answer on try n (tries past the list use its last one). */
function tryCredit_(n) { var c = tryCredits_(); return c[Math.min(Math.max(1, n), c.length) - 1]; }
function wrongCredit_() { var p = Number(TRIED_WRONG_CREDIT); return isNaN(p) ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p > 1 ? p / 100 : p)); }

/** One try graded on its own: { status, message } (a choice question's answer is the choice's number, from 1). Never names the right choice. */
function tryGrade_(item, answer) {
  answer = String(answer == null ? "" : answer).trim();
  if (!answer) return { status: "blank", message: "" };
  if (item.kind === "short") return shortGrade_(item, answer);
  if (item.kind === "dropdown") return dropGrade_(item, answer);
  var n = choicePicks_(item, answer)[0];
  if (n == null) return { status: "blank", message: "" };
  if (n === item.right[0]) return { status: "correct", message: "That's right!" };
  return { status: "incorrect", message: item.choices[n].fb || "That isn't the right answer." };   // (Donny, 2026-11-06: a wrong answer goes straight to its feedback)
}
/** A short answer's try: green only with every idea and no misconception (no minimum length). */
function shortGrade_(item, answer) {
  var res = gradeLocal_(item, answer), mis = misconceptionsIn_(item, answer);
  if (!res.total) return mis.length ? { status: "partial", message: "Check this: " + mis.join(" ") } : { status: "correct", message: "That's right!" };
  if (res.status === "correct" && !mis.length) return { status: "correct", message: "That's right!" };
  if (mis.length) return { status: res.status === "incorrect" ? "incorrect" : "partial", message: "Check this: " + mis.join(" ") };
  return { status: res.status, message: (res.status === "partial" ? "Good start! Still missing: " : "") + res.unmet.join(" ") };
}
/** Every number in an answer: "25 J", "25J", "25.0" and " 25 " all give [25]; "1,025" gives [1025]. */
function numbersIn_(raw) {
  var s = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).replace(/(\d),(?=\d{3}(\D|$))/g, "$1"), out = [], m, re = /-?(\d+(\.\d*)?|\.\d+)/g;
  while ((m = re.exec(s))) { var v = Number(m[0].replace(/\.$/, "")); if (isFinite(v)) out.push(v); }
  return out;
}
/** A number for "Type a number, like 25." that isn't one of this question's key words (it would give the answer away). */
function exampleNumber_(item) {
  var keys = [];
  item.criteria.concat(item.mis || []).forEach(function (cr) { cr.need.forEach(function (g) { keys = keys.concat(g); }); });
  var pick = [25, 12, 40, 7, 3].filter(function (n) { return keys.indexOf(String(n)) === -1; })[0];
  return pick == null ? 9 : pick;
}
/** A question's tries so far, graded with today's key: used, max, left, done, rightTry (the try its first right answer came on, 0 = none),
 *  needHint (a hint must be opened before the next try), credit (the share of its points earned) and status (its color). */
function tryState_(idx, t) {
  var item = ITEMS[idx], a = t && t.a ? t.a : [], max = maxTries_(item), rightTry = 0;
  for (var k = 0; k < a.length && !rightTry; k++) if (tryGrade_(item, a[k]).status === "correct") rightTry = k + 1;
  var used = a.length, done = !!rightTry || used >= max;
  var hinted = (t && t.h ? t.h : []).some(function (h) { return h.after >= used; });
  var needHint = !!HINT_BEFORE_RETRY && !!(item.hints && Object.keys(item.hints).length) && !done && used > 0 && !hinted;
  return { used: used, max: max, left: done ? 0 : Math.max(0, max - used), done: done, rightTry: rightTry, needHint: needHint,
           credit: rightTry ? tryCredit_(rightTry) : used ? wrongCredit_() : 0,
           status: rightTry ? "correct" : !used ? "blank" : done ? "incorrect" : "partial" };
}
/** What the page gets for a question with tries (getStudentState, tryAnswer): its color and feedback, tries { used, max, left, needHint,
 *  done, rightTry }, the wrong choices picked so far (numbers from 1), credit, and only once it's done, the right choice and the Explanation. */
function triedFeedback_(idx, rec) {
  var item = ITEMS[idx], t = rec && rec.tries ? rec.tries[idx] : null, st = tryState_(idx, t), a = t && t.a ? t.a : [];
  var out = { status: st.status, message: "", credit: st.credit,
              tries: { used: st.used, max: st.max, left: st.left, needHint: st.needHint, done: st.done, rightTry: st.rightTry } };
  var drop = item.kind === "dropdown";
  if (item.choices && !drop) out.wrong = a.map(function (x) { return choicePicks_(item, x)[0]; })
                                 .filter(function (n, k, all) { return n != null && n !== item.right[0] && all.indexOf(n) === k; }).map(function (n) { return n + 1; });
  if (!st.used) return out;
  var last = a[st.used - 1];
  if (drop) {   // DROPDOWNS: the last try's picks (the student's own) and which boxes were right, like IXL; never a wrong box's right answer while it's open
    var lp = dropPicks_(item, last);
    out.picks = lp.map(function (n) { return n + 1; });
    out.marks = lp.map(function (n, k) { return n === item.right[k]; });
  }
  if (st.rightTry) {
    out.message = "That's right!" + (st.rightTry > 1 ? " Right on try " + st.rightTry + ", so it earns " + Math.round(st.credit * 100) + "%." : "");
  } else if (st.done) {
    var picked = item.choices && !drop ? item.choices[choicePicks_(item, last)[0]] : null;
    out.message = (picked && picked.fb ? picked.fb + " " : "") + "You're out of tries for this one." + (drop ? " The right answers are shown in green." : item.choices ? " The right answer is shown in green." : "");
  } else {
    out.message = tryGrade_(item, last).message;
  }
  if (st.done) {
    if (drop) out.right = item.right.map(function (n) { return n + 1; });
    else if (item.choices) out.right = item.right[0] + 1;
    if (item.explain) { out.explain = item.explain; out.message += " " + item.explain; }
  }
  return out;
}
/** A try's answer as the teacher reads it: "D. mass and velocity", "C. (picture: KE mass - curve)", "FALSE", "1/2 | equal to | 1/4", or the typed words. */
function answerText_(item, a) {
  if (!item.choices) return String(a == null ? "" : a);
  if (item.kind === "dropdown") return dropPicks_(item, a).map(function (n) { return n === -1 ? "(blank)" : item.choices[n].t; }).join(" | ");
  var n = choicePicks_(item, a)[0];
  if (n != null && item.choices[n].img && pictureName_(item.choices[n].img)) return String.fromCharCode(65 + n) + ". (picture: " + pictureName_(item.choices[n].img) + ")";
  return n == null ? "" : item.kind === "truefalse" ? item.choices[n].t : choiceText_(item, a);
}
/** Live Results cell: "D. mass and velocity | right on try 2 | hint: clue", "C. 28 J | out of tries (2) | hints: clue, video". */
function triedCellText_(idx, rec) {
  var item = ITEMS[idx], t = rec.tries ? rec.tries[idx] : null;
  if (!t || (!(t.a || []).length && !(t.h || []).length)) return "";
  var st = tryState_(idx, t), parts = [], kinds = [];
  if (st.used) parts.push(answerText_(item, t.a[st.used - 1]));
  parts.push(st.rightTry ? "right on try " + st.rightTry : st.done ? "out of tries (" + st.used + ")" : st.used ? st.used + " of " + st.max + " tries used" : "no tries yet");
  (t.h || []).forEach(function (h) { if (kinds.indexOf(h.k) === -1) kinds.push(h.k); });
  if (kinds.length) parts.push((kinds.length > 1 ? "hints: " : "hint: ") + kinds.join(", "));
  return parts.join(" | ");
}

// FAST CHECK (2026.11.06b, Donny: "a long delay after Check"). Like a typed answer's Check, a try is two calls:
//   1. quickTry: no waiting in line and no writing to the sheet. It checks the try against the student's tries so far and answers
//      at once. The try goes into the short-term cache right away ("tp:<email>:<question>", a list of { id, a: answer, k: hint }),
//      so it counts from that moment: a second quickTry (another tab, or a student calling it by hand) sees it, and can't use it twice.
//   2. tryAnswer, in the background: saves it in the student's record (in the line), with Live Results and the Answer Log.
// The record keeps every saved try's attemptId (t.ids), so a try is saved once however many times it's sent, and a later try's save
// also saves any earlier one still waiting (a dropped connection), in order. getStudentState shows the waiting tries too.
var TRY_WAIT_SECS = 21600;
var TRY_REPEAT_NOTE = "You already tried that one. (That didn't use a try.)";
function tryWaitKey_(email, idx) { return "tp:" + email + ":" + idx; }
/** The tries this student's page checked (quickTry) for a question, in order; some may be saved already (their ids are in t.ids). */
function waitingTries_(email, idx) { var v = cacheGet_(tryWaitKey_(email, idx)); return Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; }
function putWaitingTries_(email, idx, list) { cachePut_(tryWaitKey_(email, idx), list.slice(-12), TRY_WAIT_SECS); }
/** Every tried question's waiting tries in one cache read: { idx: [ ... ] }. */
function allWaitingTries_(email) {
  var c = cache_(), keys = [], out = {};
  ITEMS.forEach(function (it, i) { if (isTried_(it)) keys.push(tryWaitKey_(email, i)); });
  if (!c || !keys.length) return out;
  try { var got = c.getAll(keys); keys.forEach(function (k) { if (got[k]) { var v = JSON.parse(got[k]); if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length) out[k.split(":").pop()] = v; } }); } catch (e) {}
  return out;
}
/** A copy of a question's saved tries (t) with its waiting tries added after them, in order, until the question is done. */
function withWaiting_(idx, t, wait) {
  t = t || {};
  var out = { a: (t.a || []).slice(), h: (t.h || []).slice(), ids: (t.ids || (t.id ? [t.id] : [])).slice(), id: t.id || "" };
  (wait || []).forEach(function (w) {
    if (!w || !w.id || out.ids.indexOf(w.id) !== -1 || tryState_(idx, out).done) return;
    if (w.k && !out.h.some(function (h) { return h.after >= out.a.length; })) out.h.push({ k: w.k, after: out.a.length });
    out.a.push(w.a); out.ids.push(w.id); out.id = w.id;
  });
  return out;
}
/** A try as the page sends it: { item, idx, email, answer, id, seen, hk }, or { note } for a number question with no number. Throws for a bad request. */
function tryRequest_(idx, answer, attemptId, hintSeen, token) {
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !isTried_(ITEMS[idx])) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  var item = ITEMS[idx], email = currentStudent_(token);
  answer = String(answer == null ? "" : answer).trim();
  if (item.kind === "dropdown") {   // "2,3,1": one option number for each box, in order
    var dp = answer.split(/\s*,\s*/);
    if (dp.length !== item.blanks || !dp.every(function (x) { return /^\d+$/.test(x) && +x >= 1 && +x <= item.choices.length; })) return { note: DROP_BLANK_NOTE };
    answer = dp.map(Number).join(",");
  } else if (item.choices) {
    var cn = /^\d+$/.test(answer) ? Number(answer) : 0;
    if (!(cn >= 1 && cn <= item.choices.length)) throw new Error("NOTRY: Pick an answer first.");
    answer = String(cn);
  } else {
    answer = answer.replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 300);
    if (!answer) throw new Error("NOTRY: Type an answer first.");
    if (isNumberQ_(item) && !numbersIn_(answer).length) return { note: "Type a number, like " + exampleNumber_(item) + ". (That didn't use a try.)" };
  }
  return { item: item, idx: idx, email: email, answer: answer, id: String(attemptId == null ? "" : attemptId).slice(0, 64),
           seen: !!hintSeen, hk: /^(clue|example|video)$/.test(String(hintSeen)) ? String(hintSeen) : "" };
}
/** May this answer be tried now, after tries t? Throws the refusals (NOTRY: already right / out of tries, HINT: a hint first);
 *  returns { st, repeat } (repeat = this choice or answer was already tried, so it uses no try). */
function tryAllowed_(item, idx, t, answer, seen) {
  var st = tryState_(idx, t);
  if (st.rightTry) throw new Error("NOTRY: You already got this one right.");
  if (st.done) throw new Error("NOTRY: You're out of tries for this one.");
  if (st.needHint && !seen) throw new Error("HINT: Open a hint (the light bulb) before you try again.");
  var repeat = item.choices ? t.a.indexOf(answer) !== -1 : t.a.some(function (x) {
    return normalize_(x) === normalize_(answer) || (isNumberQ_(item) && JSON.stringify(numbersIn_(x)) === JSON.stringify(numbersIn_(answer)));
  });
  return { st: st, repeat: repeat };
}

/** FAST CHECK, step 1: one try, checked at once (no waiting in line, nothing written to the sheet; see FAST CHECK). The same
 *  arguments and refusals as tryAnswer. The page then saves it with tryAnswer (same attemptId) in the background. */
function quickTry(idx, answer, attemptId, hintSeen, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var p = tryRequest_(idx, answer, attemptId, hintSeen, token);
  if (p.note) return { counted: false, note: p.note };
  if (!p.id) throw new Error("NOTRY: Reload the page.");
  var close = closeForStudent_(p.email, readRoster_(), true);   // never waits on Classroom
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var rec = readRecordQuick_(p.email) || newRecord_();
  if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) throw new Error("You already turned this in, so answers are locked.");
  rec.tries = rec.tries || {};
  var wait = waitingTries_(p.email, p.idx), t = withWaiting_(p.idx, rec.tries[p.idx], wait);
  rec.tries[p.idx] = t;
  if (t.ids.indexOf(p.id) !== -1) return { result: triedFeedback_(p.idx, rec), counted: true, again: true };   // sent twice: the same result
  var ok = tryAllowed_(p.item, p.idx, t, p.answer, p.seen);
  if (ok.repeat) return { result: triedFeedback_(p.idx, rec), counted: false, note: repeatNote_(p.item) };
  wait.push({ id: p.id, a: p.answer, k: p.hk });
  putWaitingTries_(p.email, p.idx, wait);   // it counts from now on
  rec.tries[p.idx] = withWaiting_(p.idx, rec.tries[p.idx], [wait[wait.length - 1]]);
  return { result: triedFeedback_(p.idx, rec), counted: true };
}

/** One try, saved in the student's record (FAST CHECK step 2; also works on its own, as on 2026.11.06 sheets). answer = a choice's
 *  number from 1 (the page shuffles the buttons but sends the Questions tab's numbers), or the typed words. attemptId = this Check press
 *  (sent again, it gets the same result back with no second try). hintSeen = the hint opened since the last try ("clue", "example",
 *  "video"), so a hint saved by openHint a moment ago still counts. Refused with no try used: closed, locked, already right, out of tries,
 *  or a hint needed first (HINT:). No try used either (but a result): a choice or answer already tried, or a number question with no number.
 *  A try that quickTry checked is saved with the hint it had then, after any earlier waiting try of the same question. */
function tryAnswer(idx, answer, attemptId, hintSeen, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var p = tryRequest_(idx, answer, attemptId, hintSeen, token), item = p.item, email = p.email;
  if (p.note) return { counted: false, note: p.note };
  idx = p.idx;
  var roster = readRoster_(), due = dueForStudent_(email, roster), close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);   // before waiting in line
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000), out, made = [];
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) throw new Error("You already turned this in, so answers are locked.");
    rec.tries = rec.tries || {};
    var t = rec.tries[idx] || { a: [], h: [] };
    t.a = t.a || []; t.h = t.h || []; t.ids = t.ids || (t.id ? [t.id] : []);
    if (p.id && t.ids.indexOf(p.id) !== -1) out = { result: triedFeedback_(idx, rec), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade, counted: true, again: true };   // saved already: the same result
    else {
      var wait = waitingTries_(email, idx), at = -1, todo, note = "";
      wait.forEach(function (w, k) { if (p.id && w.id === p.id) at = k; });
      todo = at === -1 ? [{ id: p.id, a: p.answer, k: p.hk, seen: p.seen }]   // not checked by quickTry first
                       : wait.slice(0, at + 1).filter(function (w) { return t.ids.indexOf(w.id) === -1; }).map(function (w) { return { id: w.id, a: w.a, k: w.k, seen: true }; });
      keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due);   // first save after the due date: remember the work as it was at the due date (kept only if a try is saved)
      try {
        todo.forEach(function (x, k) {
          var ok = tryAllowed_(item, idx, t, x.a, x.seen);
          if (ok.repeat) { if (k === todo.length - 1) note = repeatNote_(item); return; }
          if (x.k && !t.h.some(function (h) { return h.after >= ok.st.used; })) t.h.push({ k: x.k, after: ok.st.used });   // openHint hadn't landed yet
          t.a.push(x.a); if (x.id) t.ids.push(x.id); t.id = x.id;
          made.push(x.a);
        });
      } catch (err) {   // the sheet refuses it (out of tries in another tab ...): it stops counting as a waiting try too
        if (at !== -1) putWaitingTries_(email, idx, wait.filter(function (w) { return w.id !== p.id; }));
        throw err;
      }
      if (t.ids.length > 20) t.ids = t.ids.slice(-20);
      if (made.length) {
        rec.tries[idx] = t;
        rec.answers[idx] = t.a[t.a.length - 1];
        rec.statuses[idx] = tryState_(idx, t).status;
        if (rec.drafts) rec.drafts[idx] = null;
        rec.checks = (rec.checks || 0) + made.length;
        rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
        writeRecord_(email, rec);
        renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
      }
      out = { result: triedFeedback_(idx, rec), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade, counted: made.length > 0 };
      if (note) out.note = note;
    }
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  // Teacher-only extras happen after the student's save is done, outside the line.
  if (made.length) {
    var n0 = out.result.tries.used - made.length;
