    made.forEach(function (a, k) { try { logEvent_(email, idx, tryGrade_(item, a).status, answerText_(item, a) + " (try " + (n0 + k + 1) + ")"); } catch (e) {} });
    maybeRenderSummary_();
  }
  return out;
}

/** The sheet's owner (the account the web app runs as, "Execute as: Me"): the teacher. */
function teacherEmail_() { try { return String(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || "").toLowerCase(); } catch (e) { return ""; } }
/** TEACHER'S OWN COPY (2026.11.06c, Donny: "on the copy I open I want to be able to reset my answers"): erases the teacher's OWN work
 *  (answers, tries, hints, drafts, picture links, turned in) so the assignment can be tried again from the start, as a student would.
 *  Only the sheet's owner, signed in as themselves, can: a student can never reset (that would give them every try back). The Answer Log
 *  keeps its rows; Live Results shows the teacher Not started again, and the Question Summary no longer counts the teacher's tries. */
function resetMyAnswers(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  if (!email || email !== teacherEmail_()) throw new Error("Only the teacher can reset answers.");
  var roster = readRoster_(), lock = lockOrBusy_(20000);
  try {
    writeRecord_(email, newRecord_());
    renderLiveRow_(email, newRecord_(), roster, 0);
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  cacheDel_(ITEMS.map(function (it, i) { return tryWaitKey_(email, i); }));   // tries still waiting to be saved (FAST CHECK)
  try { renderSummary_(); } catch (e) {}
  return { reset: true };
}

/** The light bulb: records that the student opened a hint (kind = "clue", "example" or "video") on a question with tries. The page
 *  shows the hint at once and calls this in the background, retrying quietly; { saved: false } = busy, try again. */
function openHint(idx, kind, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx); kind = String(kind == null ? "" : kind);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !isTried_(ITEMS[idx]) || !ITEMS[idx].hints || !ITEMS[idx].hints[kind]) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  var email = currentStudent_(token), roster = readRoster_(), due = dueForStudent_(email, roster, true);
  if (isClosed_(closeForStudent_(email, roster, true))) return { saved: false, locked: true };   // the quarter is over: nothing changes
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(4000)) return { saved: false };   // busy moment: the page quietly tries again
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) return { saved: false, locked: true };
    rec.tries = rec.tries || {};
    var t = rec.tries[idx] || { a: [], h: [] }, used = withWaiting_(idx, t, waitingTries_(email, idx)).a.length;   // (counting tries still waiting to be saved)
    t.a = t.a || []; t.h = t.h || [];
    if (!t.h.some(function (h) { return h.k === kind && h.after === used; }) && t.h.length < 40) t.h.push({ k: kind, after: used });
    rec.tries[idx] = t;
    rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
    return { saved: true };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}

// ============================== REVIEW GAME ==============================
// A review game sheet (SKILL_NAME "review-game", page /pass/review/, like Wayground) has only multiple choice questions:
// Choice 1 ... Choice 6 columns and a Right choice (1, 3 = select all that apply). Each question gets ONE try: answerChoice saves
// the first answer and says right or wrong (then which choices were right), and the grade is the share right
// (Full credit at 70% = 10/10 with 70% or more). Game points are only for fun and the leaderboard: GAME_BASE for a right answer,
// GAME_STREAK_STEP more for each right answer in a row (up to GAME_STREAK_MAX steps), and a power-up every GAME_POWER_EVERY answers:
//   double = this question's points x2;  saver = a wrong answer here keeps the streak;  boost = the streak jumps up 3 first;
//   fifty = 50/50, two wrong choices taken away;  second = a wrong first pick gets one more try (2026.11.03b).
// fifty and second happen on the page, which has the scrambled keys (gameStateFor_): the page takes the choices away, or keeps
// a wrong first pick to itself and sends only the second one. Here they are only counted off, like the others.
// Start Over (Settings "Students can start over") keeps the best try in rec.best, so the grade is the best try (workGrade_),
// and rec.topPoints keeps the most points from any try (the leaderboards). Finishing a game marks it turned in.
// QUESTIONS PER TRY (Settings "Questions per try", 2026.11.10): each try gets QUESTIONS_PER_TRY of the bank at random, kept in
// rec.game.set (sorted question numbers, 0-based), first the ones the last try didn't have. Everything that counts questions counts
// only the try's set: done, the grade (40 of 40 right = full credit), Green/Red/Blank, the best try (rec.best.set), the on-time copy
// (rec.onTime.set), the website row and the power-ups. A try with no set means the first QUESTIONS_PER_TRY questions, or every
// question when the setting is 0 or the bank is no bigger (so a sheet's work from before 2026.11.10 keeps its grade). A try's set
// is stored with its first answer (newSet_ is the same for the same student and try, so the page and the sheet agree before that)
// and right away on Start Over (a fresh random one then).
// The website leaderboard (Settings "Website leaderboard address" and "key"): each student's top points, and right answers in the
// best try, go to <address>/api/review/results when they finish a game, and every hour for games in progress (syncWebsiteBoard_).
var GAME_BASE = 500, GAME_STREAK_STEP = 100, GAME_STREAK_MAX = 5, GAME_POWER_EVERY = 4;
var GAME_POWERS = ["double", "saver", "boost", "fifty", "second"];
var BOARD_TOP = 10, BOARD_CACHE_SECS = 30;

/** "1, 3" -> [0, 2]: the choices picked (in range, each once, sorted). */
function choicePicks_(item, answer) {
  var out = [];
  String(answer == null ? "" : answer).split(/[\s,]+/).forEach(function (p) {
    var n = /^\d+$/.test(p) ? Number(p) - 1 : -1;
    if (n >= 0 && n < item.choices.length && out.indexOf(n) === -1) out.push(n);
  });
  return out.sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });
}
/** Right only when exactly the right choices are picked (a select-all question has no part credit, like Wayground).
 *  The answer is already saved when this is shown, so it says which choices were right. */
function choiceFeedback_(item, answer) {
  var picks = choicePicks_(item, answer);
  if (!picks.length) return { status: "blank", message: "" };
  var ok = picks.length === item.right.length && picks.every(function (p, k) { return p === item.right[k]; });
  return { status: ok ? "correct" : "incorrect", message: ok ? "Correct!" : "Incorrect", picks: picks, right: item.right.slice(), explain: item.explain || "" };
}
/** A saved answer as the teacher reads it: "B. at rest" (a picture choice is "C. (picture)"). */
function choiceText_(item, answer) {
  return choicePicks_(item, answer).map(function (n) {
    var c = item.choices[n];
    return String.fromCharCode(65 + n) + ". " + (c.img ? "(picture)" : c.t);
  }).join(" + ");
}

function newGame_(attempt) { return { points: 0, streak: 0, bestStreak: 0, answered: 0, right: 0, powers: {}, attempt: attempt || 1 }; }
/** This try's game. email: a try not started yet (no answers, no set) gets its questions here (see QUESTIONS PER TRY). */
function gameOf_(rec, email) {
  if (!rec.game) rec.game = newGame_(1);
  var g = rec.game;
  if (email && !g.set && !g.answered) {
    var set = newSet_("set|" + String(email).toLowerCase() + "|" + g.attempt, g.attempt > 1 ? trySet_(null) : null);
    if (set) g.set = set;
  }
  return g;
}
/** The game's questions (0-based question numbers, in the Questions tab's order). */
function gameIdx_() { var out = []; ITEMS.forEach(function (it, i) { if (it.choices) out.push(i); }); return out; }
function gameQuestions_() { return gameIdx_().length; }
/** Settings "Questions per try" as a whole number (0 = every question). */
function perTry_() { var n = Math.floor(Number(QUESTIONS_PER_TRY) || 0); return n > 0 ? n : 0; }
/** The questions in a try: its own set, else (no set) the first QUESTIONS_PER_TRY questions, or all of them. */
function trySet_(set) {
  if (Array.isArray(set) && set.length) {
    var mine = set.filter(function (i) { return ITEMS[i] && ITEMS[i].choices; });
    if (mine.length) return mine;
  }
  var all = gameIdx_(), n = perTry_();
  return n && n < all.length ? all.slice(0, n) : all;
}
/** The questions a piece of work counts: a record (rec.game.set), a best try or an on-time copy (.set). null = not a review game. */
function workSet_(work) { return isGame_() ? trySet_(work.game ? work.game.set : work.set) : null; }
function setMap_(list) { var m = {}; (list || []).forEach(function (i) { m[i] = true; }); return m; }
/** A new try's questions: QUESTIONS_PER_TRY of the bank (sorted), mixed by seed, first the ones not in prev (the last try's).
 *  null = every question is in each try (Questions per try blank or 0, or no smaller than the bank). */
function newSet_(seed, prev) {
  var all = gameIdx_(), n = perTry_();
  if (!n || n >= all.length) return null;
  var was = setMap_(prev), mix = {};
  all.forEach(function (i) { mix[i] = parseInt(quickHash_(seed + "|" + i), 36); });
  all.sort(function (a, b) { return (was[a] ? 1 : 0) - (was[b] ? 1 : 0) || mix[a] - mix[b] || a - b; });
  return all.slice(0, n).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; });
}
/** Every question of this try answered. */
function gameDone_(rec) { return trySet_(rec.game && rec.game.set).every(function (i) { return !!rec.answers[i]; }); }
/** A try as the page shows it: right answers, questions, percent right. set = the try's stored set (see trySet_). */
function trySummary_(statuses, answers, set) {
  var list = trySet_(set), right = 0;
  list.forEach(function (i) { if ((statuses || [])[i] === "correct") right++; });
  var f = creditFrac_(statuses || [], answers, null, list);
  return { right: right, total: list.length, pct: Math.round((f || 0) * 100) };
}
/** What the game page needs about this student's game (getStudentState, answerChoice, startOver).
 *  keys: each question's right choices, scrambled (choiceKey_), so the page can say right or wrong the moment a choice is
 *  clicked (Donny, 2026-10-26: the trip to the sheet took 2 to 3 seconds) while answerChoice still saves and grades every answer.
 *  A student could only find the answers by working through the page's code; Donny chose instant over that. */
function gameStateFor_(rec, email) {
  var g = gameOf_(rec, email), salt = g.attempt + "|" + String(email || "").toLowerCase(), set = trySet_(g.set), mine = setMap_(set);
  return { points: g.points, streak: g.streak, bestStreak: g.bestStreak, answered: g.answered, powers: g.powers, attempt: g.attempt,
           topPoints: Math.max(rec.topPoints || 0, g.points), best: rec.best ? trySummary_(rec.best.statuses, rec.best.answers, rec.best.set) : null,
           now: trySummary_(rec.statuses, rec.answers, g.set), done: gameDone_(rec),
           set: set,   // this try's questions (QUESTIONS PER TRY): the page shows only these
           perTry: perTry_(),
           startOver: !!START_OVER, shuffleQ: !!SHUFFLE_QUESTIONS, shuffleC: !!SHUFFLE_CHOICES,
           website: websiteBoardOn_(), powerEvery: GAME_POWER_EVERY,
           rules: { base: GAME_BASE, step: GAME_STREAK_STEP, max: GAME_STREAK_MAX },
           salt: salt, keys: ITEMS.map(function (it, i) { return it.choices && mine[i] ? choiceKey_(salt, i, it.right) : ""; }) };   // (only this try's questions)
}
/** cyrb53, a quick 53-bit hash (the game page has the very same function, quickHash). */
function quickHash_(str) {
  var h1 = 0xdeadbeef, h2 = 0x41c6ce57;
  for (var i = 0, ch; i < str.length; i++) { ch = str.charCodeAt(i); h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ ch, 2654435761); h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ ch, 1597334677); }
  h1 = Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  h2 = Math.imul(h2 ^ (h2 >>> 16), 2246822507) ^ Math.imul(h1 ^ (h1 >>> 13), 3266489909);
  return (4294967296 * (2097151 & h2) + (h1 >>> 0)).toString(36);
}
/** A question's scrambled key: the hash of "salt|question|choices" (0-based choices, sorted, comma separated). */
function choiceKey_(salt, i, picks) { return quickHash_(salt + "|" + i + "|" + picks.join(",")); }

/** The review game's only way to answer: picks = [choice numbers from 1] (or "1, 3"); power = a power-up to use on it, or "".
 *  The first answer to a question on this try stands (a second tab or a retried save gets that first answer back, again: true). */
function answerChoice(idx, picks, power, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !ITEMS[idx].choices) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  var item = ITEMS[idx];
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  var answer = choicePicks_(item, Array.isArray(picks) ? picks.join(",") : picks).map(function (n) { return n + 1; }).join(", ");
  if (!answer) throw new Error("Pick an answer first.");
  var result = choiceFeedback_(item, answer);   // grade before waiting in line
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000), out, fresh = false;
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_(), g = gameOf_(rec, email);
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn && !rec.answers[idx]) throw new Error("You already finished this game, so it's locked.");
    if (!setMap_(trySet_(g.set))[idx]) throw new Error("This question isn't part of your game. Reload the page.");   // (QUESTIONS PER TRY)
    if (rec.answers[idx]) {
      out = { result: choiceFeedback_(item, rec.answers[idx]), again: true, gained: 0, power: "", earned: "", game: gameStateFor_(rec, email), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade };
    } else {
      keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due);   // first save after the due date: remember the work as it was at the due date
      power = GAME_POWERS.indexOf(String(power || "")) !== -1 && (g.powers[power] || 0) > 0 ? String(power) : "";
      if (power) g.powers[power]--;
      if (power === "boost") g.streak += 3;
      var gained = 0;
      if (result.status === "correct") {
        g.streak++; g.right++;
        gained = GAME_BASE + GAME_STREAK_STEP * Math.min(g.streak - 1, GAME_STREAK_MAX);
        if (power === "double") gained *= 2;
      } else if (power !== "saver") g.streak = 0;
      g.points += gained;
      g.bestStreak = Math.max(g.bestStreak, g.streak);
      g.answered++;
      var earned = "";
      if (g.answered % GAME_POWER_EVERY === 0 && g.answered < trySet_(g.set).length) {   // (none after the try's last question)
        earned = GAME_POWERS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GAME_POWERS.length)];
        g.powers[earned] = (g.powers[earned] || 0) + 1;
      }
      rec.topPoints = Math.max(rec.topPoints || 0, g.points);
      rec.answers[idx] = answer;
      rec.statuses[idx] = result.status;
      rec.checks = (rec.checks || 0) + 1;
      rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
      if (gameDone_(rec) && !rec.turnedIn) rec.turnedIn = rec.updated;   // finishing the game turns it in
      writeRecord_(email, rec);
      renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
      fresh = true;
      out = { result: result, again: false, gained: gained, power: power, earned: earned, game: gameStateFor_(rec, email), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade };
    }
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  // Teacher-only extras happen after the student's save is done, outside the line.
  if (fresh) {
    try { logEvent_(email, idx, result.status, choiceText_(item, answer)); } catch (e) {}
    maybeRenderSummary_();
    if (out.game.done) { cacheDel_(["board"]); try { pushToWebsite_([email]); } catch (e) {} }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Start Over: clears this try's answers (the best try so far is kept in rec.best) and starts the game points again. */
function startOver(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  if (!isGame_() || !START_OVER) throw new Error("Starting over is turned off for this game.");
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000), out;
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_(), g = gameOf_(rec, email);
    if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && rec.turnedIn) throw new Error("You already finished this game, so it's locked.");
    keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due);
    keepBest_(rec);
    var fresh = newRecord_();
    rec.answers = fresh.answers; rec.statuses = fresh.statuses;
    rec.game = newGame_(g.attempt + 1);
    var set = newSet_(Math.random() + "|" + Date.now() + "|" + email, trySet_(g.set));   // a new mix of questions, first the ones the last try didn't have
    if (set) rec.game.set = set;
    rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
    out = { game: gameStateFor_(rec, email), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade, results: ITEMS.map(function () { return { status: "blank", message: "" }; }) };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  cacheDel_(["board"]);
  maybeRenderSummary_();
  return out;
}
/** Keeps this try as the best one when it earned more of the credit (a tie goes to the newer try). */
function keepBest_(rec) {
  var g = gameOf_(rec);
  if (!g.answered) return;
  var now = creditFrac_(rec.statuses, rec.answers, null, workSet_(rec)) || 0, was = rec.best ? creditFrac_(rec.best.statuses, rec.best.answers, null, workSet_(rec.best)) || 0 : -1;
  if (now >= was) {
    rec.best = { answers: rec.answers.slice(), statuses: rec.statuses.slice(), attempt: g.attempt, points: g.points, at: rec.updated };
    if (g.set) rec.best.set = g.set.slice();   // the questions that try had (QUESTIONS PER TRY), so it's graded over them
  }
}

/** The in-game leaderboard: the top points in the student's own period and in every period, plus where they stand. */
function getLeaderboard(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("board", email, 600);
  var all = cacheGet_("board");
  if (!all) { all = boardRows_(); cachePut_("board", all, BOARD_CACHE_SECS); }
  var p = readRoster_()[email], period = p && p.period != null ? String(p.period) : "";
  function view(rows) {
    var place = 0, list = rows.map(function (r, i) {
      if (i === 0 || r.v !== rows[i - 1].v) place = i + 1;   // equal points share a place: 1, 2, 2, 4
      return { place: place, name: r.n, period: r.p, points: r.v, me: r.e === email };
    });
    var top = list.slice(0, BOARD_TOP), mine = null;
    if (!top.some(function (x) { return x.me; })) list.forEach(function (x) { if (x.me) mine = x; });
    return { rows: top, mine: mine, players: list.length };
  }
  return { period: period, mine: view(all.filter(function (r) { return r.p === period; })), all: view(all) };
}
/** Everyone with game points: { e: email (stays on the server), n: "Maya R.", p: period, v: top points }, most points first. */
function boardRows_() {
  var recs = allRecords_(), roster = readRoster_(), names = shortNames_(roster), rows = [];
  Object.keys(recs).forEach(function (e) {
    var r = recs[e], v = Math.max(r.topPoints || 0, r.game ? r.game.points || 0 : 0);
    if (v > 0) rows.push({ e: e, n: names[e] || "Student", p: roster[e] && roster[e].period != null ? String(roster[e].period) : "", v: v });
  });
  return rows.sort(function (a, b) { return b.v - a.v || a.n.localeCompare(b.n); });
}
/** "Maya R." for each student (more of the last name when two would look the same), like the website's leaderboard. */
function shortNames_(roster) {
  var out = {}, groups = {};
  Object.keys(roster).forEach(function (e) {
    var parts = String(roster[e].name || "").trim().split(/\s+/), first = parts[0] || "Student", last = parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1] : "";
    (groups[first.toLowerCase()] = groups[first.toLowerCase()] || []).push({ e: e, first: first, last: last });
  });
  Object.keys(groups).forEach(function (k) {
    var list = groups[k];
    list.forEach(function (s) {
      if (!s.last) { out[s.e] = s.first; return; }
      var n = 1;
      while (n < s.last.length && list.some(function (o) { return o !== s && o.last.slice(0, n).toLowerCase() === s.last.slice(0, n).toLowerCase(); })) n++;
      out[s.e] = s.first + " " + s.last.slice(0, n) + ".";
    });
  });
  return out;
}

/** The website leaderboard is set up in Settings: an https address and the key from its Leaderboard page. */
function websiteBoardOn_() {
  return isGame_() && /^https:\/\/[\w.-]+(:\d+)?\/?$/.test(String(WEBSITE_BOARD_URL || "").trim()) && /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/i.test(String(WEBSITE_BOARD_KEY || "").trim());
}
/** One student's row for the website: top points, right answers and questions in their best try. */
function websiteRow_(email, rec) {
  var fNow = creditFrac_(rec.statuses, rec.answers, null, workSet_(rec)) || 0, fBest = rec.best ? creditFrac_(rec.best.statuses, rec.best.answers, null, workSet_(rec.best)) || 0 : -1;
  var t = fBest > fNow ? trySummary_(rec.best.statuses, rec.best.answers, rec.best.set) : trySummary_(rec.statuses, rec.answers, rec.game && rec.game.set);
  return { email: email, points: Math.max(rec.topPoints || 0, rec.game ? rec.game.points || 0 : 0), right: t.right, total: t.total, pct: t.pct,
           passed: Math.max(fNow, fBest) >= FULL_CREDIT_AT - 1e-9 };   // full credit (Settings "Full credit at")
}
/** Sends these students' rows (all students when emails is null) to the website. Returns { ok, saved } or { ok: false, error }.
 *  withTitle: send even when no student has played, so the website learns the game's title (it files a review game under the
 *  topic its title names, and only learns the title from a send). */
function pushToWebsite_(emails, withTitle) {
  if (!websiteBoardOn_()) return { ok: false, error: "off" };
  var recs = emails ? {} : allRecords_();
  if (emails) emails.forEach(function (e) { var r = readRecord_(e); if (r) recs[e] = r; });
  var rows = Object.keys(recs).filter(function (e) { return recs[e].game && recs[e].checks; }).map(function (e) { return websiteRow_(e, recs[e]); });
  if (!rows.length && !withTitle) return { ok: true, saved: 0, rows: [] };
  var url = String(WEBSITE_BOARD_URL).trim().replace(/\/$/, "") + "/api/review/results";
  var res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { method: "post", contentType: "application/json", muteHttpExceptions: true,
    payload: JSON.stringify({ key: String(WEBSITE_BOARD_KEY).trim(), game: { id: openSS_().getId(), title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE }, rows: rows.slice(0, 500) }) });
  var body = {};
  try { body = JSON.parse(res.getContentText()) || {}; } catch (e) { body = {}; }
  if (res.getResponseCode() !== 200) return { ok: false, error: body.error || ("The website answered " + res.getResponseCode() + ".") };
  return { ok: true, saved: body.saved || 0, rows: rows };
}
/** What the website was last told the game is called (BOARD_TITLE): a new title, or a new website, sends everything again. */
function boardTitleKey_() { return ASSIGNMENT_TITLE + "|" + String(WEBSITE_BOARD_URL || "").trim(); }
function sendAllToWebsite_() {
  var res = pushToWebsite_(null, true);
  if (!res.ok) return res;
  var sent = {};
  res.rows.forEach(function (w) { sent[w.email] = w.points + "|" + w.right + "|" + w.total; });
  props_().setProperty("BOARD_SENT", JSON.stringify(sent));
  props_().setProperty("BOARD_TITLE", boardTitleKey_());
  return res;
}
/** The hourly check: sends the students whose points or right answers changed since the last send. The first time (and after the
 *  title changes) it sends every student and the title, so the website files the game under its topic (2026.11.03b: the Mass,
 *  Acceleration, and Collisions game had sent its points before the website kept titles, so it only showed under All topics). */
function syncWebsiteBoard_() {
  loadConfig_();
  if (!websiteBoardOn_()) return;
  if (props_().getProperty("BOARD_TITLE") !== boardTitleKey_()) { sendAllToWebsite_(); return; }
  var sent = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("BOARD_SENT") || "{}"), recs = allRecords_(), todo = [];
  Object.keys(recs).forEach(function (e) {
    if (!recs[e].game || !recs[e].checks) return;
    var w = websiteRow_(e, recs[e]), k = w.points + "|" + w.right + "|" + w.total;
    if (sent[e] !== k) todo.push(e);
  });
  if (!todo.length) return;
  var res = pushToWebsite_(todo);
  if (!res.ok) return;
  res.rows.forEach(function (w) { sent[w.email] = w.points + "|" + w.right + "|" + w.total; });
  props_().setProperty("BOARD_SENT", JSON.stringify(sent));
}
/** Menu (review game only): send every student's points to the website leaderboard now and say what happened. */
function sendPointsToWebsite() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  if (!websiteBoardOn_()) {
    ui.alert("The website leaderboard isn't set up yet.\n\nIn the Settings tab, fill in Website leaderboard address (like https://vocabulary-room901.pages.dev) and Website leaderboard key (from that website's Leaderboard page: sign in as the teacher, then open Review game key at the bottom).");
    return;
  }
  var res = sendAllToWebsite_();
  if (!res.ok) { ui.alert("The website didn't take the points: " + res.error + "\n\nCheck the address and the key in the Settings tab."); return; }
  ui.alert((res.rows.length ? "Sent " + res.rows.length + " student" + (res.rows.length === 1 ? "'s" : "s'") + " game points. The website put " + res.saved + " on its Leaderboard (students who aren't on the website's class roster are left off)."
                            : "No one has played yet, so there were no points to send.") +
           "\n\nThe website also has this game's title now, \"" + ASSIGNMENT_TITLE + "\", so the game shows under the Leaderboard topic its title names.");
}

// ============================== PICTURE UPLOADS ==============================
// A question with Answer type "upload" takes a picture instead of typing. The page shrinks the picture
// (longest side 1600 px, JPEG) before sending it. It's saved privately in this sheet's "student pictures"
// folder (script property UPLOAD_FOLDER); the student's answer is the file's Drive link. Not graded.
var UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES = 6 * 1024 * 1024;

/** The Drive folder for student pictures: made next to this spreadsheet the first time it's needed. */
function uploadFolder_() {
  var id = props_().getProperty("UPLOAD_FOLDER");
  if (id) { try { var f = DriveApp.getFolderById(id); if (!f.isTrashed()) return f; } catch (e) {} }
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(20000);
  try {
    id = props_().getProperty("UPLOAD_FOLDER");   // another student may have just made it
    if (id) { try { var f2 = DriveApp.getFolderById(id); if (!f2.isTrashed()) return f2; } catch (e) {} }
    var name = ASSIGNMENT_TITLE + " - student pictures", parent = null;
    try { var ps = DriveApp.getFileById(openSS_().getId()).getParents(); if (ps.hasNext()) parent = ps.next(); } catch (e) { parent = null; }
    var folder = parent ? parent.createFolder(name) : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
    props_().setProperty("UPLOAD_FOLDER", folder.getId());
    return folder;
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}
/** The Drive file ID in a link, or "". */
function driveId_(u) {
  var m = /(?:\/d\/|[?&]id=)([\w-]{20,})/.exec(String(u || ""));
  return m ? m[1] : "";
}
/** Not graded: a picture upload or drawing, or a graph with no label boxes (it only shows the student's data). */
function ungraded_(it) { return !!(it.upload || (it.table && it.table.graph && it.table.graph.labels === false)); }
function hasUploads_() { return ITEMS.some(function (it) { return it.upload; }); }

/** The page sends a picture for an upload question (base64, no "data:" prefix). Saves it and records the link. */
function uploadPicture(idx, data, mime, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !ITEMS[idx].upload) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("upload", email, 40);
  mime = String(mime || "").toLowerCase();
  var ext = { "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/png": "png", "image/webp": "webp", "image/gif": "gif" }[mime];
  if (!ext) throw new Error("UPLOAD: That file isn't a picture. Use a photo or a screenshot (JPG or PNG).");
  data = String(data == null ? "" : data).replace(/^data:[^,]*,/, "");
  if (data.length > UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES * 1.4) throw new Error("UPLOAD: That picture is too big. Take a screenshot of it and try that instead.");
  var bytes;
  try { bytes = Utilities.base64Decode(data); } catch (e) { throw new Error("UPLOAD: That picture didn't come through. Try again."); }
  if (!bytes.length || bytes.length > UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error("UPLOAD: That picture didn't come through. Try again.");
  var roster = readRoster_();
  var due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);
  var close = closeForStudent_(email, roster);
  if (isClosed_(close)) throw new Error(closedError_(close));
  var before = readRecord_(email);
  if (LOCK_AFTER_TURN_IN && before && before.turnedIn) throw new Error("You already turned this in, so answers are locked.");
  var p = roster[email] || {};
  var name = (p.period !== "" && p.period != null ? "P" + p.period + " " : "") + (p.name || email.split("@")[0]) +
             " - Q" + (idx + 1) + " - " + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz_(), "M-d-yyyy h.mm a") + "." + ext;
  var file;
  try { file = uploadFolder_().createFile(Utilities.newBlob(bytes, mime, name)); }
  catch (e) { throw new Error("BUSY: Couldn't save the picture to Google Drive right now (" + e.message + ")."); }
  var url = file.getUrl(), old = "", out, lock;
  try { lock = lockOrBusy_(20000); } catch (e) { try { file.setTrashed(true); } catch (e2) {} throw e; }
  try {
    var rec = readRecord_(email) || newRecord_();
    keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due);
    old = rec.answers[idx] || "";
    rec.answers[idx] = url;
    rec.statuses[idx] = "correct";
    if (rec.drafts) rec.drafts[idx] = null;
    rec.checks = (rec.checks || 0) + 1;
    rec.updated = new Date().toISOString();
    writeRecord_(email, rec);
    renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due);
    out = { result: feedbackFor_(idx, url), grade: lateGrade_(rec, due).grade, url: url };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  var oldId = driveId_(old);   // the picture this one replaces goes to your Drive trash (kept 30 days)
  if (oldId && oldId !== file.getId()) { try { DriveApp.getFileById(oldId).setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {} }
  try { logEvent_(email, idx, "uploaded", url); } catch (e) {}
  maybeRenderSummary_();
  return out;
}

/** The student's own uploaded picture, so the page can show it again after a reload. null = none. */
function getMyUpload(idx, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !ITEMS[idx].upload) throw new Error("Unknown question.");
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("mypic", email, 200);
  var rec = readRecord_(email), id = rec ? driveId_(rec.answers[idx]) : "";
  if (!id) return null;
  var blob;
  try { blob = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getBlob(); } catch (e) { return null; }
  var mime = String(blob.getContentType() || "");
  if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp)$/.test(mime)) return null;
  var bytes = blob.getBytes();
  if (bytes.length > UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES) return null;
  return { mime: mime, data: Utilities.base64Encode(bytes) };
}

/** What students receive for each question: text, picture, links. Never the criteria. */
function questionsForClient_() {
  return ITEMS.map(function (it) {
    return { text: it.q, img: imageUrl_(it.img), imgCaption: it.imgCaption || "", upload: !!it.upload, draw: !!it.draw,
             table: it.table ? { cols: it.table.cols, rows: it.table.rows, lab: it.table.lab || null, graph: it.table.graph || null, pairs: !!it.table.pairs, origin: !!it.table.origin, noGraph: !!it.table.noGraph } : null, minSentences: it.minSentences || 0,
             points: it.points == null ? null : it.points,
             choices: it.choices ? it.choices.map(function (c) { return { t: c.t, img: imageUrl_(c.img) }; }) : null,   // never which one is right (nor a choice's feedback)
             multi: !!(it.choices && it.kind !== "dropdown" && it.right.length > 1),   // "Select all correct options" (not how many)
             kind: isTried_(it) ? it.kind : "",   // LIMITED TRIES: "choice", "truefalse", "short" or "dropdown" ("" = everything else)
             blanks: isTried_(it) && it.kind === "dropdown" ? it.blanks : 0,   // DROPDOWNS: how many ___ (the page counts them too)
             dtable: isTried_(it) && it.dtable ? { cols: it.dtable.cols, rows: it.dtable.rows } : null,   // a dropdown's table, display only
             hints: isTried_(it) && it.hints ? { clue: it.hints.clue || "", example: it.hints.example || "", video: it.hints.video || "" } : null,   // the light bulb (hints never cost points)
             boxes: (it.boxes || []).map(function (b) { return { label: b.label, x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h }; }),
             links: (it.links || []).filter(function (l) { return l && buttonUrl_(l.url); }) };
  });
}

/** The section strips for the page: title, buttons, and the picture under the strip (as a direct image address). */
function sectionsForClient_() {
  return SECTIONS.map(function (s) {
    return { before: s.before, title: s.title, links: (s.links || []).filter(function (l) { return l && buttonUrl_(l.url); }),
             img: imageUrl_(s.img), imgCaption: s.imgCaption || "" };
  });
}

/** Turns a Google Drive share link into a direct image address; other https links pass through. */
function imageUrl_(u) {
  u = String(u || "").trim();
  if (!u) return "";
  var m = /drive\.google\.com\/(?:file\/d\/|open\?id=|uc\?(?:export=\w+&)?id=|thumbnail\?id=)([\w-]{20,})/.exec(u);
  if (m) return "https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id=" + m[1] + "&sz=w1600";
  return /^https:\/\//.test(u) ? u : "";
}

// ============================== WHO IS THIS STUDENT? (CLASS PASS) ==============================
// Google tells this script who the student is only when their SCHOOL account is the first Google account
// signed in to the browser (always true on school Chromebooks). Anywhere else the student signs in with
// CLASS PASS (the student link, see CLASS PASS below): "Sign in with Google", or, only while the Settings
// tab allows it, their school email and the 6-digit code emailed to it. Either one signs that browser in for
// SIGNIN_DAYS. Only students on the Roster tab can sign in this way. (The same as Video Check's.)
var SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES = 10;    // an emailed code works this long
var SIGNIN_TRIES = 5;            // wrong tries allowed per code
var SIGNIN_CODES_PER_HOUR = 5;   // codes one student can be sent per hour
var SIGNIN_DAYS = 14;            // a sign-in keeps that browser signed in this long
// "Sign in with Google" on the Class Pass page: Google's own account list, so a student picks their school
// account even when another account is signed in first. The client is Class Pass's own (Google Cloud project
// "Video Check sign-in", Audience: Internal). GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID (Settings tab "Class Pass Google client ID")
// replaces it only if Google ever deletes it; "off" hides the button.
var CLASS_PASS_CLIENT_ID = "3149691222-tatln28vl1l2e57nprt4t4lco0d9q5p1.apps.googleusercontent.com";
var GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = "";
// The emailed code (Settings tab "Allow emailed sign-in code"). Off: the page doesn't offer it and
// sendSignInCode / verifySignInCode refuse. Turn it on if Google sign-in is blocked for students.
var ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE = false;

var SIGNIN_KEEP = 5;             // sign-ins kept per student (phone, laptop, ...); older ones end
var SIGNIN_MAIL_PER_HOUR = 120;  // codes this sheet sends per hour, all students together
var SIGNIN_MAIL_PER_DAY = 400;   // ... and per day, so a flood can't use up your Gmail sending quota
var SIGNIN_MAIL_RESERVE = 50;    // emails a day always left for your own use
var VIA_POST_ = false;           // true while doPost runs: those requests count only their sign-in token (see doPost)

/** { email, via }: via is "google" (school account signed in first) or "code" (emailed code or Sign in with Google). */
function whoIs_(token) {
  // PASTE SAFETY: code for another kind of sheet never reads or saves a student's work
  if (wrongKind_()) throw new Error("This assignment is being updated right now. Please tell your teacher, and try again later.");
  // A request to doPost is identified only by its token, never by the browser's Google sign-in, so another
  // website can't make a signed-in student's browser save answers for them.
  var email = VIA_POST_ ? "" : String(Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || "").toLowerCase();
  if (email) return { email: email, via: "google" };
  email = sessionEmail_(token);
  return email ? { email: email, via: "code" } : { email: "", via: "" };
}

function currentStudent_(token) {
  var email = whoIs_(token).email;
  if (!email) throw new Error("SIGNIN: You're signed out. Sign in with Class Pass to keep working.");
  return email;
}

function signInState_() {
  return { needSignIn: true, title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE, domain: studentDomain_(readRoster_()), minutes: SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES,
           googleClient: googleClient_(), code: !!ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE, passLink: passLink_() };
}

/** The Class Pass page asks this first (no sign-in needed): what this sheet is, and how students may sign in.
    The teacher's Assignment Launchpad (class website /launchpad) asks classPass("launchpad") and also gets the grading
    settings it shows on this sheet's card: Automatic grades, whether the hourly check that uploads them is running,
    and Full credit at (nothing private; every student's page already gets Full credit at). */
function classPass(ask) {
  loadConfig_();
  var out = { classPass: 1, kind: SKILL_NAME, version: SKILL_VERSION, title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE, domain: studentDomain_(readRoster_()),
              googleClient: googleClient_(), code: !!ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE, minutes: SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES };
  if (wrongKind_()) out.sheetKind = sheetKind_();   // PASTE SAFETY: the Launchpad shows "wrong code" (the student page stops in whoIs_)
  if (ask === "launchpad") {
    var hourly = false;
    try { hourly = autoTriggers_().length > 0; } catch (e) {}
    out.settings = { autoGrades: !!AUTO_GRADES, hourly: hourly, fullCreditAt: Number(FULL_CREDIT_AT) };
    launchpadExtras_(out);
  }
  return out;
}

/** UPDATE FROM THE LAUNCHPAD (2026.11.10b): what the Class List sheet needs to update this sheet's code for the teacher (the Launchpad's
    Update now): where this code lives (its Script ID, nothing secret), whether the questions are in the Questions tab (a website release
    carries none), and which Settings rows or Questions columns Set up / repair sheets would add (the card says to run it). */
function launchpadExtras_(out) {
  try { out.scriptId = ScriptApp.getScriptId(); } catch (e) {}
  try { out.questionsInTab = questionsInTab_(); } catch (e) {}
  try { var m = missingPieces_(openSS_()), rep = m.settings.concat(m.columns); if (rep.length) out.repair = rep.slice(0, 8); } catch (e) {}
}

/** The Google client ID for Sign in with Google: the Settings tab's, or Class Pass's own, or "" when it's "off". */
function googleClient_() {
  var id = String(GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID == null ? "" : GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID).trim();
  if (/^(off|no|none)$/i.test(id)) return "";
  return /^[\w.-]+\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/.test(id) ? id : CLASS_PASS_CLIENT_ID;
}

/** " Or get a code by email instead." while codes are allowed, else a pointer to the teacher. */
function orCode_() { return ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE ? " Or get a code by email instead." : " If it keeps happening, tell your teacher."; }
/** Refuses an emailed-code request while the Settings tab doesn't allow codes (not just hidden on the page). */
function assertCodeAllowed_() {
  if (!ALLOW_SIGNIN_CODE) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Emailed sign-in codes are turned off for this assignment. Use Sign in with Google, or ask your teacher.");
}

/** The part after @ that most students on the roster use (the teacher's, if there's no roster yet). */
function studentDomain_(roster) {
  var count = {}, best = "", most = 0;
  Object.keys(roster || {}).forEach(function (e) {
    var d = e.split("@")[1];
    if (!d) return;
    count[d] = (count[d] || 0) + 1;
    if (count[d] > most) { most = count[d]; best = d; }
  });
  return best || String(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || "").toLowerCase().split("@")[1] || "";
}

/** What the student typed, as a full lowercase email ("jdoe" gets @ + the school domain), or "". */
function typedEmail_(typed, domain) {
  var e = String(typed == null ? "" : typed).toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "");
  if (e && e.indexOf("@") === -1 && domain) e += "@" + domain;
  return /^[^@]+@[^@]+\.[a-z]{2,}$/.test(e) ? e : "";
}

/** Sign-in step 1 (called by the page): email a 6-digit code to a student on the roster. */
function sendSignInCode(typed) {
  loadConfig_();
  assertCodeAllowed_();
  var roster = readRoster_(), domain = studentDomain_(roster);
  var email = typedEmail_(typed, domain);
  if (!email) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Type your whole school email address, like yourname@" + domain + ".");
  if (!mayUseSignIn_(email, roster, domain)) {
    throw new Error("SIGNIN: " + email + " isn't on the class list for this assignment. Check the spelling. If it's right, ask your teacher to refresh the roster.");
  }
  var c = cache_(), now = Date.now(), code;
  var busyText = "SIGNIN: Codes can't be sent right now. Wait a few minutes and try again, or ask your teacher.";
  if (!c) throw new Error(busyText);
  // The limits are counted inside the script lock, so requests that arrive together can't slip past them.
  // (Only the counting: the email itself is sent after the lock is let go.)
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) throw new Error("BUSY: Lots of students are signing in. Try again in a moment.");
  try {
    var sentKey = "signin:sent:" + email;
    var sent = (cacheGet_(sentKey) || []).filter(function (t) { return now - t < 3600000; });
    // Asked again within 45 s (often because the answer to the first request got lost): same code, no new email.
    if (sent.length && now - sent[sent.length - 1] < 45000 && cacheGet_("signin:code:" + email)) return { sent: true, email: email, minutes: SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES, again: true };
    if (sent.length >= SIGNIN_CODES_PER_HOUR) {
      throw new Error("SIGNIN: That's too many codes for one hour. Use the code in your newest email, or wait a while and try again.");
    }
    var hourKey = "signin:hour:" + Math.floor(now / 3600000), hourCount = Number(c.get(hourKey)) || 0;
    var today = Math.floor(now / 86400000), day = {};
    try { day = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("MAIL_DAY") || "{}"); } catch (e) { day = {}; }
    var dayCount = day.d === today ? Number(day.n) || 0 : 0;
    if (hourCount >= SIGNIN_MAIL_PER_HOUR || dayCount >= SIGNIN_MAIL_PER_DAY || MailApp.getRemainingDailyQuota() <= SIGNIN_MAIL_RESERVE) throw new Error(busyText);
    code = ("00000" + (parseInt(Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "").slice(0, 8), 16) % 1000000)).slice(-6);
    cachePut_("signin:code:" + email, { c: code, n: 0, x: now + SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES * 60000 }, SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES * 60);
    sent.push(now);
    cachePut_(sentKey, sent, 3600);
    c.put(hourKey, String(hourCount + 1), 3600);
    props_().setProperty("MAIL_DAY", JSON.stringify({ d: today, n: dayCount + 1 }));
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: email,
    subject: code + " is your Class Pass code for " + ASSIGNMENT_TITLE,
    body: "Your Class Pass sign-in code is " + code + ".\n\n" +
          "Type it on the \"" + ASSIGNMENT_TITLE + "\" page to keep working. It works for " + SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES + " minutes.\n\n" +
          "If you didn't ask for a code, you can ignore this email. Nobody can sign in as you without it."
  });
  return { sent: true, email: email, minutes: SIGNIN_CODE_MINUTES };
}

/** Who may sign in on the page (emailed code or Google): students on the Roster tab (any address at the
 *  school's domain before there's a roster), plus the teacher, to preview as a student. */
function mayUseSignIn_(email, roster, domain) {
  var teacher = String(Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || "").toLowerCase();
  var listed = Object.keys(roster).length ? !!roster[email] : email.split("@")[1] === domain;
  return listed || email === teacher;
}

/** Sign-in step 2 (called by the page): check the code. Returns a token the page keeps in the browser. */
function verifySignInCode(typed, code) {
  loadConfig_();
  assertCodeAllowed_();
  var email = typedEmail_(typed, studentDomain_(readRoster_()));
  code = String(code == null ? "" : code).replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (!email) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That code has expired. Press Send a new code.");
  var key = "signin:code:" + email, doneKey = "signin:done:" + email, badKey = "signin:bad:" + email, now = Date.now();
  // Checked and counted inside the script lock, so guesses sent all at once still only get SIGNIN_TRIES tries.
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(5000)) throw new Error("BUSY: Lots of students are signing in. Try again in a moment.");
  try {
    // The same right code again within 2 minutes (the answer to the first try got lost): the same sign-in.
    var done = cacheGet_(doneKey);
    if (done && done.c === code) return { token: done.t, email: email };
    var bad = Number(cacheRaw_(badKey)) || 0;
    if (bad >= SIGNIN_TRIES * 3) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Too many wrong codes this hour. Wait a while, then press Send a new code.");
    var rec = cacheGet_(key);
    if (!rec || rec.x < now) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That code has expired. Press Send a new code.");
    if (code !== rec.c) {
      rec.n = (rec.n || 0) + 1;
      cachePut_(badKey, bad + 1, 3600);
      if (rec.n >= SIGNIN_TRIES) { cacheDel_([key]); throw new Error("SIGNIN: Too many wrong tries. Press Send a new code."); }
      cachePut_(key, rec, Math.max(1, Math.ceil((rec.x - now) / 1000)));
      throw new Error("SIGNIN: That code isn't right. Use the code in the newest email.");
    }
    cacheDel_([key]);
    var token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "") + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
    saveSession_(token, email);
    cachePut_(doneKey, { c: code, t: token }, 120);
    return { token: token, email: email };
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}

/**
 * "Sign in with Google" on the Class Pass page (called by the page). Google's own sign-in page gives the page an
 * ID token: a note signed by Google saying which account signed in, and for which app. Google's token check
 * confirms the note is real and current; this makes sure it was made for THIS app's client ID, the address is
 * verified, and the student is on the roster. Returns the same kind of token as an emailed code, plus the
 * assignment itself (state: what getStudentState gives) and what the sheet is (kind), so the page needs no other
 * trip to the sheet before it opens the assignment.
 */
function signInWithGoogle(idToken) {
  loadConfig_();
  var cid = googleClient_();
  if (!cid) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Google sign-in is turned off for this assignment." + orCode_());
  idToken = String(idToken == null ? "" : idToken).trim();
  if (idToken.length > 8000 || !/^[\w-]+\.[\w-]+\.[\w-]+$/.test(idToken)) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Google's sign-in didn't come through. Try again." + orCode_());
  // First read what the token itself says (not trusted yet: Google's check below confirms it), so junk sent to this
  // sheet is refused without using up Google checks, and one prankster can't lock the whole class out for an hour.
  var said = tokenClaims_(idToken);
  if (!said) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Google's sign-in didn't come through. Try again." + orCode_());
  var email = String(said.email || "").toLowerCase();
  if (!googleClaimsOk_(said, cid, email)) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That Google sign-in can't be used here. Sign in with your school account." + orCode_());
  // Every sheet shares Class Pass's Google client, so an ID token by itself would work at any of them for an hour.
  // The Class Pass page ends the nonce (inside the signed token) with the deployment it sends the token to: only
  // a token made for THIS web app counts, so a link to some other web app can't collect a student's sign-in and use it here.
  var ownIds = ownAppIds_(), forApp = String(said.nonce || "").split(".").pop();
  if (!ownIds.length) throw new Error("SIGNIN: Google sign-in isn't ready for this assignment's link yet. Please tell your teacher: run Show student link in the sheet's menu once." + orCode_());
  if (ownIds.indexOf(forApp) === -1) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That Google sign-in was for a different assignment. Open this assignment's link again, then sign in.");
  var roster = readRoster_();
  if (!mayUseSignIn_(email, roster, studentDomain_(roster))) {
    throw new Error("SIGNIN: " + email + " isn't on the class list for this assignment. Sign in with your school account. If that's the one you used, ask your teacher to refresh the roster.");
  }
  // Caps on Google checks: per student, then for the whole sheet (they use your daily URL-fetch quota, which Drive
  // videos need too). A class signs in well under both.
  var hour = Math.floor(Date.now() / 3600000);
  var tryKey = "signin:gtry:" + email + ":" + hour, tries = Number(cacheRaw_(tryKey)) || 0;
  if (tries >= 10) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That's a lot of sign-ins for one hour. Wait a while, then try again.");
  cachePut_(tryKey, tries + 1, 3600);
  var hourKey = "signin:gchk:" + hour, checks = Number(cacheRaw_(hourKey)) || 0;
  if (checks >= Math.max(1000, 12 * Object.keys(roster).length)) throw new Error("BUSY: Lots of Google sign-ins right now. Try again in a few minutes.");
  cachePut_(hourKey, checks + 1, 3600);
  var r;
  try {
    r = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://oauth2.googleapis.com/tokeninfo", { method: "post", payload: { id_token: idToken }, muteHttpExceptions: true });
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("BUSY: Couldn't reach Google to check the sign-in (" + e.message + ").");
  }
  var status = r.getResponseCode(), c = null;
  if (status >= 500 || status === 429) throw new Error("BUSY: Google's sign-in check is busy. Try again in a minute.");
  try { c = JSON.parse(r.getContentText()); } catch (e) { c = null; }
  if (status !== 200 || !c) throw new Error("SIGNIN: That Google sign-in ran out or didn't work. Press Sign in with Google again.");
  // Google's answer is the one that counts: the same checks again, on what Google says.
  if (!googleClaimsOk_(c, cid, email) || String(c.email || "").toLowerCase() !== email || (c.nonce != null && String(c.nonce) !== String(said.nonce || ""))) {
    throw new Error("SIGNIN: That Google sign-in can't be used here. Sign in with your school account." + orCode_());
  }
  var token = Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "") + Utilities.getUuid().replace(/-/g, "");
  saveSession_(token, email);
  var out = { token: token, email: email, kind: SKILL_NAME };   // kind: which page opens, so the page needn't wait for classPass
  // The Class Pass page opens the assignment next: its first answer comes along, so that's one trip to the sheet
  // instead of two. (Pages from before 2026.09.30 ignore it and ask for it themselves.)
  try { var st = getStudentState(token); if (st && !st.needSignIn) out.state = st; } catch (e) { /* the page asks */ }
  return out;
}
/** Made for Class Pass's client, by Google, not run out, with a verified email address. */
function googleClaimsOk_(c, cid, email) {
  return c.aud === cid && (c.iss === "accounts.google.com" || c.iss === "https://accounts.google.com") &&
         Number(c.exp) * 1000 > Date.now() && String(c.email_verified) === "true" && /^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+$/.test(email);
}
/** The claims an ID token carries (its middle part), or null. Only Google's check proves they're real. */
function tokenClaims_(t) {
  try {
    var p = String(t).split(".")[1].replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/");
    while (p.length % 4) p += "=";
    var c = JSON.parse(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(p)).getDataAsString());
    return c && typeof c === "object" ? c : null;
  } catch (e) { return null; }
}

/** This web app's own deployment IDs: the one in the student link (saved by Show student link and step 2), and the one
 *  Google reports (only an /exec link counts). A Google sign-in is accepted only when it was made for one of them. */
function ownAppIds_() {
  var ids = [];
  [function () { return props_().getProperty("WEBAPP_URL"); }, function () { return ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); }].forEach(function (get) {
    var u = "";
    try { u = String(get() || ""); } catch (e) { u = ""; }
    var m = /\/macros\/s\/([\w-]+)\/exec$/.exec(plainUrl_(u));
    if (m && ids.indexOf(m[1]) === -1) ids.push(m[1]);
  });
  return ids;
}

/** "Sign out" on the page: forget this browser's code sign-in. */
function signOut(token) {
  if (/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(String(token || ""))) {
    var k = sessionKey_(token);
    props_().deleteProperty(k);
    cacheDel_([k]);
  }
  return { signedOut: true };
}

/** Sign-ins are kept as script properties named by a hash of the token, never the token itself. */
function sessionKey_(token) {
  var d = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, String(token), Utilities.Charset.UTF_8);
  return "SIGNIN_" + d.map(function (b) { return ("0" + (b & 255).toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join("");
}
function saveSession_(token, email) {
  var p = props_(), now = Date.now(), all = p.getProperties(), mine = [], gone = [];
  Object.keys(all).forEach(function (k) {   // tidy up sign-ins that have run out
    if (k.indexOf("SIGNIN_") !== 0) return;
    var s = null;
    try { s = JSON.parse(all[k]); } catch (e) {}
    if (!s || !(s.x > now)) { p.deleteProperty(k); gone.push(k); return; }
    if (s.e === email) mine.push({ k: k, x: s.x });
  });
  // Only this student's newest few sign-ins stay, so signing in over and over can't fill the script's storage
  // (linked classes and automatic grades are kept there too).
  mine.sort(function (a, b) { return b.x - a.x; });
  var old = mine.slice(SIGNIN_KEEP - 1).map(function (m) { return m.k; });
  old.forEach(function (k) { p.deleteProperty(k); });
  gone = gone.concat(old);
  if (gone.length) cacheDel_(gone);   // and their short-term copies
  p.setProperty(sessionKey_(token), JSON.stringify({ e: email, x: now + SIGNIN_DAYS * 86400000 }));
}
function sessionEmail_(token) {
  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(String(token || ""))) return "";
  var k = sessionKey_(token), s = cacheGet_(k);
  if (!s) {
    try { s = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty(k) || "null"); } catch (e) { s = null; }
    if (s) cachePut_(k, s, 21600);
  }
  return s && s.x > Date.now() ? s.e : "";
}

// ============================== CLASS PASS (the student link, on the class website) ==============================
// Google refuses to open any web app in a browser where another Google account is signed in before the
// school one, whatever the access setting, before any of this code runs. So the link students get is
// CLASS PASS, a page on the class website: CLASS_PASS_URL + "?app=" + this deployment's ID. The same page
// serves every Video Check and Self-Check sheet (it asks classPass() which one this is).
//   1. It loads this web app out of sight (doGet with ?probe=1, probePage_), which only reports whether
//      Google told this script who is signed in.
//   2. If Google did (school Chromebooks), the student goes on to the plain /exec link, exactly as before.
//   3. If not, the student signs in with Class Pass (WHO IS THIS STUDENT above), and the assignment runs on the class
//      website (/pass/check/, this same Index.html). It talks to doPost with fetch, and those requests carry no
//      Google sign-in (so there's nothing for two accounts to mix up), which is why the deployment's access is
//      "Anyone".
// Both ways save to this spreadsheet.
var CLASS_PASS_URL = "https://physical-science-8.pages.dev/pass/";   // "" = students get the plain /exec link

/** Everything the class website may call, and nothing else (no teacher or menu functions, ever). */
var API_ = { getStudentState: getStudentState, gradeAnswer: gradeAnswer, checkAnswer: checkAnswer, saveDraft: saveDraft,
             turnIn: turnIn, getPicture: getPicture, signOut: signOut, classPass: classPass,
             uploadPicture: uploadPicture, getMyUpload: getMyUpload, getArticle: getArticle, getArticlePicture: getArticlePicture,
             sendSignInCode: sendSignInCode, verifySignInCode: verifySignInCode, signInWithGoogle: signInWithGoogle,
             answerChoice: answerChoice, startOver: startOver, getLeaderboard: getLeaderboard, tryAnswer: tryAnswer, openHint: openHint, quickTry: quickTry, resetMyAnswers: resetMyAnswers };
var API_NO_TOKEN_ = { classPass: true, sendSignInCode: true, verifySignInCode: true, signInWithGoogle: true };

/**
 * The class website's requests: {fn, args, token} as text. The token goes in as the function's LAST argument
 * here on the server (missing arguments become null), so one Class Pass page works with every sheet's version.
 * Answers {ok: true, result} or {ok: false, error}; errors keep their SIGNIN:/BUSY:/NOT OPEN: starts.
 */
function doPost(e) {
  VIA_POST_ = true;   // identified by the token only (whoIs_): the class website never sends the browser's Google sign-in
  var out;
  try {
    var req = null;
    try { req = JSON.parse((e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || ""); } catch (err) { req = null; }
    if (!req || typeof req.fn !== "string" || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(API_, req.fn)) throw new Error("Unknown request.");
    var f = API_[req.fn], n = API_NO_TOKEN_[req.fn] ? f.length : f.length - 1;
    var args = (Array.isArray(req.args) ? req.args : []).slice(0, n);
    while (args.length < n) args.push(null);
    if (!API_NO_TOKEN_[req.fn]) args.push(typeof req.token === "string" ? req.token : "");
    out = { ok: true, result: f.apply(null, args) };
  } catch (err) {
    out = { ok: false, error: String((err && err.message) || err) };
  }
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(out)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

/** "https://physical-science-8.pages.dev" from CLASS_PASS_URL, or "". */
function classPassOrigin_() {
  var m = /^https:\/\/[^\/?#]+/.exec(String(CLASS_PASS_URL || ""));
  return m ? m[0] : "";
}

/**
 * Class Pass's out-of-sight check (doGet with ?probe=1). Tells the Class Pass page only whether Google said who
 * is signed in (so the plain link will open for them), and only if that page is the class website.
 * from = the Class Pass page's own address; the local test pages use http://localhost.
 */
function probePage_(from) {
  var known = false;
  try { known = !!Session.getActiveUser().getEmail(); } catch (e) { known = false; }
  from = String(from || "");
  var to = from && (from === classPassOrigin_() || /^http:\/\/localhost(:\d+)?$/.test(from)) ? from : "";
  // (The message is named videoCheckProbe for every app: the Class Pass page listens for that name.)
  var html = to ? "<script>try { window.top.postMessage({ videoCheckProbe: " + (known ? 1 : 0) + " }, " + JSON.stringify(to) + "); } catch (e) {}</script>" : "";
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(html).setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

/** The link students get: Class Pass for this deployment, or the plain /exec link if there's no Class Pass. */
function classPassLink_(url) {
  url = plainUrl_(url);
  var m = /\/macros\/s\/([\w-]+)\/exec$/.exec(url);
  return CLASS_PASS_URL && m ? CLASS_PASS_URL + "?app=" + m[1] : url;
}
/** The Class Pass link for the /exec page's sign-in card (from the link saved by Show student link or step 2), or "". */
function passLink_() {
  var url = "";
  try { url = props_().getProperty("WEBAPP_URL") || ""; } catch (e) { url = ""; }
  if (!url) try { url = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl() || ""; } catch (e) { url = ""; }
  var link = classPassLink_(url);
  return CLASS_PASS_URL && link.indexOf(CLASS_PASS_URL) === 0 ? link : "";
}

/**
 * A question's picture, for the class website (Drive only shows a picture shared with the district to a browser
 * whose FIRST Google account is a school one). Only a Drive picture in the Questions tab, only for a signed-in
 * student, and only an image file.
 */
/** A limit per student per hour (or per secs), so one signed-in page run in a loop can't use up the quotas every sheet shares. */
function perHour_(what, email, max, secs) {
  secs = secs || 3600;
  var k = "rate:" + what + ":" + email + ":" + Math.floor(Date.now() / (secs * 1000)), n = Number(cacheRaw_(k)) || 0;
  if (n >= max) throw new Error("BUSY: That's a lot for now. Wait a few minutes, then reload the page.");
  cachePut_(k, n + 1, secs);
}
var PICTURE_MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
function getPicture(idx, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("pic", email, 300);
  var sm = /^s(\d+)$/.exec(String(idx)), it;   // "s0", "s1": a section picture (under a blue strip)
  var cm = /^c(\d+)\.(\d+)$/.exec(String(idx));   // "c4.2": a review game question's picture choice (question 5, choice 3)
  if (sm) { it = SECTIONS[Number(sm[1])]; if (!it || !it.img) throw new Error("Unknown picture."); }
  else if (cm) { var cq = ITEMS[Number(cm[1])]; it = cq && cq.choices ? cq.choices[Number(cm[2])] : null; if (!it || !it.img) throw new Error("Unknown picture."); }
  else {
    idx = Number(idx);
    it = ITEMS[idx];
    if (!(idx >= 0 && idx < ITEMS.length) || !it.img) throw new Error("Unknown picture.");
  }
  var m = /drive\.google\.com\/(?:file\/d\/|open\?id=|uc\?(?:export=\w+&)?id=|thumbnail\?id=)([\w-]{20,})/.exec(String(it.img));
  if (!m) throw new Error("Unknown picture.");   // other https pictures load on the page directly
  // Kept in the short-term cache for an hour, so a class opening the page reads each picture from Drive about once, not once per student.
  var hit = bigCacheGet_("pic:" + m[1]), cut = hit ? hit.indexOf("|") : -1;
  if (cut > 0) return { mime: hit.slice(0, cut), data: hit.slice(cut + 1) };
  var blob;
  try { blob = DriveApp.getFileById(m[1]).getBlob(); } catch (e) { throw new Error("BUSY: Couldn't read the picture from Drive right now (" + e.message + ")."); }
  var mime = String(blob.getContentType() || "");
  if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp|bmp)$/.test(mime)) throw new Error("Unknown picture.");
  var bytes = blob.getBytes();
  if (bytes.length > PICTURE_MAX_BYTES) throw new Error("Unknown picture.");
  var data = Utilities.base64Encode(bytes);
  bigCachePut_("pic:" + m[1], mime + "|" + data, 3600);
  return { mime: mime, data: data };
}

// ============================== ARTICLE FROM A GOOGLE DOC (side-by-side reading pages) ==============================
// A reading page shows an article beside the questions. The teacher writes the article in an ordinary Google Doc and pastes its
// link in Settings (Article Google Doc link). Self-Check > Update the article from the Google Doc (or the hourly check, when the
// Doc has changed) turns the Doc into simple HTML plus its pictures and keeps them in the hidden tab _article (do not edit):
// a class of students never opens the Doc, the pictures arrive one at a time, and the article travels with File > Make a copy.
// How the Doc is read (docToArticle_): Title -> the big title; Heading 1 to 6 -> a blue strip; a paragraph starting "Q:" or "A:" ->
// the question/answer look; bullets and numbers -> lists; a paragraph that is only a link -> a blue button; a table row holding a
// picture and some text -> the picture beside the text (a row of pictures with short captions -> pictures side by side);
// bold, italic and links are kept; page breaks and empty paragraphs are ignored. Everything it writes is escaped text and a
// short list of tags, and the page rebuilds it again from an allowed list (safeArticle in Index.html).
var SH_ARTICLE = "_article (do not edit)";
var ARTICLE_CHUNK = 40000;                    // characters per cell (a cell holds up to 50,000)
var ARTICLE_MAX_PIC_BYTES = 3 * 1024 * 1024;  // a bigger picture is left out (and the teacher is told)
var ARTICLE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES = 12 * 1024 * 1024;
var ARTICLE_MAX_PICS = 60;
var ARTICLE_IMG_TYPES = /^image\/(png|jpeg|gif|webp|bmp)$/;
var ARTICLE_MAX_WIDTH = 460;                  // pictures are shown at most this many pixels wide

/** The Doc's id from a pasted link (or the bare id), or "". */
function docIdOf_(link) {
  var s = String(link == null ? "" : link).trim();
  var m = /\/d\/([\w-]{20,})/.exec(s) || /[?&]id=([\w-]{20,})/.exec(s);
  if (m) return m[1];
  return /^[\w-]{25,}$/.test(s) ? s : "";
}

/** What is stored: { v, name, src (the Doc's last change, ms), html: {start, count}, htmlLen, pics: [{mime, w, h, start, count, len}] } or null.
    Kept in cell B1 of the hidden tab, so a copy of the sheet keeps its article. */
function articleMeta_() {
  var hit = cacheGet_("article:meta");
  if (hit) return hit.none ? null : hit;
  var meta = null;
  try {
    var sh = openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_ARTICLE);
    if (sh && sh.getLastRow() >= 1) { var raw = String(sh.getRange(1, 2).getValue() || ""); meta = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null; }
  } catch (e) { meta = null; }
  cachePut_("article:meta", meta || { none: true }, 60);
  return meta;
}
function articleVersion_() { var m = articleMeta_(); return m ? m.v : 0; }

function readChunks_(sh, part, expectLen) {
  var vals = sh.getRange(part.start, 1, part.count, 1).getValues(), s = "";
  for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) s += String(vals[i][0]);
  if (s.length !== expectLen) throw new Error("BUSY: The article is being updated. Try again in a moment.");   // read while the teacher was updating it
  return s;
}

/** The reading page asks for the article in one trip: its text and, while they add up to ARTICLE_INLINE_MAX characters, its pictures
    (pics[i].data). A bigger picture still comes by itself (getArticlePicture). It is read from the hidden tab in one go and kept in the
    short-term cache under its version, so a whole class opening the page at once reads the tab about once. */
var ARTICLE_INLINE_MAX = 1500000;
function getArticle(token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("article", email, 200);
  var meta = articleMeta_();
  if (!meta) return { has: false };
  var parts = [meta.html], lens = [meta.htmlLen], inl = [], sum = 0;
  meta.pics.forEach(function (p, i) { if (sum + p.len <= ARTICLE_INLINE_MAX) { sum += p.len; inl.push(i); parts.push(p); lens.push(p.len); } });
  var key = "art:" + meta.v + ":" + ARTICLE_INLINE_MAX, all = bigCacheGet_(key);
  if (all == null || all.length !== meta.htmlLen + sum) {
    var last = 0;
    parts.forEach(function (p) { last = Math.max(last, p.start + p.count - 1); });
    var vals = openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_ARTICLE).getRange(1, 1, last, 1).getValues();
    all = parts.map(function (p, k) {
      var s = "";
      for (var r = p.start; r < p.start + p.count; r++) s += String(vals[r - 1][0]);
      if (s.length !== lens[k]) throw new Error("BUSY: The article is being updated. Try again in a moment.");   // read while the teacher was updating it
      return s;
    }).join("");
    bigCachePut_(key, all, 21600);
  }
  var at = meta.htmlLen, data = {};
  inl.forEach(function (i) { data[i] = all.substr(at, meta.pics[i].len); at += meta.pics[i].len; });
  return { has: true, v: meta.v, html: all.slice(0, meta.htmlLen),
           pics: meta.pics.map(function (p, i) { var o = { mime: p.mime, w: p.w, h: p.h }; if (data[i] != null) o.data = data[i]; return o; }) };
}
function getArticlePicture(idx, token) {
  loadConfig_();
  var email = currentStudent_(token);
  perHour_("articlepic", email, 600);
  var meta = articleMeta_();
  idx = Number(idx);
  if (!meta || !(idx >= 0 && idx < meta.pics.length)) throw new Error("Unknown picture.");
  var p = meta.pics[idx];
  return { mime: p.mime, data: readChunks_(openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_ARTICLE), p, p.len) };
}

/** Menu: Self-Check > Update the article from the Google Doc. */
function updateArticleFromDoc() {
  menuOnly_();
  clearConfigCache_();
  loadConfig_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), r;
  try { r = refreshArticle_(); }
  catch (e) { ui.alert("The article was not updated.\n\n" + e.message); return; }
  ui.alert("Article updated from \"" + r.name + "\".\n\n" + r.summary + (r.notes.length ? "\n\nThings to know:\n- " + r.notes.join("\n- ") : "") +
           "\n\nStudents see the new article the next time they load the page.");
}

/** Reads the Doc named in Settings and stores the article. Throws a plain-words message when it can't. Never shows pop-ups (the hourly check uses it). */
function refreshArticle_() {
  var id = docIdOf_(ARTICLE_DOC);
  if (!id) throw new Error("Paste the link to your Google Doc in the Settings tab first (the row \"Article Google Doc link\"), then run this again.");
  var doc;
  try { doc = DocumentApp.openById(id); }
  catch (e) { throw new Error("Couldn't open that Google Doc (" + e.message + "). Check the link, and that you (the teacher) can open the Doc."); }
  var conv = docToArticle_(doc);
  if (!conv.html.replace(/\s/g, "")) throw new Error("The Doc seems to have no text or pictures in it, so the article was left as it was.");
  var modified = 0;
  try { modified = DriveApp.getFileById(id).getLastUpdated().getTime(); } catch (e) {}
  saveArticle_(conv, doc.getName(), modified);
  return { name: doc.getName(), notes: conv.notes,
           summary: conv.counts.headings + " heading(s), " + conv.counts.paragraphs + " paragraph(s), " + conv.counts.lists + " list(s), " +
                    conv.pics.length + " picture(s) (" + Math.round(conv.bytes / 1024) + " KB)." };
}

/** The hourly check: update the article by itself when the Doc has changed since it was last stored. */
function articleAutoRefresh_() {
  var id = docIdOf_(ARTICLE_DOC);
  if (!id) return;
  var meta = articleMeta_();
  if (!meta || !meta.src) return;   // never stored by hand yet (or the Doc's date wasn't known): the teacher presses the menu item first
  if (DriveApp.getFileById(id).getLastUpdated().getTime() <= meta.src) return;
  refreshArticle_();
}

/** Writes the article into the hidden tab: the text and each picture as 40,000-character pieces, one per row (column A); the index in B1. */
function saveArticle_(conv, name, modified) {
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(30000);
  try {
    var ss = openSS_(), sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_ARTICLE);
    if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(SH_ARTICLE); try { sh.hideSheet(); } catch (e) {} }
    var rows = [], meta = { v: Date.now(), name: name, src: modified, htmlLen: conv.html.length, pics: [] };
    var add = function (str) {
      var start = rows.length + 1, n = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < str.length; i += ARTICLE_CHUNK) { rows.push([str.substr(i, ARTICLE_CHUNK)]); n++; }
      if (!n) { rows.push([""]); n = 1; }
      return { start: start, count: n };
    };
    meta.html = add(conv.html);
    conv.pics.forEach(function (p) { var r = add(p.data); meta.pics.push({ mime: p.mime, w: p.w, h: p.h, start: r.start, count: r.count, len: p.data.length }); });
    sh.clearContents();
    sh.getRange(1, 1, rows.length, 1).setValues(rows);
    sh.getRange(1, 2).setValue(JSON.stringify(meta));
    cacheDel_(["article:meta"]);
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
}

/** A Google Doc -> { html, pics: [{mime, w, h, data (base64)}], notes: [text for the teacher], counts, bytes }. */
function docToArticle_(doc) {
  var T = DocumentApp.ElementType, H = DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading, G = DocumentApp.GlyphType;
  var pics = [], notes = [], counts = { headings: 0, paragraphs: 0, lists: 0 }, bytes = 0;
  function esc(s) { return String(s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;"); }
  function note(m) { if (notes.length < 12 && notes.indexOf(m) === -1) notes.push(m); }

  /** One picture: stored once and referred to by number. Returns { html, w } or null. */
  function picture(img) {
    var blob, mime, raw;
    try { blob = img.getBlob(); mime = String(blob.getContentType() || ""); raw = blob.getBytes(); }
    catch (e) { note("A picture couldn't be read, so it was left out."); return null; }
    if (!ARTICLE_IMG_TYPES.test(mime)) { note("A picture of type " + mime + " can't be shown, so it was left out (use a PNG or JPEG picture)."); return null; }
    if (pics.length >= ARTICLE_MAX_PICS) { note("Only the first " + ARTICLE_MAX_PICS + " pictures are used."); return null; }
    if (raw.length > ARTICLE_MAX_PIC_BYTES) { note("A picture is " + (Math.round(raw.length / 1048576 * 10) / 10) + " MB, which is too big, so it was left out. Use a smaller picture."); return null; }
    if (bytes + raw.length > ARTICLE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES) { note("The pictures add up to more than " + Math.round(ARTICLE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES / 1048576) + " MB, so the last ones were left out."); return null; }
    var w = 0, h = 0, alt = "";
    try { w = Number(img.getWidth()) || 0; h = Number(img.getHeight()) || 0; } catch (e) {}
    if (!w || !h) { w = 300; h = 200; }
    if (w > ARTICLE_MAX_WIDTH) { h = Math.round(h * ARTICLE_MAX_WIDTH / w); w = ARTICLE_MAX_WIDTH; }
    try { alt = img.getAltDescription() || img.getAltTitle() || ""; } catch (e) {}
    bytes += raw.length;
    pics.push({ mime: mime, w: w, h: h, data: Utilities.base64Encode(raw) });
    return { w: w, html: '<img data-pic="' + (pics.length - 1) + '" width="' + w + '" height="' + h + '" alt="' + esc(alt) + '">' };
  }
  /** Pictures standing on their own: one centered, several side by side. */
  function flowImages(imgs) {
    var prs = imgs.map(picture).filter(function (x) { return x; });
    if (!prs.length) return "";
    if (prs.length === 1) return '<figure class="' + (prs[0].w >= 250 ? "artfig artwide" : "artfig") + '">' + prs[0].html + "</figure>";
    return '<div class="artpair">' + prs.map(function (x) { return '<figure class="artfig">' + x.html + "</figure>"; }).join("") + "</div>";
  }
  function figureOf(imgs, caption) {
    var prs = imgs.map(picture).filter(function (x) { return x; });
    if (!prs.length) return "";
    return '<figure class="artfig">' + (caption ? "<figcaption>" + caption + "</figcaption>" : "") + prs.map(function (x) { return x.html; }).join("") + "</figure>";
  }

  /** A Text element as inline HTML (bold, italic, links) and plain text. */
  function textHtml(t) {
    var s = t.getText();
    if (!s) return { html: "", plain: "", linked: false };
    var idx = t.getTextAttributeIndices(), html = "", plain = "", allLinked = true, any = false;
    for (var k = 0; k < idx.length; k++) {
      var from = idx[k], to = k + 1 < idx.length ? idx[k + 1] : s.length, seg = s.substring(from, to);
      if (!seg) continue;
      var clean = seg.replace(/ /g, " "), url = "";
      try { url = t.getLinkUrl(from) || ""; } catch (e) {}
      var safe = /^https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>\\]+$/i.test(url) ? url : "";
      var part = esc(clean).replace(/[\u000b\r\n]+/g, "<br>");
      if (clean.trim()) { any = true; if (!safe) allLinked = false; }
      if (safe) part = '<a href="' + esc(safe) + '">' + part + "</a>";
      var b = false, it = false;
      try { b = t.isBold(from) === true; it = t.isItalic(from) === true; } catch (e) {}
      if (b) part = "<b>" + part + "</b>";
      if (it) part = "<i>" + part + "</i>";
      html += part; plain += clean;
    }
    return { html: html, plain: plain, linked: any && allLinked };
  }
  /** A paragraph or list item: its text as inline HTML, and its pictures. */
  function parts(p) {
    var html = "", plain = "", linked = true, any = false, imgs = [];
    for (var j = 0; j < p.getNumChildren(); j++) {
      var c = p.getChild(j), ty = c.getType();
      if (ty === T.TEXT) {
        var r = textHtml(c.asText());
        html += r.html; plain += r.plain;
        if (r.plain.trim()) { any = true; if (!r.linked) linked = false; }
      } else if (ty === T.INLINE_IMAGE) imgs.push(c.asInlineImage());
    }
    try { var pos = p.getPositionedImages(); for (var q = 0; q < pos.length; q++) imgs.push(pos[q]); } catch (e) {}
    return { html: html, plain: plain.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(), linkOnly: any && linked, imgs: imgs };
  }

  /** The blocks inside the Doc's body or a table cell. collect: pictures are handed back (a table decides where they go). */
  function blocks(container, collect) {
    var html = [], imgsOut = [], list = null, vids = [], textLen = 0, textBlocks = 0;
    function flushList() { if (list) { html.push("<" + list.tag + ">" + list.items.join("") + "</" + list.tag + ">"); counts.lists++; list = null; } }
    function flushVids() { if (vids.length) { html.push('<p class="artvids">' + vids.join(" ") + "</p>"); vids = []; } }
    function flush() { flushList(); flushVids(); }
    function pictures(imgs) { if (!imgs.length) return; flush(); if (collect) imgsOut = imgsOut.concat(imgs); else { var f = flowImages(imgs); if (f) html.push(f); } }
    for (var i = 0; i < container.getNumChildren(); i++) {
      var el = container.getChild(i), ty = el.getType();
      try {
        if (ty === T.PARAGRAPH) {
          var p = el.asParagraph(), pt = parts(p), hd = p.getHeading();
          var isTitle = hd === H.TITLE, isHead = hd === H.HEADING1 || hd === H.HEADING2 || hd === H.HEADING3 || hd === H.HEADING4 || hd === H.HEADING5 || hd === H.HEADING6;
          if (pt.plain && (isTitle || isHead)) {
            flush();
            html.push(isTitle ? '<h2 class="arttitle">' + pt.html + "</h2>" : "<h3>" + pt.html + "</h3>");
            if (isHead) counts.headings++;
          } else if (pt.plain && pt.linkOnly) {
            flushList();   // a paragraph that is only a link: a blue button (several in a row sit together)
            vids.push(pt.html.replace(/<\/?[bi]>/g, "").replace(/<a /g, '<a class="simbtn" ').replace(/(<a [^>]*>)\s+/g, "$1"));
          } else if (pt.plain) {
            flush();
            textLen += pt.plain.length; textBlocks++;
            var qa = /^((?:<[^>\/][^>]*>)*)\s*([QA]):((?:<\/[^>]+>)*)\s*/i.exec(pt.html);
            if (qa && /^[QA]:/i.test(pt.plain)) {
              var who = qa[2].toUpperCase();
              html.push('<p class="' + (who === "Q" ? "artq" : "arta") + '"><b>' + who + ":</b> " + pt.html.slice(qa[0].length) + "</p>");
            } else html.push("<p>" + pt.html + "</p>");
            counts.paragraphs++;
          }
          pictures(pt.imgs);
        } else if (ty === T.LIST_ITEM) {
          var li = el.asListItem(), lt = parts(li);
          if (lt.plain) {
            flushVids();
            var gt = li.getGlyphType(), numbered = gt === G.NUMBER || gt === G.LATIN_UPPER || gt === G.LATIN_LOWER || gt === G.ROMAN_UPPER || gt === G.ROMAN_LOWER;
            var tag = numbered ? "ol" : "ul";
            if (!list || list.tag !== tag) { flushList(); list = { tag: tag, items: [] }; }
            list.items.push("<li>" + lt.html + "</li>");
            textLen += lt.plain.length; textBlocks++;
          }
          pictures(lt.imgs);
        } else if (ty === T.TABLE) {
          flush();
          html.push(tableHtml(el.asTable()));
        } else if (ty !== T.PAGE_BREAK && ty !== T.HORIZONTAL_RULE) {
          note("Something in the Doc that isn't text, a list, a table or a picture (like a drawing or a chart) was skipped.");
        }
