      } catch (e) { note("A part of the Doc couldn't be read and was skipped (" + e.message + ")."); }
    }
    flush();
    return { html: html.join("\n"), imgs: imgsOut, textLen: textLen, textBlocks: textBlocks };
  }
  /** One picture with a short caption at most: it can sit beside text, or beside another such picture. */
  function isFigureCell(c) { return c.imgs.length === 1 && c.textLen <= 140 && c.textBlocks <= 1; }
  function tableHtml(table) {
    var out = [];
    for (var r = 0; r < table.getNumRows(); r++) {
      var row = table.getRow(r), cells = [];
      for (var c = 0; c < row.getNumCells(); c++) cells.push(blocks(row.getCell(c), true));
      var figs = cells.map(isFigureCell);
      var caption = function (cell) { return cell.textLen ? cell.html.replace(/<\/?p[^>]*>/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() : ""; };
      if (cells.length >= 2 && figs.every(function (x) { return x; })) {
        out.push('<div class="artpair">' + cells.map(function (cell) { return figureOf(cell.imgs, caption(cell)); }).join("") + "</div>");
      } else if (cells.length === 2 && (figs[0] !== figs[1]) && !(figs[0] ? cells[1] : cells[0]).imgs.length) {
        out.push('<div class="artrow">' + cells.map(function (cell, k) {
          return figs[k] ? figureOf(cell.imgs, caption(cell)) : '<div class="arttxt">' + cell.html + "</div>";
        }).join("") + "</div>");
      } else {
        cells.forEach(function (cell) { out.push(cell.html); var f = flowImages(cell.imgs); if (f) out.push(f); });
      }
    }
    return out.join("\n");
  }

  var res = blocks(doc.getBody(), false);
  return { html: res.html, pics: pics, notes: notes, counts: counts, bytes: bytes };
}


// ============================== FEEDBACK + GRADE ==============================
function feedbackFor_(idx, answer) {
  answer = String(answer || "").trim();
  if (!answer) return { status: "blank", message: "" };
  if (isTried_(ITEMS[idx])) return tryGrade_(ITEMS[idx], answer);   // one try on its own (the page gets triedFeedback_ instead)
  if (ITEMS[idx].choices) return choiceFeedback_(ITEMS[idx], answer);
  if (ITEMS[idx].upload) return { status: "correct", message: ITEMS[idx].draw ? "Drawing saved. Thanks!" : "Picture added. Thanks! (This one isn't graded.)" };
  if (ITEMS[idx].table) return tableFeedback_(ITEMS[idx], answer);
  if (ITEMS[idx].boxes && ITEMS[idx].boxes.length) return boxFeedback_(ITEMS[idx], answer);
  return sentenceRule_(ITEMS[idx], answer, textFeedback_(ITEMS[idx], answer));
}
/** "Minimum sentences": an answer with fewer complete sentences can't be green, and the hint says how many it has. */
function sentenceRule_(item, answer, fb) {
  var need = item.minSentences;
  if (!need || fb.status === "blank") return fb;
  var have = countSentences_(answer);
  if (have >= need) return fb;
  var tip = "Write at least " + need + " complete sentences, each ending with a period (you have " + have + ").";
  if (fb.status === "correct") return { status: "partial", message: fb.message.replace(/Nice work, you covered the key ideas!|Answer recorded, nice work!/, "You have the key ideas. ") + tip, frac: capFrac_(fb.frac) };
  return { status: fb.status, message: fb.message + " " + tip, frac: fb.frac };
}
/** A question held back from green (too short, too few sentences) can't earn more than the yellow share of its points. */
function capFrac_(f) { return typeof f === "number" ? Math.min(f, CREDIT.partial) : undefined; }
/** frac = the share of the question's ideas the answer shows. A question worth Points earns that share of them (see gradeFor_).
 *  A misconception in the answer (its key words are there) shows its hint as "Check this: ..." and holds the answer at yellow. */
function textFeedback_(item, answer) {
  var res = gradeLocal_(item, answer), minLen = item.short ? 0 : MIN_ANSWER_LENGTH;
  var mis = misconceptionsIn_(item, answer), check = mis.length ? "Check this: " + mis.join(" ") + " " : "";
  if (!res.total) {   // no key words: any thoughtful answer counts
    if (answer.length < minLen) return { status: "partial", message: check + "Good start. Explain a little more (at least " + minLen + " characters)." };
    return check ? { status: "partial", message: check.trim() } : { status: "correct", message: "Answer recorded, nice work!" };
  }
  var sc = "Score: " + res.score + "/" + res.total + ". ", frac = res.score / res.total;
  if (res.rows.length) sc += "Rubric: " + res.rows.map(function (r) { return r.label + " " + r.met + "/" + r.total; }).join(", ") + ". ";
  if (check) frac = capFrac_(frac);
  // Held at yellow by a misconception: no "Score: 3/3" (students read a full score as "this should be green").
  if (res.status === "correct" && check) return { status: "partial", message: "Almost! Your answer uses the right words, but part of it shows a common mix-up. Check this: " + mis.join(" "), frac: frac };
  if (res.status === "correct" && answer.length < minLen) {
    return { status: "partial", message: sc + "You used the right words, but that's too short to show your thinking. Explain in a full sentence or two (at least " + minLen + " characters).", frac: capFrac_(frac) };
  }
  if (res.status === "correct") return { status: "correct", message: sc + "Nice work, you covered the key ideas!", frac: frac };
  if (res.status === "partial") return { status: "partial", message: sc + "Good start! " + check + "Still missing: " + res.unmet.join(" "), frac: frac };
  return { status: "incorrect", message: sc + check + res.unmet.join(" "), frac: frac };
}
/** The hints of the misconceptions (common wrong ideas) this answer shows, from the Questions tab's Misconception columns. */
function misconceptionsIn_(item, answer) {
  if (!item.mis || !item.mis.length) return [];
  var padded = normalize_(answer), words = padded.trim().split(" "), nums = isNumberQ_(item) ? numbersIn_(answer) : null;   // a number question compares numbers
  return item.mis.filter(function (m) { return criterionMet_(m, padded, words, answer, nums); }).map(function (m) { return m.nudge; });
}

/** Picture-box question: each box graded by its own idea. No minimum length. */
function boxFeedback_(item, answer) {
  var n = item.boxes.length, parts = splitBoxes_(answer, n), marks = [], score = 0, unmet = [], filled = 0;
  for (var k = 0; k < n; k++) {
    var cr = item.criteria[k];
    if (!parts[k]) { marks.push("blank"); if (cr) unmet.push(cr.nudge); continue; }
    filled++;
    var padded = normalize_(parts[k]);
    var ok = !cr || criterionMet_(cr, padded, padded.trim().split(" "), parts[k]);
    marks.push(ok ? "correct" : "incorrect");
    if (ok) score++; else unmet.push(cr.nudge);
  }
  if (!filled) return { status: "blank", message: "", parts: marks };
  var sc = "Score: " + score + "/" + n + ". ", frac = score / n;
  if (score === n) return { status: "correct", message: sc + "Nice work, every box is right!", parts: marks, frac: frac };
  if (score === 0) return { status: "incorrect", message: sc + unmet.join(" "), parts: marks, frac: frac };
  return { status: "partial", message: sc + "Good start! Still missing: " + unmet.join(" "), parts: marks, frac: frac };
}

function statusesOf_(results) { return results.map(function (r) { return r.status; }); }

/** Colors -> grade out of MAX_POINTS. See SETTINGS for the knobs. A question counts 1 unless the Questions tab gives it Points;
 *  one that has Points earns the share of its ideas the answer shows (answers = the checked answers), or its color's credit when
 *  it has no key words to count. */
function gradeFor_(statuses, answers, tries, only) {
  var frac = creditFrac_(statuses, answers, tries, only);
  if (frac == null) return 0;
  var pts = Math.min(1, frac / FULL_CREDIT_AT) * MAX_POINTS;
  return Math.round(Math.round(pts / ROUND_TO) * ROUND_TO * 1000) / 1000;   // (the outer round: 96 x 0.1 is 9.600000000000001 in JavaScript)
}
/** The share of the possible credit this work earned (0 to 1), or null when nothing is graded. See gradeFor_.
 *  tries = rec.tries: a question with limited tries earns its tries' credit (see LIMITED TRIES), times its Points as usual.
 *  only = the questions to count (a review game's try: see QUESTIONS PER TRY); left out = every question. */
function creditFrac_(statuses, answers, tries, only) {
  var earned = 0, total = 0, mine = only ? setMap_(only) : null;
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) {
    var it = ITEMS[i];
    if (ungraded_(it) || (mine && !mine[i])) continue;   // picture uploads and graphs with no label boxes aren't graded
    var w = it.points == null ? 1 : it.points, st = statuses[i] || "blank", c = CREDIT[st] || 0;
    if (isTried_(it)) c = tryState_(i, tries ? tries[i] : null).credit;
    else if (it.points != null && st !== "blank" && answers) {
      var fb = feedbackFor_(i, answers[i]);
      if (typeof fb.frac === "number") c = fb.frac;
    }
    total += w; earned += w * c;
  }
  return total ? earned / total : null;
}

/** A student's grade (no late penalty): the work on the page now, or a review game's best earlier try (rec.best), whichever is higher. */
function workGrade_(work) {
  var g = gradeFor_(work.statuses || [], work.answers, work.tries, workSet_(work));   // (a review game: over the try's own questions)
  if (work.best) g = Math.max(g, gradeFor_(work.best.statuses || [], work.best.answers, work.best.tries, workSet_(work.best)));
  return g;
}

/** Green / yellow / red / blank. only = the questions to count (a review game's try), left out = every question. */
function counts_(statuses, only) {
  var c = { correct: 0, partial: 0, incorrect: 0, blank: 0 }, mine = only ? setMap_(only) : null;
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) if (!ungraded_(ITEMS[i]) && (!mine || mine[i])) c[statuses[i] || "blank"]++;
  return c;
}

// ============================== LATE WORK ==============================
// The first time a student saves an answer after their class's due date (Classroom), a copy of their work
// as it stood at the due date is kept in their record (rec.onTime). Their grade is then:
//   on-time grade + (current grade - on-time grade) x (1 - LATE_PENALTY)
// so only the points added after the due date lose 20%. 8/10 on time, 10/10 later = 8 + 2 x 0.8 = 9.6.
// If the teacher moves the due date later, a copy made before the new due date is thrown away.
// Students who never started before the due date have a blank on-time copy, so all their points are late.

var DUE_CHECK_SECS = 3600;   // ask Classroom for a class's due date at most once an hour (a student's page loading never asks)

function latePct_() { var p = Number(LATE_PENALTY) || 0; return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p)); }

/** The DUE_DATES script property: { <Classroom assignment id>: { d: due date in ms (0 = none), at: when Classroom was asked } }.
    Sheets on 2026.09.30 kept a plain number there: it's read as asked long ago. */
function dueDates_() {
  try { var k = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("DUE_DATES") || "{}"); return k && typeof k === "object" ? k : {}; } catch (e) { return {}; }
}
function dueEntry_(v) {
  if (v == null || v === "") return null;
  if (typeof v === "object") return { d: Number(v.d) || 0, at: Number(v.at) || 0, c: Number(v.c) || 0 };
  return { d: Number(v) || 0, at: 0, c: 0 };
}

/** Remembers a class's due date (ms, 0 = none) and that Classroom was asked just now.
    created: when the Classroom assignment was made (ms), kept from before when not given. Used by QUARTER CLOSE. */
function rememberDue_(cwId, ms, created) {
  try {
    var known = dueDates_(), prev = dueEntry_(known[cwId]);
    known[cwId] = { d: ms || 0, at: Date.now(), c: created || (prev && prev.c) || 0 };
    props_().setProperty("DUE_DATES", JSON.stringify(known));
  } catch (e) {}
}

/** Due date of a linked class in ms (0 = no due date). Classroom is asked at most once an hour per class, and never
    when quick (a student's page loading): that uses the last answer however old, or 0 before the first one. */
function dueOfCourse_(course, quick) {
  if (!course || !course.cwId) return 0;
  var known = dueEntry_(dueDates_()[course.cwId]);
  if (quick || (known && Date.now() - known.at < DUE_CHECK_SECS * 1000)) return known ? known.d : 0;
  if (known) rememberDue_(course.cwId, known.d);   // this hour's question is taken, so a whole class saving at once asks only once
  try {
    var d = classroomDue_(course);   // remembers the answer
    return d ? d.getTime() : 0;
  } catch (e) {
    if (!known) rememberDue_(course.cwId, 0);   // Classroom didn't answer: ask again in an hour
    return known ? known.d : 0;
  }
}

/** The linked class a roster student belongs to (with its Classroom assignment), or null. */
function courseOfStudent_(email, roster) {
  var p = (roster || readRoster_())[email];
  if (!p) return null;
  var list = courses_();
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (String(list[i].id) === String(p.courseId) && list[i].cwId) return list[i];
  return null;
}

/** A student's due date in ms, or 0 when there's no late penalty, no linked class, or no due date. quick: see dueOfCourse_. */
function dueForStudent_(email, roster, quick) {
  if (!latePct_()) return 0;
  try { return dueOfCourse_(courseOfStudent_(email, roster), quick); } catch (e) { return 0; }
}

/** Keeps (or drops) the student's copy of their work as it was at the due date. Call inside the lock, BEFORE changing answers. */
function keepOnTimeCopy_(rec, due) {
  if (rec.onTime && (!due || Date.parse(rec.onTime.taken) < due)) rec.onTime = null;   // due date removed or moved later
  if (due && !rec.onTime && Date.now() > due) {
    rec.onTime = { answers: rec.answers.slice(), statuses: rec.statuses.slice(), taken: new Date().toISOString() };
    if (rec.best) rec.onTime.best = rec.best;   // a review game's best try so far counts as on time too
    if (rec.game && rec.game.set) rec.onTime.set = rec.game.set.slice();   // ...graded over the questions its try had (QUESTIONS PER TRY)
    if (rec.tries) rec.onTime.tries = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rec.tries));   // limited tries: the tries made by the due date
  }
}

/** Grade with the late penalty: { grade, late, onTime, full }. late = true only when late work lowered the grade. */
function lateGrade_(rec, due) {
  var full = workGrade_(rec), snap = rec.onTime, pct = latePct_();
  if (!pct || !due || !snap || Date.parse(snap.taken) < due) return { grade: full, late: false };
  var onTime = workGrade_(snap);
  if (full <= onTime) return { grade: full, late: false, onTime: onTime, full: full };
  var g = Math.round((onTime + (full - onTime) * (1 - pct)) * 10) / 10;   // to a tenth, so 9.6 stays 9.6
  return { grade: g, late: g < full, onTime: onTime, full: full };
}

function pctText_() { return Math.round(latePct_() * 100) + "%"; }
function num_(n) { return String(Math.round(n * 10) / 10); }

/** The one-line explanation used on the student page, in emails, and in the teacher's grade notes. */
function lateNote_() {
  return "Late penalty: work finished after the due date earns " + Math.round((1 - latePct_()) * 100) +
         "% of its points (" + pctText_() + " off the late part only).";
}
function lateCellNote_(info) {
  return info.late ? "Late penalty: " + num_(info.onTime) + " by the due date, " + num_(info.full) + " now. The " +
         num_(info.full - info.onTime) + " late points count " + Math.round((1 - latePct_()) * 100) + "%, so the grade is " + num_(info.grade) + "." : "";
}

/** Emails one student about their late penalty. Returns "" or a problem to report. */
function mailLatePenalty_(email, info, name, due) {
  if (MailApp.getRemainingDailyQuota() <= SIGNIN_MAIL_RESERVE) return "late-penalty email not sent to " + email + " (daily email limit reached)";
  var first = String(name || "").split(" ")[0] || "there";
  var when = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(due), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "EEE M/d h:mm a");
  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: email,
    subject: "Late work penalty: " + ASSIGNMENT_TITLE,
    body: "Hi " + first + ",\n\n" +
          "Your grade for \"" + ASSIGNMENT_TITLE + "\" is " + num_(info.grade) + " / " + MAX_POINTS + ".\n\n" +
          "By the due date (" + when + ") you had " + num_(info.onTime) + " / " + MAX_POINTS + ". The work you finished after the due date " +
          "brought it up to " + num_(info.full) + " / " + MAX_POINTS + ", and late work loses " + pctText_() + ", so those " +
          num_(info.full - info.onTime) + " points count as " + num_(info.grade - info.onTime) + ".\n\n" +
          "Nice job finishing it. Reply to this email if you have a question."
  });
  return "";
}

// ============================== QUARTER CLOSE ==============================
// When the quarter an assignment belongs to is over, students can still open it and see their answers, colors,
// feedback and grade, but can't change anything: gradeAnswer / checkAnswer / saveDraft / turnIn refuse, and the page
// shows a pop-up and turns the boxes off. Which quarter: the one the class's Classroom due date falls in (no due date =
// the day step 2 created the assignment). When: the quarter's last day, at the moment that period's class starts
// (CLASS_START_TIMES, changed for some days by CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES). Quarter end dates: QUARTER_END_DATES when typed, else Classroom's grading periods, which are
// asked at most every QUARTER_CHECK_SECS and kept in the script property QUARTERS: { list: [{ end: "yyyy-MM-dd",
// name }], at, err }. A student's page loading never waits on Classroom, except once if it was never asked.

var QUARTER_CHECK_SECS = 6 * 3600;

function tz_() { return Session.getScriptTimeZone(); }
function pad2_(n) { return (n < 10 ? "0" : "") + n; }
function isDate_(v) { return Object.prototype.toString.call(v) === "[object Date]"; }
function ymdOf_(ms) { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ms), tz_(), "yyyy-MM-dd"); }
function atLocal_(ymd, mins) { return Utilities.parseDate(ymd + " " + pad2_(Math.floor(mins / 60)) + ":" + pad2_(mins % 60), tz_(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm").getTime(); }
function dayLabel_(ymd) { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(atLocal_(ymd, 720)), tz_(), "EEEE, MMMM d"); }
function timeLabel_(ms) { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ms), tz_(), "h:mm a"); }

/** The year a month falls in during this school year (July to June). */
function schoolYearOf_(month) {
  var now = new Date(), y = Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, tz_(), "yyyy")), m = Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, tz_(), "M"));
  var start = m >= 7 ? y : y - 1;
  return month >= 7 ? start : start + 1;
}

/** "10/16/2026, 12/18/2026, ..." (also 10/16, 2026-10-16, "Q2: 12/18") -> { list: [{ end, name }], bad: [text] }, sorted. */
function parseQuarterDates_(v) {
  var out = { list: [], bad: [] };
  if (v == null || v === "") return out;
  var parts = isDate_(v) ? [v] : String(v).split(/[,;\n]+/);
  parts.forEach(function (p) {
    var ymd = "", label = "";
    if (isDate_(p)) ymd = Utilities.formatDate(p, tz_(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
    else {
      p = String(p).trim();
      if (!p) return;
      var q = /\bq(?:uarter)?\s*(\d)\b/i.exec(p);
      if (q) label = "Quarter " + q[1];
      var m = /(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})/.exec(p), y, mo, d;
      if (m) { y = +m[1]; mo = +m[2]; d = +m[3]; }
      else if ((m = /(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\/(\d{2,4}))?/.exec(p))) {
        mo = +m[1]; d = +m[2]; y = m[3] ? +m[3] : schoolYearOf_(mo);
        if (y < 100) y += 2000;
      }
      if (!m || mo < 1 || mo > 12 || d < 1 || d > 31) { out.bad.push(p); return; }
      ymd = y + "-" + pad2_(mo) + "-" + pad2_(d);
    }
    out.list.push({ end: ymd, name: label });
  });
  out.list.sort(function (a, b) { return a.end < b.end ? -1 : a.end > b.end ? 1 : 0; });
  out.list.forEach(function (x, i) { if (!x.name) x.name = "Quarter " + (i + 1); });
  return out;
}

/** "1 = 8:10 AM, 6 = 9:10 AM" -> { times: { "1": 490, "6": 550 } (minutes after midnight), bad: [text] }. */
function parsePeriodTimes_(v) {
  var out = { times: {}, bad: [] };
  String(v == null ? "" : v).split(/[,;\n]+/).forEach(function (part) {
    part = part.trim();
    if (!part) return;
    var m = /^(?:per(?:iod)?\.?\s*|p\.?\s*)?(\w+)\s*(?:=|-|:|at)\s*(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*([ap])?\.?\s*m?\.?$/i.exec(part);
    if (!m) { out.bad.push(part); return; }
    var h = +m[2], min = +(m[3] || 0), ap = (m[4] || "").toLowerCase();
    if (h > 23 || min > 59) { out.bad.push(part); return; }
    if (ap === "p" && h < 12) h += 12;
    else if (ap === "a" && h === 12) h = 0;
    else if (!ap && h >= 1 && h <= 6) h += 12;   // "1:15" with no AM/PM is a school-day afternoon
    out.times[periodKey_(m[1])] = h * 60 + min;
  });
  return out;
}
function periodKey_(p) { var m = /\d+/.exec(String(p == null ? "" : p)); return m ? String(Number(m[0])) : String(p == null ? "" : p).trim().toLowerCase(); }

var WEEKDAYS_ = ["sunday", "monday", "tuesday", "wednesday", "thursday", "friday", "saturday"];

/** "Thursday: 1 = 8:10 AM, 6 = 10:33 AM; 12/17/2026: 1 = 7:45 AM; Q2: 3 = 11:00 AM" ->
    { days: { "4": times }, quarters: { "2": times }, dates: { "2026-12-17": times }, list: [{ kind, key, label, times }], bad: [text] }.
    times = { period: minutes after midnight }. "none" (or blank) = no changes. */
function parseCloseChanges_(v) {
  var out = { days: {}, quarters: {}, dates: {}, list: [], bad: [] };
  if (isDate_(v)) { out.bad.push(Utilities.formatDate(v, tz_(), "M/d/yyyy") + " (no times after it)"); return out; }
  var text = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim();
  if (!text || /^(none|off|no|-)$/i.test(text)) return out;
  text.split(/[;\n]+/).forEach(function (part) {
    part = part.trim();
    if (!part) return;
    // "Thursday: 1 = ...": the day, a colon, then the times
    var m = /^\s*([A-Za-z]+\.?|q(?:uarter)?\s*\d|\d{4}-\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}|\d{1,2}\/\d{1,2}(?:\/\d{2,4})?)\s*:\s*(.+)$/i.exec(part);
    if (!m) { out.bad.push(part); return; }
    var head = m[1].trim().replace(/\.$/, "").toLowerCase(), times = parsePeriodTimes_(m[2]);
    if (times.bad.length || !Object.keys(times.times).length) { out.bad.push(part); return; }
    var q = /^q(?:uarter)?\s*(\d)$/.exec(head), entry = null;
    if (q) entry = { kind: "quarter", key: q[1], label: "Quarter " + q[1] };
    else if (/\d/.test(head)) {
      var d = parseQuarterDates_(head);
      if (d.list.length === 1 && !d.bad.length) entry = { kind: "date", key: d.list[0].end, label: Utilities.formatDate(new Date(atLocal_(d.list[0].end, 720)), tz_(), "EEE M/d/yyyy") };
    } else {
      for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        if (head.length >= 3 && WEEKDAYS_[i].indexOf(head) === 0) { entry = { kind: "day", key: String(i), label: WEEKDAYS_[i].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + WEEKDAYS_[i].slice(1) }; break; }
      }
      if (!entry && head === "thur") entry = { kind: "day", key: "4", label: "Thursday" };
    }
    if (!entry) { out.bad.push(part); return; }
    entry.times = times.times;
    var bucket = entry.kind === "day" ? out.days : entry.kind === "quarter" ? out.quarters : out.dates;
    bucket[entry.key] = bucket[entry.key] || {};
    Object.keys(entry.times).forEach(function (k) { bucket[entry.key][k] = entry.times[k]; });
    out.list.push(entry);
  });
  return out;
}

/** The class start times used on one quarter's last day: the usual times, then the weekday's changes, the quarter's,
    and the date's (a date beats a quarter, which beats a weekday). -> { times, changed: ["Thursday", ...] } */
function timesForDay_(ymd, quarterName) {
  var t = parsePeriodTimes_(CLASS_START_TIMES).times, ch = parseCloseChanges_(CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES), changed = [];
  var out = {}; Object.keys(t).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = t[k]; });
  var wd = String(Number(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(atLocal_(ymd, 720)), tz_(), "u")) % 7);   // 0 = Sunday
  var qm = /(\d)/.exec(String(quarterName || "")), qKey = qm ? qm[1] : "";
  function lay(times, label) { if (!times) return; Object.keys(times).forEach(function (k) { out[k] = times[k]; }); changed.push(label); }
  if (ch.days[wd]) lay(ch.days[wd], WEEKDAYS_[+wd].charAt(0).toUpperCase() + WEEKDAYS_[+wd].slice(1));
  if (qKey && ch.quarters[qKey]) lay(ch.quarters[qKey], "Q" + qKey);
  if (ch.dates[ymd]) lay(ch.dates[ymd], Utilities.formatDate(new Date(atLocal_(ymd, 720)), tz_(), "M/d/yyyy"));
  return { times: out, changed: changed };
}

/** When a period closes on a quarter's last day (ms). Unlisted period = the earliest time that day; none listed = 11:59 PM. */
function closeTimeOf_(ymd, period, quarterName) {
  var t = timesForDay_(ymd, quarterName).times, key = periodKey_(period), all = Object.keys(t).map(function (k) { return t[k]; });
  var mins = key !== "" && t.hasOwnProperty(key) ? t[key] : (all.length ? Math.min.apply(null, all) : 23 * 60 + 59);
  return atLocal_(ymd, mins);
}

function quartersProp_() {
  try { var k = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("QUARTERS") || "{}"); return k && typeof k === "object" ? k : {}; } catch (e) { return {}; }
}

/** One linked class's grading period settings, straight from Classroom. */
function gradingPeriods_(courseId) {
  if (typeof Classroom !== "undefined" && Classroom.Courses && typeof Classroom.Courses.getGradingPeriodSettings === "function") {
    return Classroom.Courses.getGradingPeriodSettings(courseId);
  }
  var r = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://classroom.googleapis.com/v1/courses/" + encodeURIComponent(courseId) + "/gradingPeriodSettings",
    { headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken() }, muteHttpExceptions: true });
  var body = {};
  try { body = JSON.parse(r.getContentText() || "{}"); } catch (e) {}
  if (r.getResponseCode() !== 200) throw new Error((body.error && body.error.message) || ("Classroom answered " + r.getResponseCode()));
  return body;
}

/** Asks Classroom for the grading periods (the first linked class that has them) and remembers them. Never shows pop-ups. */
function fetchQuarters_() {
  var prev = quartersProp_(), out = { list: [], at: Date.now(), err: "" }, list = courses_();
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length && !out.list.length; i++) {
    try {
      var gp = (gradingPeriods_(list[i].id) || {}).gradingPeriods || [];
      out.list = gp.filter(function (p) { return p && p.endDate && p.endDate.year; }).map(function (p) {
        return { end: p.endDate.year + "-" + pad2_(p.endDate.month) + "-" + pad2_(p.endDate.day), name: String(p.title || "").trim() };
      }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.end < b.end ? -1 : a.end > b.end ? 1 : 0; });
      out.list.forEach(function (x, k) { if (!x.name) x.name = "Quarter " + (k + 1); });
      if (!out.list.length) out.err = "no grading periods are set in Classroom for " + list[i].name;
    } catch (e) { out.err = e.message; }
  }
  if (!list.length) out.err = "no classes are linked yet (step 1)";
  if (!out.list.length && prev.list && prev.list.length) out.list = prev.list;   // a Classroom hiccup keeps the last good answer
  props_().setProperty("QUARTERS", JSON.stringify(out));
  return out;
}

/** The quarter end dates in use: { list: [{ end, name }], src: "Settings" | "Classroom" | "", err, bad }. quick = don't ask Classroom
    (except the very first time, when it was never asked). */
function quarterEnds_(quick) {
  var typed = parseQuarterDates_(QUARTER_END_DATES);
  if (typed.list.length || typed.bad.length) return { list: typed.list, src: "Settings", err: "", bad: typed.bad };
  var k = quartersProp_(), age = Date.now() - (Number(k.at) || 0);
  if ((!k.at || (!quick && age > QUARTER_CHECK_SECS * 1000)) && courses_().length) {
    try {
      props_().setProperty("QUARTERS", JSON.stringify({ list: k.list || [], at: Date.now(), err: k.err || "" }));   // take this turn, so a class saving at once asks once
      k = fetchQuarters_();
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return { list: k.list || [], src: k.list && k.list.length ? "Classroom" : "", err: k.err || "", bad: [] };
}

/** The day an assignment counts for (ms): its due date in Classroom, else the day step 2 made it, else 0. */
function assignmentDay_(course, quick) {
  if (!course || !course.cwId) return 0;
  var d = 0;
  try { d = dueOfCourse_(course, quick); } catch (e) {}
  if (d) return d;
  var e2 = dueEntry_(dueDates_()[course.cwId]);
  return e2 && e2.c ? e2.c : 0;
}

/** When one linked class's assignment closes: { at (ms), end ("yyyy-MM-dd"), name ("Quarter 1"), basis (ms) }, or null. */
function closeForCourse_(course, period, quick) {
  if (!CLOSE_AT_QUARTER_END) return null;
  var basis = assignmentDay_(course, quick);
  if (!basis) return null;
  var q = quarterEnds_(quick).list, day = ymdOf_(basis);
  for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {
    if (day <= q[i].end) return { at: closeTimeOf_(q[i].end, period, q[i].name), end: q[i].end, name: q[i].name, basis: basis };
  }
  return null;   // due after the last quarter end date we know of
}

/** When this student's copy closes (their class and period), or null. A student in no linked class gets the earliest close. */
function closeForStudent_(email, roster, quick) {
  if (!CLOSE_AT_QUARTER_END) return null;
  try {
    roster = roster || readRoster_();
    var p = roster[email], course = courseOfStudent_(email, roster);
    if (course) return closeForCourse_(course, p && p.period !== "" && p.period != null ? p.period : course.period, quick);
    var best = null;
    courses_().forEach(function (c) { var x = closeForCourse_(c, "", true); if (x && (!best || x.at < best.at)) best = x; });
    return best;
  } catch (e) { return null; }   // never lock a student out because Classroom or a setting had a problem
}
function isClosed_(close) { return !!close && Date.now() >= close.at; }

/** Student-facing words (no em dashes). */
function closedNote_(close) { return close.name + " ended " + dayLabel_(close.end) + ". You can view your work, but it can't be changed."; }
function closedError_(close) {
  return "This assignment is locked because " + close.name + " ended " + dayLabel_(close.end) +
         ". You can still look at your work, but answers can't be changed or earn credit.";
}
/** A heads-up in the instructions during the last 3 days before closing. */
function closingSoonNote_(close) {
  var left = close.at - Date.now();
  if (left <= 0 || left > 3 * 86400000) return "";
  return "Heads up: this assignment closes when your class starts on " + dayLabel_(close.end) + " (" + timeLabel_(close.at) +
         "), the last day of " + close.name + ".";
}

/** { "1": 490, "6": 550 } -> "period 1 at 8:10 AM, period 6 at 9:10 AM" (earliest first). */
function timesText_(t) {
  return Object.keys(t).sort(function (a, b) { return t[a] - t[b]; }).map(function (k) { return "period " + k + " at " + timeLabel_(atLocal_("2026-01-05", t[k])); }).join(", ");
}

/** Menu: when each period's assignment closes, and where the quarter dates came from. */
function showQuarterCloses() {
  menuOnly_();
  clearConfigCache_();
  loadConfig_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  if (!CLOSE_AT_QUARTER_END) {
    ui.alert("Closing at the end of the quarter is OFF (Settings tab: Close assignments when the quarter ends). Students can work on this assignment any time.");
    return;
  }
  var typed = parseQuarterDates_(QUARTER_END_DATES);
  if (!typed.list.length && !typed.bad.length && courses_().length) { try { requireClassroom_(); fetchQuarters_(); } catch (e) {} }
  var q = quarterEnds_(true), times = parsePeriodTimes_(CLASS_START_TIMES), fmt = function (ms) { return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(ms), tz_(), "EEE M/d"); };
  var out = [];
  if (q.list.length) {
    out.push("Quarter end dates (" + (q.src === "Settings" ? "typed in the Settings tab" : "from Google Classroom's grading periods") + "):");
    q.list.forEach(function (x) {
      var d = timesForDay_(x.end, x.name);
      out.push("  " + x.name + ": last day " + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(atLocal_(x.end, 720)), tz_(), "EEE M/d/yyyy") +
               (d.changed.length ? " (" + d.changed.join(" + ") + " times: " + timesText_(d.times) + ")" : " (usual times)"));
    });
  } else {
    out.push("No quarter end dates yet, so this assignment never closes." +
      (q.err ? "\nGoogle Classroom didn't share its grading periods (" + q.err + ")." : "") +
      "\nType the last day of each quarter in the Settings tab (Quarter end dates), for example:\n  10/16/2026, 12/18/2026, 3/12/2027, 5/28/2027");
  }
  if (q.bad && q.bad.length) out.push("Couldn't read these quarter dates: " + q.bad.join(", ") + " (use month/day/year, like 10/16/2026).");
  var keys = Object.keys(times.times).sort(function (a, b) { return times.times[a] - times.times[b]; });
  out.push("");
  out.push(keys.length ? "On a quarter's last day, each period closes when its class starts. Usual times: " + timesText_(times.times) +
    ". Periods not listed close at the earliest time that day." : "No class start times in Settings, so every period closes at 11:59 PM on a quarter's last day.");
  if (times.bad.length) out.push("Couldn't read these class start times: " + times.bad.join(", ") + " (use period = time, like 1 = 8:10 AM).");
  var chg = parseCloseChanges_(CLOSE_TIME_CHANGES);
  chg.list.forEach(function (c) { out.push("Different times on " + (c.kind === "quarter" ? c.label + "'s last day" : c.kind === "day" ? "a " + c.label + " last day" : c.label) + ": " + timesText_(c.times) + " (other periods keep their usual time)."); });
  if (chg.bad.length) out.push("Couldn't read these different start times: " + chg.bad.join("; ") + " (use a day, a colon, then period = time, like Thursday: 1 = 8:10 AM, 6 = 10:33 AM).");
  out.push("");
  var list = courses_();
  if (!list.length) out.push("No classes linked yet (step 1).");
  list.forEach(function (c) {
    var line = "Period " + (c.period === "" ? "?" : c.period) + ": ";
    if (!c.cwId) { out.push(line + "the assignment isn't created yet (step 2)."); return; }
    var x = closeForCourse_(c, c.period, false);
    if (!x) {
      var basis = assignmentDay_(c, true);
      out.push(line + (!basis ? "no due date in Classroom yet, so it can't tell which quarter this belongs to."
                              : q.list.length ? "due " + fmt(basis) + ", after the last quarter end date listed, so it won't close." : "won't close (no quarter end dates)."));
      return;
    }
    var e = dueEntry_(dueDates_()[c.cwId]), byDue = e && e.d;
    out.push(line + (isClosed_(x) ? "CLOSED since " : "closes ") + Utilities.formatDate(new Date(x.at), tz_(), "EEE M/d h:mm a") +
             " (" + x.name + ", " + (byDue ? "due " : "no due date, created ") + fmt(x.basis) + ").");
  });
  ui.alert("Quarter close times", out.join("\n"), ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}

// ============================== STORAGE ==============================
var SS_ = null, SS_ID_ = null;   // the spreadsheet, opened once per run (opening it is slow, and a save used to open it 3 or 4 times)
function openSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("SSID") || "";
  if (SS_ && SS_ID_ === id) return SS_;
  SS_ = id ? SpreadsheetApp.openById(id) : SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  SS_ID_ = id;
  return SS_;
}
function ss_() { loadConfig_(); return openSS_(); }
function sheet_(name) {
  var s = ss_().getSheetByName(name);
  if (!s) { ensureSheets_(); s = ss_().getSheetByName(name); }
  return s;
}
function newRecord_() {
  var a = [], s = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) { a.push(""); s.push("blank"); }
  return { answers: a, statuses: s, checks: 0, updated: "", turnedIn: "" };
}
/** Row of a student in a tab, remembered so we don't scan the whole column on every save.
 *  The remembered row is double-checked, so sorting the tab can't send data to the wrong row. */
function rowOf_(sh, col, key) {
  var ck = "row:" + sh.getName() + ":" + key, c = cache_(), r = 0;
  try { r = c ? Number(c.get(ck)) || 0 : 0; } catch (e) { r = 0; }
  if (r >= 2 && r <= sh.getLastRow() && String(sh.getRange(r, col).getValue()).toLowerCase() === key) return r;
  r = findRow_(sh, col, key);
  if (r && c) try { c.put(ck, String(r), 21600); } catch (e) {}
  return r;
}

function findRow_(sh, col, key) {
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last < 2) return 0;
  var vals = sh.getRange(2, col, last - 1, 1).getValues();
  for (var i = 0; i < vals.length; i++) if (String(vals[i][0]).toLowerCase() === key) return i + 2;
  return 0;
}
var REC_ROW_ = {};   // email -> the _data row readRecord_ just found, so writeRecord_ (same run, inside the lock) needn't look again
function readRecord_(email) {
  var sh = sheet_(SH_STATE), ck = "row:" + sh.getName() + ":" + email, c = cache_(), r = 0, v = null;
  try { r = c ? Number(c.get(ck)) || 0 : 0; } catch (e) { r = 0; }
  if (r >= 2) {   // the remembered row: its email and record in ONE read (the email is checked, so sorting the tab is still safe)
    try { v = sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).getValues()[0]; } catch (e) { v = null; }
    if (v && String(v[0]).toLowerCase() !== email) v = null;
  }
  if (!v) {
    r = rowOf_(sh, 1, email);
    if (!r) { delete REC_ROW_[email]; return null; }
    v = sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).getValues()[0];
  }
  REC_ROW_[email] = r;
  return padRecord_(JSON.parse(v[1]));
}
function padRecord_(rec) {
  while (rec.answers.length < ITEMS.length) { rec.answers.push(""); rec.statuses.push("blank"); }
  return rec;
}
function writeRecord_(email, rec) {
  var sh = sheet_(SH_STATE), r = REC_ROW_[email] || rowOf_(sh, 1, email), json = JSON.stringify(rec);
  if (!r) r = sh.getLastRow() + 1;
  sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, 2).setValues([[email, json]]);
  REC_ROW_[email] = r;
  keepRecordCopy_(email, json);
}
// A copy of each student's saved work in the short-term cache ("rec:<email>", 6 hours), so opening the page again
// (getStudentState) needn't open the spreadsheet. Only writeRecord_ puts it there, always inside the save line, so the copy is
// never older than the _data tab. Saves still read the _data tab itself. The teacher editing a _data row by hand drops that
// student's copy (onEdit); Start a new school year drops them all.
var REC_CACHE_SECS = 21600;
function recKey_(email) { return "rec:" + email; }
function keepRecordCopy_(email, json) {
  var c = cache_();
  if (!c) return;
  try { if (json.length < 95000) c.put(recKey_(email), json, REC_CACHE_SECS); else c.remove(recKey_(email)); }
  catch (e) { try { c.remove(recKey_(email)); } catch (e2) {} }
}
/** The student's saved work for showing it: the short-term copy if there is one, else the _data tab. Never for a save. */
function readRecordQuick_(email) {
  var raw = cacheRaw_(recKey_(email));
  if (raw) try { return padRecord_(JSON.parse(raw)); } catch (e) {}
  return readRecord_(email);
}
function allRecords_() {
  var sh = sheet_(SH_STATE), last = sh.getLastRow(), out = {};
  if (last < 2) return out;
  sh.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function (row) {
    if (row[0]) out[String(row[0]).toLowerCase()] = JSON.parse(row[1]);
  });
  return out;
}
function readRoster_() {
  var hit = cacheGet_("roster");
  if (hit) return hit;
  var out = readRosterFromSheet_();
  cachePut_("roster", out, ROSTER_CACHE_SECS);
  return out;
}
function readRosterFromSheet_() {
  var sh = ss_().getSheetByName(SH_ROSTER), out = {};
  if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, ROSTER_HEADERS.length).getValues().forEach(function (row) {
    // Period | Name | Email | Classroom user ID | Course ID
    if (row[2]) out[String(row[2]).toLowerCase()] = { period: row[0], name: row[1], userId: String(row[3]), courseId: String(row[4]) };
  });
  return out;
}
/** Text a student typed, made safe for a cell: a leading = + - or @ would make Sheets run it as a formula. */
function safeCell_(s) { s = String(s == null ? "" : s); return /^[=+\-@]/.test(s) ? "'" + s : s; }
function logEvent_(email, idx, status, answer) {
  var p = readRoster_()[email] || {};
  sheet_(SH_LOG).appendRow([new Date(), p.period === undefined ? "" : p.period, p.name || email, email,
                            "Q" + (idx + 1), status, safeCell_(answer)]);
}

// ============================== TEACHER VIEW ==============================
function liveRowData_(email, rec, p, due) {
  p = p || {};
  var info = lateGrade_(rec, due);
  var c = counts_(rec.statuses, workSet_(rec));   // (a review game: out of the try's own questions)
  var drafts = rec.drafts || [];
  var anyDraft = drafts.some(function (d) { return d != null; });
  var status = rec.turnedIn ? "Turned in" : ((rec.checks || anyDraft) ? "In progress" : "Not started");
  if (isGame_() && rec.game) {   // a review game: how far this try is, and the best try when they started over
    var g = rec.game, now = trySummary_(rec.statuses, rec.answers, g.set), best = rec.best ? trySummary_(rec.best.statuses, rec.best.answers, rec.best.set) : null;
    status = (g.attempt > 1 ? "Try " + g.attempt + ": " : "") + (gameDone_(rec) ? "finished, " + now.pct + "%" : g.answered + " of " + now.total + " answered") +
             (best ? " (best try " + best.pct + "%)" : "");
  }
  var row = [p.period === undefined ? "" : p.period, p.name || email.split("@")[0], email, status,
             rec.updated ? new Date(rec.updated) : "", rec.checks ? info.grade : "",
             c.correct, c.partial, c.incorrect, c.blank, rec.checks || 0];
  var bg = row.map(function () { return null; });
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) {
    var d = drafts[i];
    // An autosaved draft that hasn't been checked yet is shown with a prefix; its color is still the last check.
    var shown = d != null && d !== rec.answers[i] ? d : (rec.answers[i] || "");
    if (ITEMS[i].table) shown = tableText_(ITEMS[i], shown);
    if (isTried_(ITEMS[i])) { row.push(safeCell_(triedCellText_(i, rec))); bg.push(FILL[rec.statuses[i] || "blank"]); continue; }   // the latest try, how it went, and the hints opened
    if (ITEMS[i].choices) shown = choiceText_(ITEMS[i], shown);
    row.push(d != null && d !== rec.answers[i] ? "[not checked] " + shown : safeCell_(shown));
    bg.push(FILL[rec.statuses[i] || "blank"]);
  }
  bg[3] = rec.turnedIn ? "#c9daf8" : null;
  if (info.late) bg[5] = "#fce5cd";   // Grade cell turns light orange when the late penalty applies (hover for the note)
  return { row: row, bg: bg, note: lateCellNote_(info) };
}

function renderLiveRow_(email, rec, roster, due) {
  var sh = sheet_(SH_LIVE);
  roster = roster || readRoster_();
  if (due === undefined) due = dueForStudent_(email, roster);
  var r = rowOf_(sh, LIVE_EMAIL_COL, email);
  if (!r) r = sh.getLastRow() + 1;
  var d = liveRowData_(email, rec, roster[email], due);
  sh.getRange(r, 1, 1, d.row.length).setValues([d.row]).setBackgrounds([d.bg]);
  if (d.note || rec.onTime) sh.getRange(r, 6).setNote(d.note);
}

/** Wipe and redraw every Live Results row (roster students + anyone who has started). */
function rebuildLive_() {
  var sh = sheet_(SH_LIVE), recs = allRecords_(), roster = readRoster_();
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) {
    sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, sh.getLastColumn()).clearContent().setBackground(null);
  }
  var emails = Object.keys(roster);
  Object.keys(recs).forEach(function (e) { if (!roster[e]) emails.push(e); });
  emails.sort(function (a, b) {
    var pa = roster[a] ? Number(roster[a].period) || 999 : 1000, pb = roster[b] ? Number(roster[b].period) || 999 : 1000;
    if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb;
    return String(roster[a] ? roster[a].name : a).localeCompare(String(roster[b] ? roster[b].name : b));
  });
  if (!emails.length) return;
  var rows = [], bgs = [], notes = [];
  emails.forEach(function (e) {
    var d = liveRowData_(e, recs[e] || newRecord_(), roster[e], recs[e] && recs[e].onTime ? dueForStudent_(e, roster) : 0);
    rows.push(d.row); bgs.push(d.bg); notes.push([d.note]);
  });
  sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, rows[0].length).setValues(rows).setBackgrounds(bgs);
  sh.getRange(2, 6, notes.length, 1).setNotes(notes);
}

function renderSummary_() {
  var recs = allRecords_(), roster = readRoster_(), sh = sheet_(SH_SUMMARY), rows = [];
  var started = Object.keys(recs).filter(function (k) { return recs[k].checks; });
  var periods = periodList_();
  var game = isGame_(), inTry = {};   // a review game: each student's try has only its own questions (QUESTIONS PER TRY)
  if (game) started.forEach(function (k) { inTry[k] = setMap_(workSet_(recs[k])); });
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) {
    var c = { correct: 0, partial: 0, incorrect: 0, blank: 0 }, byP = {}, given = 0;
    started.forEach(function (k) {
      if (game && !inTry[k][i]) return;   // not in this student's try: not a blank
      given++;
      var s = recs[k].statuses[i] || "blank";
      c[s]++;
      var p = roster[k] ? String(roster[k].period) : "";
      byP[p] = byP[p] || { g: 0, n: 0 };
      if (s !== "blank") { byP[p].n++; if (s === "correct") byP[p].g++; }
    });
    var answered = c.correct + c.partial + c.incorrect, first = "";
    if (isTried_(ITEMS[i])) {   // limited tries: of the students who tried it, how many got it right on the first try
      var tried = 0, firstOk = 0;
      started.forEach(function (k) { var st = tryState_(i, recs[k].tries ? recs[k].tries[i] : null); if (st.used) { tried++; if (st.rightTry === 1) firstOk++; } });
      first = tried ? firstOk / tried : "";
    }
    var row = ["Q" + (i + 1), ITEMS[i].q.slice(0, 90) + "…", c.correct, c.partial, c.incorrect, c.blank,
               answered ? c.correct / answered : "", first];
    if (game) row.splice(6, 0, given);   // Times given: how many students' tries have this question
    periods.forEach(function (p) { var b = byP[String(p)]; row.push(b && b.n ? b.g / b.n : ""); });
    rows.push(row);
  }
  var headers = summaryHeads_().concat(periods.map(function (p) { return "P" + p + " % green"; })), pctFrom = game ? 8 : 7;
  sh.clearContents();
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]).setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8");
  if (!rows.length) return;
  sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, headers.length).setValues(rows);
  if (game) sh.getRange(2, 7, rows.length, 1).setNumberFormat("0");
  sh.getRange(2, pctFrom, rows.length, headers.length - pctFrom + 1).setNumberFormat("0%");
}

// ============================== TEACHER MENU ==============================
/**
 * What this sheet's tabs are missing compared with this version of the code: Settings rows and Questions columns
 * that "Set up / repair sheets" adds. Pasting new code doesn't change the tabs, so a sheet can be on the newest code
 * and still be missing settings until repair runs. -> { settings: [labels], columns: [labels] }
 */
function missingPieces_(ss) {
  ss = ss || SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var out = { settings: [], columns: [] };
  var st = ss.getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS);
  if (st && st.getLastRow() > 1) {
    var have = {};
    st.getRange(2, 1, st.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (r) { have[String(r[0]).trim().toLowerCase()] = true; });
    SETTING_DEFS.forEach(function (d) { if (!have[d[0].toLowerCase()]) out.settings.push(d[0]); });
  } else out.settings = SETTING_DEFS.map(function (d) { return d[0]; });
  var qs = ss.getSheetByName(SH_QUESTIONS);
  if (qs && qs.getLastColumn()) {
    var heads = qs.getRange(1, 1, 1, qs.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0].map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); });
    if (isGame_()) {   // a review game's tab has only the multiple choice columns (seedGameQuestions_)
      [[/^(choice|option) 1\b/, "Choice 1, Choice 2 ..."], [/^right (choice|answer)/, "Right choice"], [/^explanation/, "Explanation"], [/^points\b/, "Points"]].forEach(function (p) {
        if (!heads.some(function (h) { return p[0].test(h); })) out.columns.push(p[1]);
      });
      return out;
    }
    [Q_COLS.boxes, Q_COLS.type, Q_COLS.table, Q_COLS.sentences, Q_COLS.short, Q_COLS.points].forEach(function (label) {
      var start = label.toLowerCase().split(" (")[0];
      if (!heads.some(function (h) { return h.indexOf(start) === 0; })) out.columns.push(label.split(" (")[0]);
    });
    if (!heads.some(function (h) { return /^misconception \d+ (hint|key words)$/.test(h); })) out.columns.push("Misconception hint and key words");
    if (!heads.some(function (h) { return /^section picture(?! caption)/.test(h); })) out.columns.push("Section picture");
    if (!heads.some(function (h) { return /^section picture caption/.test(h); })) out.columns.push("Section picture caption");
    [[/^(choice|option) 1\b(?!\s*feedback)/, "Choice 1, Choice 1 feedback ..."], [/^right (choice|answer)/, "Right choice"], [/^explanation/, "Explanation"], [/^tries\b/, "Tries"],
     [/^hint:?\s*clue/, "Hint: clue"], [/^hint:?\s*example/, "Hint: example"], [/^hint:?\s*(video|link)/, "Hint: video or link"]].forEach(function (p) {   // LIMITED TRIES
      if (triesPage_() && !heads.some(function (h) { return p[0].test(h); })) out.columns.push(p[1]);
    });
  }
  return out;
}
function missingText_(m) {
  return (m.settings.length ? "Settings tab is missing " + m.settings.length + " setting(s):\n  " + m.settings.join("\n  ") : "") +
         (m.settings.length && m.columns.length ? "\n\n" : "") +
         (m.columns.length ? "Questions tab is missing " + m.columns.length + " column(s):\n  " + m.columns.join("\n  ") : "");
}

function onOpen() {
  if (wrongKind_()) return wrongKindMenu_("Self-Check");
  buildMenu_();
  try {   // new code pasted but Set up / repair sheets not run yet: say so
    var m = missingPieces_();
    if (m.settings.length || m.columns.length) SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().toast("This sheet is missing " +
      (m.settings.length + m.columns.length) + " newer option(s), like " + (m.settings[0] || m.columns[0]) +
      ". Run Self-Check > Set up / repair sheets to add them.", "Self-Check", 15);
  } catch (e) { /* never block opening the sheet */ }
}

/** The Self-Check menu. Built again after the linked classes change, so step 3 lists each class (uploadMenus_). */
function buildMenu_() {
  if (isGame_()) return gameMenu_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var menu = ui.createMenu("Self-Check")
    .addItem("Set up / repair sheets", "setupSheets")
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("1. Link Google Classroom classes (checkboxes)", "linkClassroom")
    .addItem("2. Create the assignment in each class (draft)", "createClassroomAssignment");
  uploadMenus_(ui, menu)
    .addSubMenu(SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu("Automatic grades after the due date")
      .addItem("Turn on", "autoGradesOn")
      .addItem("Show status", "autoGradesStatus")
      .addItem("Turn off", "autoGradesOff"))
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("Show linked classes", "showLinkedClasses")
    .addItem("Show quarter close times", "showQuarterCloses")
    .addItem("Refresh roster from Classroom", "refreshRoster")
    .addItem("Recalculate all grades (after editing the rubric)", "recalculateAll")
    .addItem("Show student link", "showStudentLink")
    .addItem("Test an answer against the key", "testAnswer")
    .addItem("Check the Questions tab for problems", "checkQuestionsTab")
    .addItem("Update the article from the Google Doc", "updateArticleFromDoc")
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("Check for skill updates", "checkForSkillUpdates")
    .addItem("Start a new school year (clear student data)", "resetForNewYear")
    .addToUi();
}

/** A review game's menu: the same steps, without what only typed answers or reading pages use, plus the website leaderboard. */
function gameMenu_() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var menu = ui.createMenu("Self-Check")
    .addItem("Set up / repair sheets", "setupSheets")
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("1. Link Google Classroom classes (checkboxes)", "linkClassroom")
    .addItem("2. Create the assignment in each class (draft)", "createClassroomAssignment");
  uploadMenus_(ui, menu)
    .addSubMenu(SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu("Automatic grades after the due date")
      .addItem("Turn on", "autoGradesOn")
      .addItem("Show status", "autoGradesStatus")
      .addItem("Turn off", "autoGradesOff"))
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("Show linked classes", "showLinkedClasses")
    .addItem("Show quarter close times", "showQuarterCloses")
    .addItem("Refresh roster from Classroom", "refreshRoster")
    .addItem("Recalculate all grades (after changing a Right choice)", "recalculateAll")
    .addItem("Show student link", "showStudentLink")
    .addItem("Check the Questions tab for problems", "checkQuestionsTab")
    .addItem("Send game points to the website leaderboard now", "sendPointsToWebsite")
    .addSeparator()
    .addItem("Check for skill updates", "checkForSkillUpdates")
    .addItem("Start a new school year (clear student data)", "resetForNewYear")
    .addToUi();
}

/** Builds the menu again (after the linked classes change), so step 3 lists the right classes. Never fails the caller. */
function refreshMenu_() {
  try { if (!wrongKind_()) buildMenu_(); } catch (e) { /* the menu catches up the next time the sheet opens */ }
}

/** Step 3 and 3b as small menus: "All classes" first, then one item per linked period, so one class can be uploaded alone.
 *  A menu item can only name a function, so each period number 0 to MENU_PERIODS_ has its own (uploadDraftP3, uploadReturnP3 ...). */
var MENU_PERIODS_ = 12;
function uploadMenus_(ui, menu) {
  var draft = ui.createMenu("3. Upload grades to Classroom (draft grades)").addItem("All classes", "uploadDraftGrades");
  var back = ui.createMenu("3b. Upload grades AND return them to students").addItem("All classes", "uploadAndReturnGrades");
  var by = {};
  try {
    courses_().forEach(function (c) {
      var p = String(c.period).trim();
      if (!/^\d{1,2}$/.test(p) || Number(p) > MENU_PERIODS_) return;
      (by[Number(p)] = by[Number(p)] || []).push(c.name);
    });
  } catch (e) { /* only All classes */ }
  Object.keys(by).map(Number).sort(function (a, b) { return a - b; }).forEach(function (p) {
    var label = "Period " + p + " only (" + by[p].join(", ") + ")";
    draft.addItem(label, "uploadDraftP" + p);
    back.addItem(label, "uploadReturnP" + p);
  });
  return menu.addSubMenu(draft).addSubMenu(back);
}

/** In a copy of the spreadsheet (File > Make a copy), the script's saved student link is still the ORIGINAL's. Forget it,
 *  so Show student link and step 2 ask for this copy's own /exec link (and Class Pass checks sign-ins against it). */
function forgetCopiedLink_() {
  var was = props_().getProperty("SSID");
  if (was && was !== SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getId()) { props_().deleteProperty("WEBAPP_URL"); props_().deleteProperty("UPLOAD_FOLDER"); }
}

/** First line of every menu step: throws when called from the web app, before any work, and stops when this code is for another kind of
    sheet (see PASTE SAFETY). */
function menuOnly_() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  if (wrongKind_()) { ui.alert("Wrong code for this sheet", wrongKindText_(), ui.ButtonSet.OK); throw new Error("Stopped: this code is for " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + "."); }
}

// ============================== PASTE SAFETY (2026.10.30c) ==============================
// The first Set up / repair sheets remembers what kind of sheet this is (script property SHEET_KIND = SKILL_NAME; Video Check uses the same
// property and names). If code for another kind is pasted in later (a Lab's code into a Review Game sheet, say), the menu shows only a warning,
// and Set up, every other menu step, the hourly grade upload and the student page refuse to run until the right code is pasted back, or the
// teacher switches the sheet's kind on purpose (Make this sheet ..., e.g. a one-column sheet becoming a two-column reading page).
// A sheet that never ran Set up on this code has no SHEET_KIND yet, so nothing is checked (older sheets work as before).
var KIND_NAMES_ = { "auto-grading-activity": "Self-Check (one column)", "reading-activity": "Reading page (two columns)", "friction-article": "Friction Article",
  "friction-speed-lab": "Friction and Speed Lab", "friction-sim-lab": "Friction Simulation Lab", "review-game": "Review Game",
  "net-force-escape-room": "Escape Room", "video-check": "Video Check" };
function kindName_(k) { return KIND_NAMES_[k] || String(k || "an unknown kind"); }
function aKind_(k) { var n = kindName_(k); return (/^[AEIOU]/i.test(n) ? "an " : "a ") + n; }
var SHEET_KIND_;   // this run's copy of SHEET_KIND
function sheetKind_() {
  if (SHEET_KIND_ === undefined) { try { SHEET_KIND_ = String(props_().getProperty("SHEET_KIND") || ""); } catch (e) { SHEET_KIND_ = ""; } }
  return SHEET_KIND_;
}
function wrongKind_() { var k = sheetKind_(); return !!k && k !== SKILL_NAME; }
function wrongKindText_() {
  return "This sheet is " + aKind_(sheetKind_()) + ", but this code is for " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + ". Undo the paste.\n\n" +
    "Extensions > Apps Script: in the file you pasted into, press Ctrl+Z until the old code is back, then Save. If you already made a New version, " +
    "make another one (Deploy > Manage deployments > pencil > New version > Deploy). Until then the menu, the hourly grade upload and the student page stay stopped.\n\n" +
    "Meant to change what kind of sheet this is? Use \"Make this sheet " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + "\" in this menu.";
}
/** The menu while the code doesn't match the sheet: only the warning and the deliberate switch. */
function wrongKindMenu_(menuName) {
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().createMenu(menuName)
    .addItem("⚠ Wrong code pasted: read this", "showWrongKind")
    .addItem("Make this sheet " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME), "makeSheetThisKind")
    .addToUi();
  try { SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().toast("This sheet is " + aKind_(sheetKind_()) + ", but this code is for " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + ". Undo the paste.", "Wrong code", 30); } catch (e) {}
}
/** Menu (wrong code): what happened and how to fix it. */
function showWrongKind() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  ui.alert("Wrong code for this sheet", wrongKind_() ? wrongKindText_() : "This code matches this sheet (" + kindName_(SKILL_NAME) + "). Reload the sheet.", ui.ButtonSet.OK);
}
/** Menu (wrong code): the teacher means it. This sheet becomes the code's kind; then Set up / repair sheets as usual. */
function makeSheetThisKind() {
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  if (!wrongKind_()) { ui.alert("This sheet is already " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + ". Reload the sheet."); return; }
  var ok = ui.alert("Make this sheet " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + "?",
    "Only if you meant to change what kind of assignment this is (for example, a one-column Self-Check becoming a two-column reading page).\n\n" +
    "It is " + aKind_(sheetKind_()) + " now. Its questions, settings and student work stay. If you pasted the wrong code by accident, choose No and undo the paste instead.",
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
  if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  props_().setProperty("SHEET_KIND", SKILL_NAME);
  SHEET_KIND_ = SKILL_NAME;
  ui.alert("This sheet is now " + aKind_(SKILL_NAME) + ". Reload the sheet, run Set up / repair sheets, and make a New version (Deploy > Manage deployments > pencil > New version > Deploy).");
}

function setupSheets() {
  menuOnly_();
  clearConfigCache_();
  forgetCopiedLink_();
  props_().setProperty("SSID", SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getId());
  if (!sheetKind_()) { props_().setProperty("SHEET_KIND", SKILL_NAME); SHEET_KIND_ = SKILL_NAME; }   // PASTE SAFETY: remembered from now on
  var added = missingPieces_();
  CONFIG_LOADED_ = true;            // use the starter values while creating new tabs
  seedConfigTabs_();
  CONFIG_LOADED_ = false; CONFIG_WARNINGS_ = [];
  loadConfig_();                    // then read back whatever is in the tabs
  ensureSheets_();
  rebuildLive_();
  renderSummary_();
  if (courses_().length && !parseQuarterDates_(QUARTER_END_DATES).list.length) { try { fetchQuarters_(); } catch (e) {} }   // quarter end dates from Classroom
  try { ensureTimer_(); } catch (e) {}   // the quiet hourly check (automatic grades + Self-Check Hub status)
  writeHubStatus_();
  var picNote = "";
  if (hasUploads_()) { try { picNote = "Student pictures (upload questions) are saved privately in the Drive folder \"" + uploadFolder_().getName() + "\".\n\n"; } catch (e) {} }
  var addNote = (added.settings.length && added.settings.length < SETTING_DEFS.length) || added.columns.length
    ? "Added to this sheet (check their values):\n" + missingText_(added) + "\n\n" : "";
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(addNote + picNote + "Sheets are ready. Edit questions, answer keys, pictures and simulation buttons in the Questions tab, and grading options in the Settings tab. No redeploy needed.\n\n" +
    "Automatic grades are " + (AUTO_GRADES ? "ON" : "OFF") + " (Settings tab: Automatic grades after the due date, or the Self-Check Hub).\n\n" +
    "If you haven't yet: Deploy > New deployment > Web app (Execute as: Me, Who has access: Anyone), then use step 1 in this menu.");
}

function ensureSheets_() {
  var ss = ss_();
  // Creates the sheet if needed and (re)writes its header row if it's out of date.
  var fresh = {};
  function mk(name, headers, widths) {
    var s = ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name);
    var cur = s.getLastColumn() ? s.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0] : [];
    if (cur.join("|") !== headers.join("|")) {
      if (s.getFilter()) s.getFilter().remove();
      s.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(headers.length, s.getLastColumn() || 1)).clearContent();
      s.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers])
        .setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setFontFamily("Poppins");
      s.setFrozenRows(1);
      (widths || []).forEach(function (w, i) { if (w) s.setColumnWidth(i + 1, w); });
      fresh[name] = true;
    }
    return s;
  }
  var qh = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) qh.push("Q" + (i + 1));
  var live = mk(SH_LIVE, LIVE_FIXED.concat(qh), [60, 160, 200, 95, 130, 80, 55, 55, 55, 55, 60]);
  if (fresh[SH_LIVE]) {
    live.setFrozenColumns(2);
    live.getRange(1, FIRST_Q_COL, 1, ITEMS.length).setNotes([ITEMS.map(function (it) { return it.q; })]);
    for (var j = 0; j < ITEMS.length; j++) live.setColumnWidth(FIRST_Q_COL + j, 220);
    live.getRange("E:E").setNumberFormat("m/d h:mm am/pm");
    live.getRange(2, FIRST_Q_COL, live.getMaxRows() - 1, ITEMS.length).setWrap(false);
  }
  // Sort/filter arrows on every column (Period, Student, Grade, ...).
  if (!live.getFilter()) live.getRange(1, 1, live.getMaxRows(), LIVE_FIXED.length + ITEMS.length).createFilter();
  mk(SH_SUMMARY, summaryHeads_(), isGame_() ? [80, 420, 60, 60, 60, 60, 90, 150, 170] : [80, 420, 60, 60, 60, 60, 150, 170]);
  mk(SH_ROSTER, ROSTER_HEADERS, [60, 180, 240, 180, 140]);
  mk(SH_LOG, ["Time", "Period", "Student", "Email", "Question", "Color", "Answer"], [140, 60, 160, 200, 70, 80, 600]);
  mk(SH_STATE, ["Email", "Record (JSON)"]).hideSheet();
  try { ss.setActiveSheet(live); ss.moveActiveSheet(1); } catch (e) { /* not possible from the web app; harmless */ }
  var def = ss.getSheetByName("Sheet1");
  if (def && ss.getSheets().length > 1 && def.getLastRow() === 0) ss.deleteSheet(def);
}

function props_() { return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(); }

/** Friendly message if the Google Classroom API service hasn't been added yet. */
function requireClassroom_() {
  if (typeof Classroom === "undefined") {
    throw new Error("The Google Classroom API service isn't turned on for this script yet. In Apps Script, click Services (+) in the left sidebar, choose Google Classroom API, leave the Identifier as  Classroom  (capital C), and click Add. Then save and try again.");
  }
}

// ---------- Linked classes: [{id, name, period, cwId}] ----------
function courses_() {
  var raw = props_().getProperty("COURSES");
  if (raw) return JSON.parse(raw);
  var old = props_().getProperty("COURSE_ID");   // upgrade from the one-class version
  if (old) return [{ id: old, name: props_().getProperty("COURSE_NAME") || "", period: "", cwId: props_().getProperty("COURSEWORK_ID") || "" }];
  return [];
}
function saveCourses_(list) {
  props_().setProperty("COURSES", JSON.stringify(list));
  ["COURSE_ID", "COURSE_NAME", "COURSEWORK_ID"].forEach(function (k) { props_().deleteProperty(k); });
}
function periodList_() {
  return courses_().map(function (c) { return c.period; }).filter(function (p) { return p !== ""; })
    .sort(function (a, b) { return (Number(a) || 0) - (Number(b) || 0); });
}
/** Guess a period number from the class name/section: the number in front ("6 - Science", "3 Science"), else "Science - Period 3", "P4", "Per. 2". */
function guessPeriod_(c) {
  var front = /^\s*(\d{1,2})(?=\s|[-:.)|]|$)/.exec(c.name || "");
  if (front) return front[1];
  var t = (c.section || "") + " " + (c.name || "");
  var m = /(?:period|per\.?|pd\.?|\bp)\s*#?\s*(\d{1,2})\b/i.exec(t) || /\b(\d{1,2})(?:st|nd|rd|th)?\s*(?:period|per|pd)\b/i.exec(t) || /\b(\d)\b/.exec(t);
  return m ? m[1] : "";
}

// ---------- Step 1: tick the classes in the Classes tab (2026.11.10c) ----------
// Step 1 lists every active Classroom class in the Classes tab, each with a checkbox (the linked ones ticked). A tick, an untick or a
// typed period is saved at once by onEdit (a simple trigger, so it only reads the tab and saves the list: no Classroom calls there);
// step 2 then loads the rosters and makes the assignment. The period is the number in front of the class name ("6 - Science" is 6).
// No dialog with checkboxes: with more than one Google account signed in to the browser, a dialog's google.script.run fails.
var CLASS_HEAD_ROW = 2;   // row 1 says what to do, row 2 has the headings, the classes start on row 3
var CLASS_HEADS = ["Link", "Period", "Class", "Status", "Classroom ID"];

function courseLabel_(c) { return c.name + (c.section ? " (" + c.section + ")" : ""); }
function cleanPeriod_(v) { var p = String(v == null ? "" : v).trim(); return /^\d+$/.test(p) ? Number(p) : p; }
function periodNum_(p) { return p === "" || p == null || isNaN(Number(p)) ? 99 : Number(p); }
function byPeriod_(a, b) { return (periodNum_(a.period) - periodNum_(b.period)) || String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name)); }
/** Classes unticked in the Classes tab that had the assignment: { courseId: { cwId, name, period } }, so ticking one again finds it. */
function coursesOff_() { try { return JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("COURSES_OFF") || "{}"); } catch (e) { return {}; } }
/** "Period 3", or "Periods 1, 3 and 6". A class with no period number shows as ?. */
function periodsText_(list) {
  var p = list.map(function (x) { return x === "" ? "?" : String(x); });
  return p.length === 1 ? "Period " + p[0] : "Periods " + p.slice(0, -1).join(", ") + " and " + p[p.length - 1];
}

function linkClassroom() {
  menuOnly_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var all = [], tok;
  do {
    var resp = Classroom.Courses.list({ teacherId: "me", courseStates: ["ACTIVE"], pageToken: tok });
    all = all.concat(resp.courses || []);
    tok = resp.nextPageToken;
  } while (tok);
  var ss = ss_();
  applyClassesTab_(ss);   // ticks already in the tab count, even if onEdit couldn't save them
  var linked = courses_(), on = {}, active = {}, rows = [];
  linked.forEach(function (c) { on[String(c.id)] = c; });
  all.forEach(function (c) {
    var was = on[String(c.id)];
    active[String(c.id)] = true;
    rows.push([!!was, was ? was.period : guessPeriod_(c), courseLabel_(c), "", String(c.id)]);
  });
  linked.forEach(function (c) {   // linked, but no longer an active class (archived): listed too, so it can be unticked
    if (!active[String(c.id)]) rows.push([true, c.period, c.name, "", String(c.id)]);
  });
  if (!rows.length) { ui.alert("No active Classroom classes found where you're a teacher."); return; }
  rows.sort(function (a, b) { return (periodNum_(cleanPeriod_(a[1])) - periodNum_(cleanPeriod_(b[1]))) || String(a[2]).localeCompare(String(b[2])); });
  var sh = classesSheet_(ss, rows.length);
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, rows.length, CLASS_HEADS.length).setValues(rows);
  writeClassStatus_(ss);
  try { ss.setActiveSheet(sh); } catch (e) {}
  refreshMenu_();
  ss.toast("Tick every class getting this assignment (each tick is saved right away), then choose step 2.", "Step 1: Classes tab", 20);
}

/** The Classes tab, emptied below the headings and ready for n classes (checkboxes in Link, plain text in Classroom ID). */
function classesSheet_(ss, n) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_CLASSES) || ss.insertSheet(SH_CLASSES);
  var last = sh.getLastRow();
  if (last > CLASS_HEAD_ROW) sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, last - CLASS_HEAD_ROW, CLASS_HEADS.length).clearDataValidations().clearContent();
  sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).merge();
  sh.getRange(1, 1).setValue("Tick every class getting this assignment. Each tick is saved right away; step 2 then makes the assignment in the ticked classes. " +
    "The period is the number in front of the class name: type over it if it's wrong.")
    .setFontWeight("bold").setWrap(true).setVerticalAlignment("middle").setFontFamily("Poppins");
  sh.setRowHeight(1, 54);
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW, 1, 1, CLASS_HEADS.length).setValues([CLASS_HEADS])
    .setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setFontFamily("Poppins");
  sh.setFrozenRows(CLASS_HEAD_ROW);
  [70, 70, 300, 420, 160].forEach(function (w, i) { sh.setColumnWidth(i + 1, w); });
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 5, n, 1).setNumberFormat("@");   // long Classroom IDs stay exact
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, n, CLASS_HEADS.length).setFontFamily("Poppins").setVerticalAlignment("middle");
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, n, 2).setHorizontalAlignment("center");
  sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, n, 1).insertCheckboxes();
  sh.hideColumns(5);
  return sh;
}

/** Saves the Classes tab's ticks as the linked classes. Returns { linked, changed }, or null with no Classes tab.
 *  Never calls Classroom and never opens the spreadsheet by ID: onEdit (a simple trigger) runs it with e.source. */
function applyClassesTab_(ss) {
  var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_CLASSES);
  if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() <= CLASS_HEAD_ROW) return null;
  var rows = sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 1, sh.getLastRow() - CLASS_HEAD_ROW, CLASS_HEADS.length).getValues();
  var now = courses_(), off = coursesOff_(), was = {}, ticked = {}, linked = [];
  now.forEach(function (c) { was[String(c.id)] = c; });
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var id = String(r[4] == null ? "" : r[4]).trim();
    if (!id || ticked[id] || r[0] !== true) return;
    ticked[id] = true;
    var old = was[id] || off[id] || {};
    linked.push({ id: id, name: String(r[2] || old.name || "").trim(), period: cleanPeriod_(r[1]), cwId: old.cwId || "" });
  });
  linked.sort(byPeriod_);
  function key(list) { return JSON.stringify(list.map(function (c) { return [String(c.id), String(c.name), String(c.period), String(c.cwId || "")]; })); }
  if (key(linked) === key(now)) return { linked: now, changed: false };
  now.forEach(function (c) { if (!ticked[String(c.id)] && c.cwId) off[String(c.id)] = { cwId: c.cwId, name: c.name, period: c.period }; });
  linked.forEach(function (c) { delete off[c.id]; });
  props_().setProperty("COURSES_OFF", JSON.stringify(off));
  props_().setProperty("ROSTER_STALE", "1");   // step 2 (or the hourly check) loads the new classes' students
  saveCourses_(linked);
  return { linked: linked, changed: true };
}

/** The Classes tab's Status column: linked or not, students on the Roster, and whether step 2 made the assignment. Never fails the caller. */
function writeClassStatus_(ss) {
  try {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_CLASSES);
    if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() <= CLASS_HEAD_ROW) return;
    var n = sh.getLastRow() - CLASS_HEAD_ROW, ids = sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 5, n, 1).getValues(), on = {}, kids = {};
    courses_().forEach(function (c) { on[String(c.id)] = c; });
    var r = ss.getSheetByName(SH_ROSTER);
    if (r && r.getLastRow() > 1) r.getRange(2, 5, r.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function (v) { var k = String(v[0]); kids[k] = (kids[k] || 0) + 1; });
    sh.getRange(CLASS_HEAD_ROW + 1, 4, n, 1).setValues(ids.map(function (v) {
      var c = on[String(v[0]).trim()];
      if (!c) return [""];
      var k = kids[String(c.id)];
      return [(c.period === "" ? "Linked. Type its period number in the Period column. " : "Linked. ") + (k ? k + " students. " : "") +
              (c.cwId ? "Assignment made (step 2)." : "Next: step 2 makes the assignment here.")];
    }));
  } catch (e) { /* only a label */ }
}

/** onEdit in the Classes tab: a tick, an untick or a typed period is saved at once, and the Status column and step 3's menu follow. */
function classesEdited_(e) {
  if (wrongKind_() || e.range.getLastRow() <= CLASS_HEAD_ROW) return;
  var lock = null;
  try { lock = LockService.getScriptLock(); if (!lock.tryLock(10000)) lock = null; } catch (err) { lock = null; }
  try {
    var ss = e.source || SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), got = applyClassesTab_(ss);
    if (!got) return;
    writeClassStatus_(ss);
    if (!got.changed) return;
    refreshMenu_();
    var todo = got.linked.filter(function (c) { return !c.cwId; }).map(function (c) { return c.period; });
    ss.toast(got.linked.length ? "Linked: " + periodsText_(got.linked.map(function (c) { return c.period; })) + "." +
               (todo.length ? " Next: step 2 makes the assignment in " + periodsText_(todo) + "." : "")
             : "No classes are linked now. Tick the classes getting this assignment.", "Classes", 8);
  } finally {
    if (lock) unlock_(lock);
  }
}

function describeCourses_(list) {
  return list.map(function (c) {
    return "  Period " + (c.period === "" ? "?" : c.period) + ": " + c.name + (c.cwId ? "  (assignment created)" : "");
  }).join("\n");
}

function showLinkedClasses() {
  menuOnly_();
  try { applyClassesTab_(ss_()); } catch (e) {}
  var list = courses_();
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(list.length ? "Linked classes:\n" + describeCourses_(list) + "\n\nTo change them: step 1 (tick or untick them in the Classes tab)."
                                           : "No classes linked yet. Use step 1.");
}

function refreshRoster() {
  menuOnly_();
  var ss = ss_();
  try { applyClassesTab_(ss); } catch (e) {}
  var n = refreshRoster_();
  writeClassStatus_(ss);
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert("Roster refreshed: " + n + " students.");
}

function refreshRoster_() {
  loadConfig_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var list = courses_();
  if (!list.length) throw new Error("Link your Classroom classes first (step 1).");
  var rows = [];
  list.forEach(function (course) {
    var tok;
    do {
      var resp = Classroom.Courses.Students.list(course.id, { pageToken: tok, pageSize: 100 });
      (resp.students || []).forEach(function (s) {
        rows.push([course.period, s.profile.name.fullName, String(s.profile.emailAddress || "").toLowerCase(), s.userId, course.id]);
      });
      tok = resp.nextPageToken;
    } while (tok);
  });
  rows.sort(function (a, b) { return ((Number(a[0]) || 99) - (Number(b[0]) || 99)) || String(a[1]).localeCompare(String(b[1])); });
  var sh = sheet_(SH_ROSTER);
  if (sh.getLastRow() > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, ROSTER_HEADERS.length).clearContent();
  if (rows.length) sh.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, ROSTER_HEADERS.length).setValues(rows);
  cacheDel_(["roster"]);
  props_().deleteProperty("ROSTER_STALE");
  rebuildLive_();   // shows everyone, sorted by period then name, including students who haven't started
  renderSummary_();
  return rows.length;
}

function studentUrl_(askIfMissing) {
  var url = props_().getProperty("WEBAPP_URL");
  if (url || !askIfMissing) return plainUrl_(url);
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var r = ui.prompt("Student link", "Paste the Web app URL from Deploy > Manage deployments (it ends in /exec):", ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (r.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return "";
  url = r.getResponseText().trim();
  if (!/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/.+\/exec$/.test(url)) { ui.alert("That doesn't look like a web app URL ending in /exec."); return ""; }
  url = plainUrl_(url);
  props_().setProperty("WEBAPP_URL", url);
  return url;
}

/** The /exec link without "/a/macros/<district>/" (with it, a browser with another Google account signed in first
 *  is sent to a district sign-in page instead of the assignment). */
function plainUrl_(url) {
  return String(url || "").replace(/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/a\/macros\/[^\/]+\//, "https://script.google.com/macros/");
}

function showStudentLink() {
  menuOnly_();
  var url = studentUrl_(true);
  if (!url) return;
  var link = classPassLink_(url);
  writeHubStatus_();
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert("Class Pass student link (same for every period):\n\n" + link +
    (link !== url ? "\n\nIt opens the assignment right away wherever Google recognizes the student (school Chromebooks). Everyone else signs in with Class Pass (their school Google account) instead of seeing Google's \"Sorry, unable to open the file\" page." +
                    "\n\nDirect link (skips Class Pass; only for browsers signed in to a school account first):\n\n" + url : ""));
}

function createClassroomAssignment() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var ss = ss_(), tab = applyClassesTab_(ss);   // the Classes tab's ticks (step 1)
  var list = courses_();
  if (!list.length) { ui.alert(tab ? "Tick at least one class in the Classes tab first (step 1)." : "Do step 1 first (link your classes)."); return; }
  var count = refreshRoster_();   // every ticked class's students, so Live Results shows who hasn't started and they can sign in
  try { if (!parseQuarterDates_(QUARTER_END_DATES).list.length) fetchQuarters_(); } catch (e) {}   // quarter end dates (Show quarter close times)
  writeClassStatus_(ss);
  list = courses_();
  var todo = list.filter(function (c) { return !c.cwId; });
  if (!todo.length) {
    var again = ui.alert("Every linked class already has this assignment:\n" + describeCourses_(list) +
                         "\n\nCreate ANOTHER copy in every class?", ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
    if (again !== ui.Button.YES) return;
    todo = list;
  }
  var url = studentUrl_(true);
  if (!url) return;
  var made = [], errors = [];
  todo.forEach(function (c) {
    try {
      var cw = Classroom.Courses.CourseWork.create({
        title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE,
        description: CLASSROOM_TEXT,   // Settings "Classroom instructions"
        materials: [{ link: { url: classPassLink_(url), title: ASSIGNMENT_TITLE } }],
        workType: "ASSIGNMENT",
        state: "DRAFT",
        maxPoints: MAX_POINTS
      }, c.id);
      c.cwId = cw.id;
      rememberDue_(cw.id, 0, Date.parse(cw.creationTime || "") || Date.now());   // the day it was made (QUARTER CLOSE, until a due date is set)
      made.push("  Period " + c.period + ": " + c.name);
    } catch (e) {
      errors.push("  Period " + c.period + ": " + c.name + ": " + e.message);
    }
  });
  saveCourses_(list);
  writeHubStatus_();
  writeClassStatus_(ss);
  refreshMenu_();
  ui.alert((made.length ? "Draft assignment (" + MAX_POINTS + " points) created in:\n" + made.join("\n") +
            "\n\n" + count + " students are in the Roster and Live Results tabs." +
            "\n\nIn Classroom, open each class > Classwork, find the draft, set the due date/topic, and click Assign." : "") +
           (errors.length ? "\n\nProblems:\n" + errors.join("\n") : ""));
}

function uploadDraftGrades()     { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false); }
function uploadAndReturnGrades() { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true); }

// Step 3 for one class (the period's item in the step 3 and 3b menus, see uploadMenus_). One function per period number, 0 to MENU_PERIODS_.
function uploadDraftP0()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 0); }   function uploadReturnP0()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 0); }
