function uploadDraftP1()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 1); }   function uploadReturnP1()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 1); }
function uploadDraftP2()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 2); }   function uploadReturnP2()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 2); }
function uploadDraftP3()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 3); }   function uploadReturnP3()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 3); }
function uploadDraftP4()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 4); }   function uploadReturnP4()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 4); }
function uploadDraftP5()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 5); }   function uploadReturnP5()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 5); }
function uploadDraftP6()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 6); }   function uploadReturnP6()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 6); }
function uploadDraftP7()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 7); }   function uploadReturnP7()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 7); }
function uploadDraftP8()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 8); }   function uploadReturnP8()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 8); }
function uploadDraftP9()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 9); }   function uploadReturnP9()  { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 9); }
function uploadDraftP10() { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(false, 10); }  function uploadReturnP10() { menuOnly_(); uploadGrades_(true, 10); }

/** Step 3 / 3b: every linked class, or with a period number only that period's class(es). */
function uploadGrades_(andReturn, period) {
  loadConfig_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  try { applyClassesTab_(ss_()); } catch (e) {}   // the Classes tab's ticks (step 1)
  var list = courses_().filter(function (c) { return c.cwId; });
  if (!list.length) { ui.alert("Do steps 1 and 2 first. Grades can only go to assignments created by step 2."); return; }
  var one = period != null;
  if (one) {
    list = list.filter(function (c) { return String(c.period) === String(period); });
    if (!list.length) {
      ui.alert(courses_().some(function (c) { return String(c.period) === String(period); })
        ? "Period " + period + " doesn't have this assignment yet. Do step 2 first."
        : "No linked class is Period " + period + " now. Reload the sheet to update this menu.");
      return;
    }
  }
  if (andReturn) {
    var ok = ui.alert("This posts grades AND returns them in " + (one ? "Period " + period + " (" + list.map(function (c) { return c.name; }).join(", ") + ")" : "every linked class") +
                      ", so students will see their scores in Classroom right away. Continue?", ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
    if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  }
  var res = uploadGradesCore_(list, andReturn);
  logUpload_(one ? "Manual, Period " + period : "Manual", res, andReturn);
  ui.alert("Grades " + (andReturn ? "uploaded and returned" : "uploaded as DRAFT grades (review and click Return in Classroom)") + ":\n" +
           res.lines.join("\n") + (res.errors.length ? "\n\nProblems:\n" + res.errors.slice(0, 10).join("\n") : ""));
}

/** Posts grades for the given linked classes. No pop-ups, so it also runs from the automatic timer. */
function uploadGradesCore_(list, andReturn) {
  refreshRoster_();
  var byId = {};
  var roster = readRoster_();
  Object.keys(roster).forEach(function (email) { byId[roster[email].userId] = email; });
  var recs = allRecords_();
  var S = Classroom.Courses.CourseWork.StudentSubmissions;
  var lines = [], errors = [], ok = {};
  var mailed = JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("LATE_MAILED") || "{}"), mailedChanged = false;

  list.forEach(function (course) {
    var subs = [], tok, done = 0, skipped = 0, lateN = 0, due = 0;
    if (latePct_()) { try { var dd = classroomDue_(course); due = dd ? dd.getTime() : 0; } catch (e) { due = dueOfCourse_(course, true); } }
    try {
      do {
        var resp = S.list(course.id, course.cwId, { pageToken: tok });
        subs = subs.concat(resp.studentSubmissions || []);
        tok = resp.nextPageToken;
      } while (tok);
    } catch (e) {
      errors.push("Period " + course.period + ": couldn't read the assignment (was it deleted in Classroom?) " + e.message);
      return;
    }
    subs.forEach(function (sub) {
      var email = byId[sub.userId];
      var rec = email ? recs[email] : null;
      if (!rec || !rec.checks) {
        if (!UPLOAD_ZERO_FOR_MISSING) { skipped++; return; }
        rec = newRecord_();
      }
      var info = lateGrade_(rec, due), g = info.grade;
      if (info.late) lateN++;
      try {
        var body = { draftGrade: g }, mask = "draftGrade";
        if (andReturn) { body.assignedGrade = g; mask = "draftGrade,assignedGrade"; }
        S.patch(body, course.id, course.cwId, sub.id, { updateMask: mask });
        if (andReturn) {
          var ret = S["return"] || S["return_"];
          try { ret.call(S, {}, course.id, course.cwId, sub.id); } catch (e) { /* not turned in yet: grade still saved */ }
          // Classroom's API can't post private comments, so the late-penalty note goes by email (once per grade).
          if (info.late && LATE_EMAIL && email && mailed[email] !== g) {
            try {
              var why = mailLatePenalty_(email, info, (roster[email] || {}).name, due);
              if (why) errors.push("Period " + course.period + ": " + why);
              else { mailed[email] = g; mailedChanged = true; }
            } catch (e) { errors.push("Period " + course.period + ": late-penalty email to " + email + " failed: " + e.message); }
          }
        }
        done++;
      } catch (e) {
        errors.push("Period " + course.period + " " + (email || sub.userId) + ": " + e.message);
      }
    });
    ok[course.cwId] = true;
    lines.push("  Period " + course.period + ": " + done + " uploaded" + (skipped ? ", " + skipped + " not started (left blank)" : "") +
               (lateN ? ", " + lateN + " with the " + pctText_() + " late penalty" : ""));
  });
  if (mailedChanged) props_().setProperty("LATE_MAILED", JSON.stringify(mailed));
  return { lines: lines, errors: errors, ok: ok };
}

/** One row per upload in the "Grade Uploads" tab, so you can see what the timer did. */
function logUpload_(how, res, andReturn) {
  try {
    var ss = ss_();
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_UPLOADS);
    if (!sh) {
      sh = ss.insertSheet(SH_UPLOADS);
      sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([["Time", "How", "Result", "Problems"]])
        .setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#c9daf8").setFontFamily("Poppins");
      sh.setFrozenRows(1); sh.setColumnWidth(1, 150); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 220); sh.setColumnWidth(3, 420); sh.setColumnWidth(4, 420);
    }
    sh.appendRow([new Date(), how + (andReturn ? " (posted + returned)" : " (draft)"),
                  res.lines.join("\n").replace(/^\s+/gm, ""), res.errors.slice(0, 20).join("\n")]);
  } catch (e) { /* logging must never stop an upload */ }
}

// ============================== AUTOMATIC GRADES AFTER THE DUE DATE ==============================
// An hourly timer checks each linked class's due date in Classroom. Once it passes, grades are
// uploaded for that class, then re-sent once a day for AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS days to pick up late work.
// Each class follows its own due date. Progress is kept in the script property AUTO_STATE.

var AUTO_HANDLER = "autoUploadGrades";
var AUTO_DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;

function autoTriggers_() {
  return ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(function (t) { return t.getHandlerFunction() === AUTO_HANDLER; });
}
function autoState_() { return JSON.parse(props_().getProperty("AUTO_STATE") || "{}"); }
function saveAutoState_(s) { props_().setProperty("AUTO_STATE", JSON.stringify(s)); }

/** The Classroom due date/time as a Date, or null if none is set. Classroom stores it in UTC. */
function classroomDue_(course) {
  var cw = Classroom.Courses.CourseWork.get(course.id, course.cwId);
  var created = Date.parse(cw.creationTime || "") || 0;
  if (!cw.dueDate) { rememberDue_(course.cwId, 0, created); return null; }
  var d = cw.dueDate, t = cw.dueTime || { hours: 23, minutes: 59 };
  var due = new Date(Date.UTC(d.year, d.month - 1, d.day, t.hours || 0, t.minutes || 0));
  rememberDue_(course.cwId, due.getTime(), created);   // student saves use this for the late penalty
  return due;
}

function ensureTimer_() {
  if (!autoTriggers_().length) ScriptApp.newTrigger(AUTO_HANDLER).timeBased().everyHours(1).create();
}

function autoGradesOn() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var list = courses_().filter(function (c) { return c.cwId; });
  if (!list.length) { ui.alert("Do steps 1 and 2 first. Grades can only go to assignments created by step 2."); return; }
  addMissingSettings_(openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS));
  setSetting_(AUTO_LABEL, true);
  AUTO_GRADES = true;
  ensureTimer_();
  writeHubStatus_();
  ui.alert("Automatic grades are ON.\n\n" + autoSummary_(list) + "\n\n" +
    "About an hour after each due date, grades are " + (AUTO_RETURN_GRADES ? "posted AND returned (students see them)" : "uploaded as DRAFT grades for you to return") + ". " +
    (AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS > 0 ? "They are updated once a day for " + AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS + " more days to count late work, then uploading stops by itself." : "This happens once.") +
    (latePct_() ? "\n\n" + lateNote_() + (LATE_EMAIL && AUTO_RETURN_GRADES ? " Students with a late penalty get an email explaining it." : "") : "") +
    "\n\nChange the due date in Classroom any time; the timer reads it fresh each hour. Every upload is listed in the Grade Uploads tab.");
}

function autoGradesOff() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  addMissingSettings_(openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS));
  setSetting_(AUTO_LABEL, false);
  AUTO_GRADES = false;
  try { ensureTimer_(); } catch (e) {}   // the quiet hourly check stays, so the Settings tab or the Self-Check Hub can turn it back on
  writeHubStatus_();
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert("Automatic grades are OFF. You can still use step 3 any time, and turn this back on from the menu, the Settings tab, or the Self-Check Hub.");
}

function autoGradesStatus() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  requireClassroom_();
  var list = courses_().filter(function (c) { return c.cwId; });
  var timer = autoTriggers_().length > 0;
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert("Automatic grades are " + (AUTO_GRADES ? "ON" : "OFF") + "." +
    (AUTO_GRADES && !timer ? "\n\nThe hourly check isn't running, so nothing will upload. Run Self-Check > Set up / repair sheets to start it." : "") + "\n\n" +
    (list.length ? autoSummary_(list) : "No classes have the assignment yet (steps 1 and 2)."));
}

function autoSummary_(list) {
  var st = autoState_(), now = Date.now();
  var fmt = function (d) { return Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "EEE M/d h:mm a"); };
  return list.map(function (c) {
    var line = "  Period " + c.period + ": ";
    var s = st[c.cwId];
    try {
      var due = classroomDue_(c);
      if (!due) return line + "no due date set in Classroom yet. It will wait until you set one.";
      line += "due " + fmt(due);
      if (!s) return line + (due.getTime() > now ? ". Grades will upload after that." : ". The due date passed, so grades upload within the hour.");
      var until = new Date(new Date(s.first).getTime() + AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS * AUTO_DAY_MS);
      return line + ". First uploaded " + fmt(new Date(s.first)) + (until.getTime() > now ? ", updating daily until " + fmt(until) : ", finished") + ".";
    } catch (e) {
      return line + "couldn't read it from Classroom (" + e.message + ")";
    }
  }).join("\n");
}

/** Runs every hour from the timer. Never shows pop-ups. Uploads grades only when automatic grades are ON;
    every run also answers Self-Check Hub requests and writes this sheet's status for the hub. */
function autoUploadGrades(e) {
  // Only this sheet's own hourly timer may run it (the web app lets anyone call a function by name).
  if (!e || !e.triggerUid || !autoTriggers_().some(function (t) { return String(t.getUniqueId()) === String(e.triggerUid); })) return;
  if (wrongKind_()) return;   // PASTE SAFETY: code for another kind of sheet never uploads grades
  try {
    var st0 = openSS_() && openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS);
    if (addMissingSettings_(st0)) clearConfigCache_();   // code updated without Set up / repair: add the new rows (the timer was on, so automatic grades stay on)
  } catch (err) {}
  loadConfig_();
  try { hubRequests_(); } catch (err) {}
  try {   // classes ticked in the Classes tab but step 2 not run yet: load their students anyway (Class Pass signs in only Roster students)
    if (props_().getProperty("ROSTER_STALE") && courses_().length) { requireClassroom_(); refreshRoster_(); writeClassStatus_(openSS_()); }
  } catch (err) {}
  try { articleAutoRefresh_(); } catch (err) {}   // a reading page's article follows its Google Doc
  try { syncWebsiteBoard_(); } catch (err) {}     // a review game's points in progress go to the website leaderboard
  if (AUTO_GRADES) {
    requireClassroom_();
    var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
    if (!lock.tryLock(60000)) { writeHubStatus_(); return; }   // another upload is running; try next hour
    try {
      autoRun_();
    } finally {
      unlock_(lock);
    }
  }
  try { stopTimerIfFinished_(); } catch (err) {}
  writeHubStatus_();
}

/** One automatic-grades pass (inside the script lock). */
function autoRun_() {
  var st = autoState_(), now = Date.now(), todo = [], allDone = true;
  courses_().filter(function (c) { return c.cwId; }).forEach(function (c) {
    var s = st[c.cwId];
    if (s && (AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS <= 0 || now - new Date(s.first).getTime() > AUTO_UPDATE_DAYS * AUTO_DAY_MS + 2 * 3600000)) return;   // finished
    var cl = closeForCourse_(c, c.period, false);   // also keeps the quarter dates fresh
    if (cl && s && isClosed_(cl) && new Date(s.last).getTime() > cl.at) return;     // quarter over and grades sent since: nothing can change
    allDone = false;
    var due;
    try { due = classroomDue_(c); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!due || due.getTime() > now) return;                                        // not due yet (or no due date)
    if (s && now - new Date(s.last).getTime() < AUTO_DAY_MS - 30 * 60 * 1000) return; // already sent in the last day
    todo.push(c);
  });
  if (todo.length) {
    var res = uploadGradesCore_(todo, AUTO_RETURN_GRADES);
    var stamp = new Date(now).toISOString();
    todo.forEach(function (c) {
      if (!res.ok[c.cwId]) return;
      st[c.cwId] = st[c.cwId] ? { first: st[c.cwId].first, last: stamp } : { first: stamp, last: stamp };
    });
    saveAutoState_(st);
    logUpload_("Automatic (after due date)", res, AUTO_RETURN_GRADES);
    allDone = false;
  }
}

/** The hourly check stops itself once every linked class has closed for the quarter and nothing more can be uploaded.
    (Moving a due date into the next quarter later? Run Set up / repair sheets to start it again.) */
function stopTimerIfFinished_() {
  if (!CLOSE_AT_QUARTER_END) return;
  var list = courses_().filter(function (c) { return c.cwId; });
  if (!list.length) return;
  var st = autoState_();
  var done = list.every(function (c) {
    var cl = closeForCourse_(c, c.period, true);
    if (!isClosed_(cl)) return false;
    if (!AUTO_GRADES) return true;
    var s = st[c.cwId];
    return (s && new Date(s.last).getTime() > cl.at) || !dueOfCourse_(c, true);
  });
  if (done) autoTriggers_().forEach(function (t) { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
}

// ============================== SELF-CHECK HUB ==============================
// The hub (a separate spreadsheet) reads the Settings tab and this hidden status tab, and can change Settings values.
// It asks for anything else (for now: "recalc") by typing it in the Hub request cell; the hourly check does it.
var HUB_REQ_ROW = 8, HUB_RESULT_ROW = 9;

/** Writes this sheet's status to the hidden _hub tab. Never shows pop-ups and never fails the caller. */
function writeHubStatus_() {
  try {
    loadConfig_();
    var ss = openSS_();
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(SH_HUB);
    if (!sh) { sh = ss.insertSheet(SH_HUB); sh.hideSheet(); sh.setColumnWidth(1, 180); sh.setColumnWidth(2, 600); }
    var st = autoState_(), known = dueDates_();
    var classes = courses_().map(function (c) {
      var due = dueEntry_(known[c.cwId]) || { d: 0, at: 0, c: 0 }, cl = null;
      try { cl = c.cwId ? closeForCourse_(c, c.period, true) : null; } catch (e) {}
      var s = st[c.cwId] || null;
      return { period: c.period, name: c.name, id: c.id, cwId: c.cwId || "", due: due.d, dueAsked: due.at, created: due.c,
               close: cl ? { at: cl.at, name: cl.name, end: cl.end } : null, closed: isClosed_(cl),
               firstAuto: s ? s.first : "", lastAuto: s ? s.last : "" };
    });
    var link = "";
    try { var u = studentUrl_(false); link = u ? classPassLink_(u) : ""; } catch (e) {}
    var timer = false;
    try { timer = autoTriggers_().length > 0; } catch (e) {}
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 7, 2).setValues([
      ["Self-Check Hub status", "Written by this sheet's script for the Self-Check Hub. Don't edit."],
      ["Updated", new Date()],
      ["Skill version", SKILL_VERSION],
      ["Automatic grades", AUTO_GRADES ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      ["Hourly check", timer ? "running" : "stopped"],
      ["Student link", link],
      ["Classes (JSON)", JSON.stringify(classes)]
    ]);
    sh.getRange(HUB_REQ_ROW, 1).setValue("Hub request");
    sh.getRange(HUB_RESULT_ROW, 1).setValue("Last hub request");
  } catch (e) { /* the hub status must never stop anything else */ }
}

/** Does what the Self-Check Hub asked for (typed in the Hub request cell), then clears the request. */
function hubRequests_() {
  var sh = openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_HUB);
  if (!sh) return;
  var req = String(sh.getRange(HUB_REQ_ROW, 2).getValue() || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!req) return;
  sh.getRange(HUB_REQ_ROW, 2).setValue("");
  var result;
  try {
    if (req === "recalc") result = "Recalculated " + recalcCore_() + " students.";
    else result = "Didn't understand the request \"" + req + "\".";
  } catch (e) { result = "Problem: " + e.message; }
  sh.getRange(HUB_RESULT_ROW, 2).setValue(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz_(), "M/d h:mm a") + ": " + result);
}

// ============================== CHECK FOR SKILL UPDATES ==============================
// Releases come from two places (2026.10.30b), and the newer one wins:
// 1. The class website: SKILL_UPDATES_URL + "<skill name>/latest.json" ({ kind, version, files: { "Code.gs": "Code.gs.txt", ... } }) and those
//    files next to it, written by npm run release in Donny's class-tools repo. Its Code.gs carries NO starter questions or answer keys (the
//    website is public), so it only suits a sheet whose questions are in its Questions tab (questionsInTab_).
// 2. A Drive folder named SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER, with plain-text files named
//      "<skill name> v<YYYY.MM.DD> - What changed.txt"
//      "<skill name> v<YYYY.MM.DD> - Code.gs"
//      "<skill name> v<YYYY.MM.DD> - Index.html"
//    The same folder can hold releases for any of your skills; each sheet only looks at its own SKILL_NAME.
// Either way a release is { sawFolder, version, web, files: { "Code.gs": { url, text() }, ... } }.

/** Newest release of a skill in the Drive updates folder (version null when there is none). */
function latestSkillRelease_(name) {
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER), best = null, byVer = {}, sawFolder = false;
  while (folders.hasNext()) {
    sawFolder = true;
    var files = folders.next().getFiles();
    while (files.hasNext()) {
      var f = files.next();
      var m = /^(.+) v(\d{4}\.\d{2}\.\d{2}[a-z]?) - (.+)$/.exec(f.getName());
      if (!m || m[1] !== name) continue;
      (byVer[m[2]] = byVer[m[2]] || {})[m[3]] = f;
      if (!best || m[2] > best) best = m[2];   // YYYY.MM.DD sorts correctly as text
    }
  }
  var out = {};
  Object.keys(best ? byVer[best] : {}).forEach(function (label) {
    var f = byVer[best][label];
    out[label] = { url: f.getUrl(), text: function () { return f.getBlob().getDataAsString("UTF-8"); } };
  });
  return { sawFolder: sawFolder, version: best, files: out, web: false };
}

/** The newest release on the class website, or null (none there, or the site didn't answer). Only the three known files, by plain names. */
function webSkillRelease_(name) {
  if (!SKILL_UPDATES_URL) return null;
  var base = SKILL_UPDATES_URL + encodeURIComponent(name) + "/", j = null;
  try {
    var r = UrlFetchApp.fetch(base + "latest.json", { muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true });
    if (r.getResponseCode() === 200) j = JSON.parse(r.getContentText("UTF-8"));
  } catch (e) { return null; }
  if (!j || j.kind !== name || !/^\d{4}\.\d{2}\.\d{2}[a-z]?$/.test(String(j.version)) || !j.files) return null;
  var out = {};
  ["What changed.txt", "Code.gs", "Index.html"].forEach(function (label) {
    var file = String(j.files[label] || "");
    if (!/^[\w.-]+$/.test(file)) return;
    var url = base + file;
    out[label] = { url: url, text: function () {
      var t = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, { muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true });
      return t.getResponseCode() === 200 ? t.getContentText("UTF-8") : "";
    } };
  });
  return { sawFolder: true, version: String(j.version), files: out, web: true };
}

/** Are this sheet's questions in its Questions tab? (If not, it runs on the starter questions built into its code, which website releases don't carry.) */
function questionsInTab_() {
  var qs = openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_QUESTIONS);
  return !!qs && qs.getLastRow() > 1;
}

/** The release to offer: the newer of the website's and the Drive folder's. With the same version in both, the website's, unless this sheet's
    questions live only in its code (then the Drive copy, which still carries them). Throws only when neither place could be read. */
function skillRelease_(name) {
  var web = webSkillRelease_(name), drive = null, err = null;
  try { drive = latestSkillRelease_(name); } catch (e) { err = e; }
  if (!drive) { if (web) return web; throw err; }
  if (!web || !drive.version) return web || drive;
  if (web.version > drive.version) return web;
  if (web.version < drive.version) return drive;
  return questionsInTab_() ? web : drive;
}

function htmlEsc_(s) {
  return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}

/** Menu: Self-Check > Check for skill updates. */
function checkForSkillUpdates() {
  menuOnly_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi(), rel;
  try { rel = skillRelease_(SKILL_NAME); }
  catch (e) { ui.alert("Couldn't reach the class website or your Google Drive: " + e.message); return; }
  if (!rel.sawFolder || !rel.version) {
    ui.alert("Check for skill updates",
      "This sheet is on " + SKILL_NAME + " version " + SKILL_VERSION + ".\n\n" +
      "The class website has no release for " + SKILL_NAME + ", and " +
      (rel.sawFolder ? "neither does the \"" + SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER + "\" folder in your Google Drive."
                     : "there's no folder named \"" + SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER + "\" in your Google Drive.") +
      "\n\nAsk Claude: \"Release " + SKILL_NAME + " and publish it.\"", ui.ButtonSet.OK);
    return;
  }
  var from = rel.web ? "from the class website" : "from your Drive folder " + SKILL_UPDATES_FOLDER;
  // a website release has no starter questions: a sheet whose questions are only in its code must copy them into its Questions tab first
  var noQs = rel.web && !questionsInTab_();
  var style = "<style>body{font-family:Poppins,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;color:#111;margin:0}" +
    "h2{font-size:17px;margin:0 0 8px}.v{background:#c9daf8;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:6px;margin-bottom:10px}" +
    "pre{white-space:pre-wrap;background:#f3f3f3;border:1px solid #bbb;padding:8px;max-height:150px;overflow:auto;font-family:inherit;font-size:13px}" +
    "ol{padding-left:20px;margin:6px 0}li{margin:4px 0}button{font-family:inherit;font-weight:700;border:2px solid #111;border-radius:6px;background:#111;color:#fff;padding:6px 12px;cursor:pointer;margin:2px 0}" +
    "textarea{position:absolute;left:-9999px}.ok{color:#38761d;font-weight:600}.kind{font-size:24px;font-weight:700;margin:0 0 4px}.kindw{font-size:12px;color:#555}</style>" +
    "<div class='kindw'>This sheet and this code are for</div><div class='kind'>" + htmlEsc_(kindName_(SKILL_NAME)) + "</div>";   // PASTE SAFETY: which kind, in large text
  function text(label) { var f = rel.files[label]; try { return f ? f.text() : ""; } catch (e) { return ""; } }
  function copyBtn(id, label) {
    if (label === "Code.gs" && noQs) return "<b>First: Self-Check &gt; Set up / repair sheets.</b> This sheet's questions are only in its code (its Questions tab is empty), " +
      "and releases on the class website don't carry questions. Set up copies them into the Questions tab; then check for updates again.";
    return rel.files[label] ? "<button onclick=\"cp('" + id + "',this)\">Copy " + label + "</button>" +
      " <a href='" + htmlEsc_(rel.files[label].url) + "' target='_blank'>open file</a>" : "(" + label + " is missing from the release)";
  }
  var copyJs = "<script>function cp(id,b){var t=document.getElementById(id);t.select();var ok=false;try{ok=document.execCommand('copy')}catch(e){}" +
    "if(!ok&&navigator.clipboard){navigator.clipboard.writeText(t.value).then(function(){b.textContent='Copied!'});return}" +
    "b.textContent=ok?'Copied!':'Copy failed - use open file';}</script>";
  if (rel.version <= SKILL_VERSION) {
    var miss = missingPieces_();
    if (miss.settings.length || miss.columns.length) {
      var fix = ui.alert("Code is up to date, but the tabs aren't",
        "This sheet's code is version " + SKILL_VERSION + " (the newest is " + rel.version + "), but its tabs were made by an older version.\n\n" +
        missingText_(miss) + "\n\nAdd them now? (Same as Self-Check > Set up / repair sheets. Your current settings, questions and student work are not changed. New settings start at their usual defaults.)",
        ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
      if (fix === ui.Button.YES) setupSheets();
      return;
    }
    // Up to date: the same release's files can still be copied again, e.g. to give a sheet its two-column Index (SKILL_NAME
    // reading-activity) from any computer. Only when the release IS this version, so a newer sheet is never offered older code.
    var same = rel.version === SKILL_VERSION;
    var again = !same ? "" : "<b>Need these files again?</b> For example, the Index file still shows one column, or this is a new sheet:<ol>" +
      "<li>" + copyBtn("ix", "Index.html") + "<br>Extensions &gt; Apps Script &gt; click the <b>Index</b> file: Ctrl+A, Ctrl+V (paste), Save.</li>" +
      "<li>" + copyBtn("gs", "Code.gs") + "<br>Only if Code.gs needs replacing too: click <b>Code.gs</b>, Ctrl+A, Ctrl+V, Save.</li>" +
      "<li>Deploy &gt; Manage deployments &gt; pencil &gt; Version: <b>New version</b> &gt; Deploy. (Keeps the same student link.)</li></ol>" +
      "<textarea id='gs'>" + htmlEsc_(text("Code.gs")) + "</textarea><textarea id='ix'>" + htmlEsc_(text("Index.html")) + "</textarea>" + copyJs;
    ui.showModalDialog(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(style + "<h2 class='ok'>You're up to date.</h2><div class='v'>This sheet: version " +
      htmlEsc_(SKILL_VERSION) + " (" + htmlEsc_(SKILL_NAME) + ")<br>Newest release: version " + htmlEsc_(rel.version) + " (" + htmlEsc_(from) + ")</div>" + again)
      .setWidth(same ? 520 : 420).setHeight(same ? 450 : 230), "Check for skill updates");
    return;
  }
  var notes = text("What changed.txt"), gs = noQs ? "" : text("Code.gs"), idx = text("Index.html");
  var body = style +
    "<h2>A newer version is available</h2>" +
    "<div class='v'>This sheet: version " + htmlEsc_(SKILL_VERSION) + "<br>Newest release: <b>version " + htmlEsc_(rel.version) + "</b> (" + htmlEsc_(from) + ")</div>" +
    (notes ? "<b>What changed</b><pre>" + htmlEsc_(notes) + "</pre>" : "") +
    "<b>How to update (about 3 minutes)</b><ol>" +
    "<li>" + copyBtn("gs", "Code.gs") + "<br>Then Extensions &gt; Apps Script &gt; <b>Code.gs</b>: click in the code, Ctrl+A, Ctrl+V (paste), Save.</li>" +
    "<li>" + copyBtn("ix", "Index.html") + "<br>Click the <b>Index</b> file: Ctrl+A, Ctrl+V, Save.</li>" +
    "<li>Deploy &gt; Manage deployments &gt; pencil &gt; Version: <b>New version</b> &gt; Deploy. (Keeps the same student link.)</li>" +
    "<li>Reload this sheet, then Self-Check &gt; <b>Set up / repair sheets</b>. Approve if Google asks.</li></ol>" +
    "Your questions, answer keys, settings and student work stay; they live in the tabs, not the code." +
    "<textarea id='gs'>" + htmlEsc_(gs) + "</textarea><textarea id='ix'>" + htmlEsc_(idx) + "</textarea>" + copyJs;
  ui.showModalDialog(HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(body).setWidth(560).setHeight(610), "Check for skill updates");
}

/** Try an answer against the key without being a student. */
function testAnswer() {
  menuOnly_();
  loadConfig_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var q = ui.prompt("Test an answer", "Which question number? (1-" + ITEMS.length + ")", ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (q.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var n = parseInt(q.getResponseText(), 10);
  if (!(n >= 1 && n <= ITEMS.length)) { ui.alert("That isn't a question number."); return; }
  var it = ITEMS[n - 1];
  if (it.table && it.table.graph) { ui.alert("Q" + n + " is a graph of the numbers students type in " + it.table.graph.lines.map(function (L) { return "Q" + L.from; }).join(" and ") + ". Try it on the student page: each axis label colors itself (Idea 1 checks the x-axis label, Idea 2 the y-axis label" + (graphHasTop_(it) ? ", Idea 3 the top axis label" : "") + ")."); return; }
  if (it.table) { ui.alert("Q" + n + " is a data table with a graph. Try it on the student page: the table, the title and the two axis labels each color themselves (Ideas 1 to 3 check the title, x-axis and y-axis labels)."); return; }
  if (it.upload) { ui.alert("Q" + n + " is a " + (it.draw ? "drawing" : "picture upload") + ". It isn't graded: it turns green once the student saves one."); return; }
  var how = isTried_(it) && it.kind === "dropdown"
    ? "\n\nType the choice number for each of its " + it.blanks + " boxes, in order, separated by commas (like 2, 3, 2):\n" + it.choices.map(function (c, k) { return (k + 1) + ". " + c.t; }).join("\n")
    : isTried_(it) && it.choices
    ? "\n\nType the number of a choice to try it:\n" + it.choices.map(function (c, k) { return (k + 1) + ". " + (c.img ? "(picture)" : c.t); }).join("\n")
    : isTried_(it) ? "\n\nType a sample student answer (one try; " + (isNumberQ_(it) ? "a number question: 25, 25 J and 25.0 all read 25" : "no minimum length") + "):"
    : it.boxes && it.boxes.length
    ? "\n\nThis question has " + it.boxes.length + " boxes on the picture (" + it.boxes.map(function (b) { return b.label; }).join(", ") +
      "). Type one answer per box, separated by |   e.g.  answer one | answer two | answer three"
    : "\n\nType a sample student answer:";
  var a = ui.prompt("Q" + n, it.q + how, ui.ButtonSet.OK_CANCEL);
  if (a.getSelectedButton() !== ui.Button.OK) return;
  var r = feedbackFor_(n - 1, it.kind === "dropdown" ? a.getResponseText().replace(/\s+/g, "") : a.getResponseText());
  if (isTried_(it)) {   // one try: right or not, and the feedback a student would see for it
    ui.alert("Q" + n + ", that try would be:  " + ({ correct: "RIGHT", partial: "NOT RIGHT YET (part of it)", incorrect: "NOT RIGHT", blank: "NOT AN ANSWER" })[r.status] + "\n\n" + (r.message || "(nothing to grade)") +
      (r.status === "correct" ? "" : "\n\nStudents get " + maxTries_(it) + (maxTries_(it) === 1 ? " try" : " tries") + " on this question.") + (it.explain ? "\n\nExplanation (shown once it's done): " + it.explain : ""));
    return;
  }
  var color = { correct: "GREEN", partial: "YELLOW", incorrect: "RED", blank: "BLANK" }[r.status];
  var per = r.parts ? "\n\nBoxes: " + it.boxes.map(function (b, k) { return b.label + " = " + ({ correct: "green", incorrect: "red", blank: "blank" })[r.parts[k]]; }).join(", ") : "";
  ui.alert("Q" + n + " would show:  " + color + per + "\n\n" + (r.message || "(no feedback for a blank answer)"));
}

/** Clears students, roster, results and linked classes; keeps questions, key and settings. */
function resetForNewYear() {
  menuOnly_();
  var ui = SpreadsheetApp.getUi();
  var ok = ui.alert("Start a new school year?",
    "This permanently clears ALL student answers, the roster, the answer log, and the linked classes in THIS spreadsheet. " +
    "Your questions, answer key, and settings stay.\n\nTip: File > Make a copy first if you want to keep this year's results.\n\nContinue?",
    ui.ButtonSet.YES_NO);
  if (ok !== ui.Button.YES) return;
  var ss = ss_();
  try { cacheDel_(Object.keys(allRecords_()).map(recKey_)); } catch (e) {}   // last year's short-term copies of student work
  [SH_STATE, SH_LOG, SH_ROSTER, SH_LIVE, SH_SUMMARY].forEach(function (n) {
    var sh = ss.getSheetByName(n);
    if (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1) sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, Math.max(1, sh.getLastColumn())).clearContent().setBackground(null);
  });
  var ct = ss.getSheetByName(SH_CLASSES);   // last year's ticks (step 1 lists this year's classes again)
  if (ct) { try { ss.deleteSheet(ct); } catch (e) {} }
  ["COURSES", "COURSE_ID", "COURSE_NAME", "COURSEWORK_ID", "AUTO_STATE", "DUE_DATES", "LATE_MAILED", "QUARTERS", "UPLOAD_FOLDER", "COURSES_OFF", "ROSTER_STALE"].forEach(function (k) { props_().deleteProperty(k); });
  var all = props_().getProperties(), gone = [];   // last year's Class Pass sign-ins (and their short-term copies)
  Object.keys(all).forEach(function (k) { if (k.indexOf("SIGNIN_") === 0) { props_().deleteProperty(k); gone.push(k); } });
  if (gone.length) cacheDel_(gone);
  clearConfigCache_();   // last year's roster copy too
  try { addMissingSettings_(openSS_().getSheetByName(SH_SETTINGS)); setSetting_(AUTO_LABEL, false); AUTO_GRADES = false; } catch (e) {}   // automatic grades off for the new year
  forgetCopiedLink_();
  props_().setProperty("SSID", SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getId());
  renderSummary_();
  writeHubStatus_();
  refreshMenu_();
  ui.alert("Cleared. Next: Self-Check > 1. Link Google Classroom classes (this year's periods), then step 2.\n" +
           "If you typed quarter end dates or class start times in the Settings tab, change them to this year's.\n" +
           "Your student link stays the same unless you made a new deployment.");
}

/** Re-grade every saved answer (use after changing ITEMS or the CREDIT settings). */
function recalculateAll() {
  menuOnly_();
  var n = recalcCore_();
  SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert("Recalculated " + n + " students.");
}

/** Re-grades everything. Never shows pop-ups (the hourly check runs it for the Self-Check Hub). Returns the student count. */
function recalcCore_() {
  clearConfigCache_();
  CONFIG_LOADED_ = false;
  loadConfig_();
  var lock = lockOrBusy_(30000), recs;   // so an answer a student saves right now isn't overwritten
  try {
    recs = allRecords_();
    Object.keys(recs).forEach(function (email) {
      var rec = recs[email];
      // (a question with limited tries: its color from all its tries, graded with today's key, so a fixed Right choice re-grades them)
      for (var i = 0; i < ITEMS.length; i++) rec.statuses[i] = isTried_(ITEMS[i]) ? tryState_(i, rec.tries ? rec.tries[i] : null).status : feedbackFor_(i, rec.answers[i]).status;
      if (rec.onTime) for (var k = 0; k < ITEMS.length; k++) rec.onTime.statuses[k] = isTried_(ITEMS[k]) ? tryState_(k, rec.onTime.tries ? rec.onTime.tries[k] : null).status : feedbackFor_(k, (rec.onTime.answers || [])[k]).status;
      [rec.best, rec.onTime && rec.onTime.best].forEach(function (b) {   // a review game's best try is graded by today's key too
        if (b) for (var j = 0; j < ITEMS.length; j++) b.statuses[j] = feedbackFor_(j, (b.answers || [])[j]).status;
      });
      writeRecord_(email, rec);
    });
  } finally {
    unlock_(lock);
  }
  rebuildLive_();
  renderSummary_();
  return Object.keys(recs).length;
}

// ============================== GRADING ENGINE (unchanged from the Sheet version) ==============================
function gradeLocal_(item, answer) {
  var padded = normalize_(answer);
  var words = padded.trim().split(" "), nums = isNumberQ_(item) ? numbersIn_(answer) : null;   // a short number question compares numbers (see LIMITED TRIES)
  var total = item.criteria.length, score = 0, unmet = [], rows = [], rowAt = {};   // (keyed "r:" + name, so any row name is safe)
  for (var i = 0; i < item.criteria.length; i++) {
    var cr = item.criteria[i], met = criterionMet_(cr, padded, words, answer, nums);
    if (cr.row) {   // an idea tagged with a rubric row ([Use of Data] ...): count how many of that row's ideas the answer shows
      var rk = "r:" + cr.row;
      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rowAt, rk)) { rowAt[rk] = rows.length; rows.push({ label: cr.row, met: 0, total: 0 }); }
      var rw = rows[rowAt[rk]]; rw.total++; if (met) rw.met++;
    }
    if (met) score++;
    else unmet.push((cr.row ? cr.row + ": " : "") + (cr.nudge || "Reread the related part of the directions and try again."));
  }
  var status = score === total ? "correct" : (score === 0 ? "incorrect" : "partial");
  return { status: status, score: score, total: total, unmet: unmet, rows: rows };
}

/** raw = the answer as typed (for the sentence checks); the other two are normalize_(raw) and its words.
 *  nums = the numbers in raw (numbersIn_) on a number question, else null: a key word that is a number then matches that number. */
function criterionMet_(criterion, padded, words, raw, nums) {
  var need = criterion.need;
  for (var s = 0; s < need.length; s++) {
    var set = need[s], hit = false;
    if (set[0] === "!") {   // NOT group: fails if any of these words appear
      for (var u = 1; u < set.length; u++) if (answerHasTerm_(padded, words, set[u], nums)) return false;
      continue;
    }
    if (set[0] === "@s") { if (countSentences_(raw) < Number(set[1])) return false; continue; }   // at least N complete sentences
    if (set[0] === "@c") { if (!clearlyWritten_(raw)) return false; continue; }                   // clearly written (see clearlyWritten_)
    if (/^@\d+$/.test(set[0])) {   // at least N of these words
      var got = 0, want = Number(set[0].slice(1));
      for (var g = 1; g < set.length; g++) if (answerHasTerm_(padded, words, set[g], nums)) got++;
      if (got < want) return false;
      continue;
    }
    for (var t = 0; t < set.length; t++) {
      if (answerHasTerm_(padded, words, set[t], nums)) { hit = true; break; }
    }
    if (!hit) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

/** "Clearly written": at least 3 sentences, the answer ends with . ! or ?, and every sentence starts with a capital letter
 *  (or a number) and has at least 4 words. A cheap stand-in for a rubric's "clearly written" (it can't judge meaning). */
function clearlyWritten_(raw) {
  var text = String(raw == null ? "" : raw).trim();
  if (!/[.!?]["')\]]*$/.test(text)) return false;
  var pieces = text.split(/[.!?]+["')\]]*(?:\s+|$)/).map(function (p) { return p.trim(); }).filter(function (p) { return p; });
  if (pieces.length < 3) return false;
  return pieces.every(function (p) {
    var first = /[A-Za-z0-9]/.exec(p), n = p.split(/\s+/).filter(function (w) { return /[a-z0-9]/i.test(w); }).length;
    return n >= 4 && first && !/[a-z]/.test(first[0]);
  });
}

function normalize_(s) {
  return " " + String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9%\s]/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() + " ";
}

function answerHasTerm_(padded, words, term, nums) {
  if (nums && /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(term)) return nums.some(function (n) { return n === Number(term); });   // a number question: 25 matches 25 J, 25J and 25.0
  if (term.charAt(0) === "#") return term === "#num" && words.some(function (w) { return /\d/.test(w); });   // #num: any number
  if (term.charAt(0) === "^") { var lead = term.slice(1); return !!lead && padded.indexOf(" " + lead + " ") === 0; }   // ^if: the answer starts with it
  if (term.indexOf(" ") !== -1) {
    return padded.indexOf(" " + term + " ") !== -1 || padded.indexOf(term) !== -1;
  }
  if (term.length >= 4) {
    var stems = [term];
    if (term.charAt(term.length - 1) === "e") stems.push(term.slice(0, -1));
    for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
      for (var k = 0; k < stems.length; k++) {
        if (stems[k].length >= 4 && words[i].indexOf(stems[k]) === 0) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
  var forms = [term, term + "s", term + "es", term + "ed", term + "ing", term + "d", term + "r", term + "er"];
  for (var j = 0; j < words.length; j++) {
    if (forms.indexOf(words[j]) !== -1) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
